sau  iminā pāḷinayaina jātassa nāmarūpassa nibbattilakkhaṇaṃ jātiṃ uppādaṃ abhinavākāraṃ ‘‘udayau’’ti, vipariṇāmalakkhaṇaṃ khayaṃ bhaṅgaṃ ‘‘vayau’’ti samanupassati| sau aivaṃ pajānāti ‘‘imassa nāmarūpassa uppattitau pubbai anuppan‍nassa rāsi vā nicayau vā natthi, uppaj‍jamānassāpi rāsitau vā nicayatau vā āgamanaṃ nāma natthi, nirujjhamānassāpi disāvidisāgamanaṃ nāma natthi, niruddhassāpi aikasmiṃ ṭhānai rāsitau nicayatau nidhānatau avaṭṭhānaṃ  nāma natthi| yathā pana vīṇāya vādiyamānāya uppan‍nasaddassa naiva uppattitau pubbai san‍nicayau atthi, na uppaj‍jamānau san‍nicayatau āgatau, na nirujjhamānassa disāvidisāgamanaṃ atthi, na niruddhau katthaci san‍nicitau tiṭṭhati, atha khau vīṇañ‍ca upavīṇañ‍ca purisassa ca taj‍jaṃ vāyāmaṃ paṭic‍ca ahutvā sambhauti, hutvā paṭivaiti| aivaṃ sabbaipi rūpārūpinau dhammā ahutvā sambhaunti, hutvā paṭivaintī’’ti|

724. aivaṃ saṅkhaipatau udayabbayamanasikāraṃ katvā puna yāni aitassaiva udayabbayañāṇassa vibhaṅgai –

‘‘avij‍jāsamudayā rūpasamudayauti pac‍cayasamudayaṭṭhaina rūpakkhandhassa udayaṃ passati| taṇhāsamudayā… kammasamudayā… āhārasamudayā rūpasamudayauti pac‍cayasamudayaṭṭhaina rūpakkhandhassa udayaṃ passati| nibbattilakkhaṇaṃ passantaupi rūpakkhandhassa udayaṃ passati| rūpakkhandhassa udayaṃ passantau imāni pañ‍ca lakkhaṇāni passati|

‘‘avij‍jāniraudhā rūpaniraudhauti pac‍cayaniraudhaṭṭhaina rūpakkhandhassa vayaṃ passati| taṇhāniraudhā… kammaniraudhā… āhāraniraudhā rūpaniraudhauti pac‍cayaniraudhaṭṭhaina rūpakkhandhassa vayaṃ passati| vipariṇāmalakkhaṇaṃ passantaupi rūpakkhandhassa vayaṃ passati| rūpakkhandhassa vayaṃ passantaupi imāni pañ‍ca lakkhaṇāni passati’’ (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.50)|

tathā ‘‘avij‍jāsamudayā vaidanāsamudayauti pac‍cayasamudayaṭṭhaina vaidanākkhandhassa udayaṃ passati| taṇhāsamudayā… kammasamudayā… phassasamudayā vaidanāsamudayauti pac‍cayasamudayaṭṭhaina vaidanākkhandhassa udayaṃ passati| nibbattilakkhaṇaṃ passantaupi vaidanākkhandhassa  udayaṃ passati| vaidanākkhandhassa udayaṃ passantau imāni pañ‍ca lakkhaṇāni passati| avij‍jāniraudhā… taṇhāniraudhā… kammaniraudhā… phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhauti pac‍cayaniraudhaṭṭhaina vaidanākkhandhassa vayaṃ passati| vipariṇāmalakkhaṇaṃ passantaupi vaidanākkhandhassa vayaṃ passati| vaidanākkhandhassa vayaṃ passantau imāni pañ‍ca lakkhaṇāni passati’’ (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.50)|

vaidanākkhandhassa  viya ca sañ‍ñāsaṅkhāraviñ‍ñāṇakkhandhānaṃ| ayaṃ pana visaisau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa phassaṭṭhānai ‘‘nāmarūpasamudayā, nāmarūpaniraudhā’’ti –

aivaṃ aikaikassa khandhassa udayabbayadassanai dasa dasa katvā pañ‍ñāsa lakkhaṇāni vuttāni| taisaṃ vasaina aivampi rūpassa udayau aivampi rūpassa vayau, aivampi rūpaṃ udaiti, aivampi rūpaṃ vaitīti pac‍cayatau caiva khaṇatau ca vitthāraina manasikāraṃ karauti|

725. tassaivaṃ manasikarautau ‘‘iti kirimai dhammā ahutvā sambhaunti, hutvā paṭivaintī’’ti ñāṇaṃ visadataraṃ hauti| tassaivaṃ pac‍cayatau caiva khaṇatau ca dvaidhā udayabbayaṃ passatau sac‍capaṭic‍casamuppādanayalakkhaṇabhaidā pākaṭā haunti|

726. yañhi sau avij‍jādisamudayā khandhānaṃ samudayaṃ, avij‍jādiniraudhā ca khandhānaṃ niraudhaṃ passati, idamassa pac‍cayatau udayabbayadassanaṃ| yaṃ pana nibbattilakkhaṇavipariṇāmalakkhaṇāni passantau khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ passati, idamassa khaṇatau udayabbayadassanaṃ, uppattikkhaṇaiyaiva hi nibbattilakkhaṇaṃ| bhaṅgakkhaṇai ca vipariṇāmalakkhaṇaṃ|

727. ic‍cassaivaṃ pac‍cayatau caiva khaṇatau ca dvaidhā udayabbayaṃ passatau pac‍cayatau udayadassanaina samudayasac‍caṃ pākaṭaṃ hauti janakāvabaudhatau| khaṇatau udayadassanaina dukkhasac‍caṃ pākaṭaṃ hauti jātidukkhāvabaudhatau| pac‍cayatau vayadassanaina niraudhasac‍caṃ pākaṭaṃ hauti pac‍cayānuppādaina pac‍cayavataṃ anuppādāvabaudhatau| khaṇatau vayadassanaina dukkhasac‍camaiva pākaṭaṃ hauti maraṇadukkhāvabaudhatau| yañ‍cassa  udayabbayadassanaṃ, maggauvāyaṃ laukikauti maggasac‍caṃ pākaṭaṃ hauti tatra sammauhavighātatau|

728. pac‍cayatau cassa udayadassanaina anulaumau paṭic‍casamuppādau pākaṭau hauti, ‘‘imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hautī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.404; saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.21; udā॰ 1) avabaudhatau| pac‍cayatau vayadassanaina paṭilaumau paṭic‍casamuppādau pākaṭau hauti, ‘‘imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhatī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.406; saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.21; udā॰ 2) avabaudhatau| khaṇatau pana udayabbayadassanaina paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā dhammā pākaṭā haunti saṅkhatalakkhaṇāvabaudhatau| udayabbayavantau hi saṅkhatā, tai ca paṭic‍casamuppan‍nāti|

729. pac‍cayatau cassa udayadassanaina aikattanayau pākaṭau hauti haituphalasambandhaina santānassa anupacchaidāvabaudhatau| atha suṭṭhutaraṃ ucchaidadiṭṭhiṃ pajahati| khaṇatau udayadassanaina nānattanayau pākaṭau hauti navanavānaṃ uppādāvabaudhatau| atha suṭṭhutaraṃ sassatadiṭṭhiṃ pajahati| pac‍cayatau cassa udayabbayadassanaina abyāpāranayau pākaṭau hauti dhammānaṃ avasavattibhāvāvabaudhatau| atha suṭṭhutaraṃ attadiṭṭhiṃ pajahati| pac‍cayatau pana udayadassanaina aivaṃdhammatānayau pākaṭau hauti pac‍cayānurūpaina phalassa uppādāvabaudhatau| atha suṭṭhutaraṃ akiriyadiṭṭhiṃ pajahati|

730. pac‍cayatau cassa udayadassanaina anattalakkhaṇaṃ pākaṭaṃ hauti dhammānaṃ nirīhakattapac‍cayapaṭibaddhavuttitāvabaudhatau| khaṇatau udayabbayadassanaina anic‍calakkhaṇaṃ pākaṭaṃ hauti hutvā abhāvāvabaudhatau, pubbantāparantavivaikāvabaudhatau ca| dukkhalakkhaṇampi pākaṭaṃ hauti udayabbayaihi paṭipīḷanāvabaudhatau| sabhāvalakkhaṇampi pākaṭaṃ hauti udayabbayaparicchin‍nāvabaudhatau| sabhāvalakkhaṇai saṅkhatalakkhaṇassa tāvakālikattampi pākaṭaṃ hauti udayakkhaṇai vayassa, vayakkhaṇai ca udayassa abhāvāvabaudhatauti|

731. tassaivaṃ pākaṭībhūtasac‍capaṭic‍casamuppādanayalakkhaṇabhaidassa ‘‘aivaṃ kira nāmimai dhammā anuppan‍napubbā uppaj‍janti, uppan‍nā nirujjhantī’’ti nic‍canavāva hutvā saṅkhārā  upaṭṭhahanti| na kaivalañ‍ca nic‍canavā, sūriyuggamanai ussāvabindu viya udakabubbuḷau viya udakai daṇḍarāji viya āraggai sāsapau viya vij‍juppādau viya ca parittaṭṭhāyinau| māyāmarīcisupinantaalātacak‍kagandhabbanagaraphaiṇakadaliādayau viya assārā nissārāti cāpi upaṭṭhahanti|

aittāvatānaina ‘‘vayadhammamaiva uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nañ‍ca vayaṃ upaitī’’ti iminā ākāraina samapañ‍ñāsa lakkhaṇāni paṭivijjhitvā ṭhitaṃ udayabbayānupassanaṃ nāma taruṇavipassanāñāṇaṃ adhigataṃ hauti, yassādhigamā āraddhavipassakauti saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

vipassanupak‍kilaisakathā

732. athassa  imāya taruṇavipassanāya āraddhavipassakassa dasa vipassanupak‍kilaisā uppaj‍janti| vipassanupak‍kilaisā hi paṭivaidhappattassa ariyasāvakassa caiva vippaṭipan‍nakassa ca nikkhittakammaṭṭhānassa kusītapuggalassa nuppaj‍janti| sammāpaṭipan‍nakassa pana yuttapayuttassa āraddhavipassakassa kulaputtassa uppaj‍jantiyaiva|

katamai pana tai dasa upak‍kilaisāti? aubhāsau, ñāṇaṃ, pīti, passaddhi, sukhaṃ, adhimaukkhau, paggahau, upaṭṭhānaṃ, upaikkhā, nikantīti| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘kathaṃ dhammuddhac‍caviggahitamānasaṃ hauti? anic‍catau manasikarautau aubhāsau uppaj‍jati, ‘aubhāsau dhammau’ti aubhāsaṃ āvaj‍jati, tatau vikkhaipau uddhac‍caṃ| taina uddhac‍caina viggahitamānasau anic‍catau upaṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| dukkhatau… anattatau upaṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti’’|

tathā ‘‘anic‍catau manasikarautau ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati…pai॰… pīti… passaddhi… sukhaṃ… adhimaukkhau… paggahau… upaṭṭhānaṃ… upaikkhā… nikanti uppaj‍jati, ‘nikanti dhammau’ti nikantiṃ āvaj‍jati, tatau vikkhaipau uddhac‍caṃ| taina uddhac‍caina viggahitamānasau anic‍catau upaṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| dukkhatau… anattatau upaṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānātī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.6)|

733. tattha  aubhāsauti vipassanaubhāsau| tasmiṃ uppan‍nai yaugāvacarau ‘‘na vata mai itau pubbai aivarūpau aubhāsau uppan‍napubbau, addhā maggappattausmi phalapattausmī’’ti amaggamaiva ‘‘maggau’’ti, aphalamaiva ca ‘‘phala’’nti gaṇhāti| tassa amaggaṃ ‘‘maggau’’ti aphalaṃ ‘‘phala’’nti gaṇhatau vipassanāvīthi uk‍kantā nāma hauti| sau attanau mūlakammaṭṭhānaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā aubhāsamaiva assādaintau nisīdati|

sau khau panāyaṃ aubhāsau kassaci bhikkhunau pal‍laṅkaṭṭhānamattamaiva aubhāsaintau uppaj‍jati| kassaci  antaugabbhaṃ| kassaci bahigabbhampi| kassaci sakalavihāraṃ, gāvutaṃ, aḍḍhayaujanaṃ, yaujanaṃ, dviyaujanaṃ, tiyaujanaṃ…pai॰… kassaci pathavītalatau yāva akaniṭṭhabrahmalaukā aikālaukaṃ kurumānau| bhagavatau pana dasasahassilaukadhātuṃ aubhāsaintau udapādi|

aivaṃ vaimattatāya cassa idaṃ vatthu – cittalapabbatai kira dvikuṭṭagaihassa antau dvai thairā nisīdiṃsu| taṃdivasañ‍ca kāḷapakkhupausathau hauti, maighapaṭalacchan‍nā disā, rattibhāgai caturaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ tamaṃ pavattati| athaikau thairau āha – ‘‘bhantai, mayhaṃ idāni caitiyaṅgaṇamhi sīhāsanai pañ‍cavaṇṇāni kusumāni pañ‍ñāyantī’’ti| taṃ itarau āha – ‘‘anacchariyaṃ, āvusau, kathaisi, mayhaṃ panaitarahi mahāsamuddamhi yaujanaṭṭhānai macchakacchapā pañ‍ñāyantī’’ti|

ayaṃ pana vipassanupak‍kilaisau yaibhuyyaina samathavipassanālābhinau uppaj‍jati| sau samāpattivikkhambhitānaṃ kilaisānaṃ asamudācāratau ‘‘arahā aha’’nti cittaṃ uppādaiti uc‍cavālikavāsī mahānāgatthairau viya haṃkanakavāsī mahādattatthairau viya cittalapabbatai niṅkapaiṇṇakapadhānagharavāsī cūḷasumanatthairau viya ca|

tatridaṃ aikavatthuparidīpanaṃ – talaṅgaravāsī dhammadin‍natthairau kira nāma aikau pabhin‍napaṭisambhidau mahākhīṇāsavau mahatau bhikkhusaṅghassa auvādadāyakau  ahausi| sau aikadivasaṃ attanau divāṭṭhānai nisīditvā ‘‘kin‍nu khau amhākaṃ ācariyassa uc‍cavālikavāsīmahānāgatthairassa samaṇabhāvakic‍caṃ matthakaṃ pattaṃ, nau’’ti āvaj‍jantau puthuj‍janabhāvamaivassa disvā ‘‘mayi agacchantai puthuj‍janakālakiriyamaiva karissatī’’ti ca ñatvā iddhiyā vaihāsaṃ uppatitvā divāṭṭhānai nisin‍nassa thairassa samīpai aurauhitvā vanditvā vattaṃ dassaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| ‘‘kiṃ, āvusau dhammadin‍na, akālai āgatausī’’ti ca vuttai ‘‘pañhaṃ, bhantai, pucchituṃ āgataumhī’’ti āha| tatau ‘‘pucchāvusau, jānamānā kathayissāmā’’ti vuttai pañhasahassaṃ pucchi|

thairau pucchitapucchitaṃ asaj‍jamānauva kathaisi| tatau ‘‘atitikkhaṃ vau, bhantai, ñāṇaṃ, kadā tumhaihi ayaṃ dhammau adhigatau’’ti vuttai ‘‘itau saṭṭhivassakālai, āvusau’’ti āha| samādhimpi, bhantai, vaḷañ‍jaithāti, na yidaṃ, āvusau, bhāriyanti| taina hi, bhantai, aikaṃ hatthiṃ māpaithāti| thairau sabbasaitaṃ hatthiṃ māpaisi| idāni, bhantai, yathā ayaṃ hatthī añ‍citakaṇṇau pasāritanaṅguṭṭhau sauṇḍaṃ mukhai pakkhipitvā bhairavaṃ kauñ‍canādaṃ karauntau tumhākaṃ abhimukhau āgacchati, tathā naṃ karauthāti| thairau tathā katvā vaigaina āgacchatau hatthissa bhairavaṃ ākāraṃ disvā uṭṭhāya palāyituṃ āraddhau| tamainaṃ khīṇāsavatthairau hatthaṃ pasāraitvā cīvarakaṇṇai gahaitvā ‘‘bhantai, khīṇāsavassa sāraj‍jaṃ nāma hautī’’ti āha|

sau tamhi kālai attanau puthuj‍janabhāvaṃ ñatvā ‘‘avassayau mai, āvusau, dhammadin‍na hauhī’’ti vatvā pādamūlai uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīdi| ‘‘bhantai, tumhākaṃ avassayau bhavissāmic‍caivāhaṃ āgatau, mā cintayitthā’’ti kammaṭṭhānaṃ kathaisi| thairau kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā caṅkamaṃ āruyha tatiyai padavārai aggaphalaṃ arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| thairau kira dausacaritau ahausi| aivarūpā bhikkhū aubhāsai kampanti|

734. ñāṇanti vipassanāñāṇaṃ| tassa kira rūpārūpadhammai tulayantassa tīraintassa vissaṭṭhaindavajiramiva avihatavaigaṃ tikhiṇaṃ sūraṃ ativisadaṃ ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati|

pītīti  vipassanāpīti| tassa kira tasmiṃ samayai khuddakāpīti, khaṇikāpīti, auk‍kantikāpīti, ubbaigāpīti, pharaṇāpītīti ayaṃ pañ‍cavidhā pīti sakalasarīraṃ pūrayamānā uppaj‍jati|

passaddhīti vipassanāpassaddhi| tassa kira tasmiṃ samayai rattiṭṭhānai vā divāṭṭhānai vā nisin‍nassa kāyacittānaṃ naiva darathau, na gāravaṃ, na kakkhaḷatā, na akammañ‍ñatā, na gailañ‍ñaṃ, na vaṅkatā hauti, atha khau panassa kāyacittāni passaddhāni lahūni mudūni kammañ‍ñāni suvisadāni ujukāniyaiva haunti| sau imaihi passaddhādīhi anuggahitakāyacittau tasmiṃ samayai amānusiṃ nāma ratiṃ anubhavati| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ –

‘‘suñ‍ñāgāraṃ paviṭṭhassa, santacittassa bhikkhunau|

amānusī rati hauti, sammā dhammaṃ vipassatau||

‘‘yatau  yatau sammasati, khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ|

labhatī pītipāmauj‍jaṃ, amataṃ taṃ vijānata’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 373-374)|

aivamassa imaṃ amānusiṃ ratiṃ sādhayamānā lahutādisampayuttā passaddhi uppaj‍jati|

sukhanti vipassanāsukhaṃ| tassa kira tasmiṃ samayai sakalasarīraṃ abhisandayamānaṃ atipaṇītaṃ sukhaṃ uppaj‍jati|

adhimaukkhauti saddhā| vipassanāsampayuttāyaiva hissa cittacaitasikānaṃ atisayapasādabhūtā balavatī saddhā uppaj‍jati|

paggahauti vīriyaṃ| vipassanāsampayuttamaiva hissa asithilaṃ anac‍cāraddhaṃ supaggahitaṃ vīriyaṃ uppaj‍jati|

upaṭṭhānanti sati| vipassanāsampayuttāyaiva hissa supaṭṭhitā supatiṭṭhitā nikhātā acalā pabbatarājasadisā sati uppaj‍jati| sau yaṃ yaṃ ṭhānaṃ āvaj‍jati saman‍nāharati manasikarauti pac‍cavaikkhati, taṃ taṃ ṭhānamassa aukkhanditvā pakkhanditvā dibbacakkhunau paralaukau viya satiyā upaṭṭhāti|

upaikkhāti  vipassanupaikkhā caiva āvaj‍janupaikkhā ca| tasmiṃ hissa samayai sabbasaṅkhāraisu majjhattabhūtā vipassanupaikkhāpi balavatī uppaj‍jati| manaudvārai āvaj‍janupaikkhāpi| sā hissa taṃ taṃ ṭhānaṃ āvaj‍jantassa vissaṭṭhaindavajiramiva pattapuṭai pakkhitta tattanārācau viya ca sūrā tikhiṇā hutvā vahati|

nikantīti vipassanānikanti| aivaṃ aubhāsādipaṭimaṇḍitāya hissa vipassanāya ālayaṃ kurumānā sukhumā santākārā nikanti uppaj‍jati| yā nikanti kilaisauti pariggahaitumpi na sak‍kā hauti|

yathā  ca aubhāsai, aivaṃ aitaisupi añ‍ñatarasmiṃ uppan‍nai yaugāvacarau ‘‘na vata mai itau pubbai aivarūpaṃ ñāṇaṃ uppan‍napubbaṃ, aivarūpā pīti, passaddhi, sukhaṃ, adhimaukkhau, paggahau, upaṭṭhānaṃ, upaikkhā, nikanti uppan‍napubbā, addhā maggappattausmi phalappattausmī’’ti amaggamaiva ‘‘maggau’’ti aphalamaiva ca ‘‘phala’’nti gaṇhāti| tassa amaggaṃ ‘‘maggau’’ti aphalaṃ ‘‘phala’’nti gaṇhatau vipassanāvīthi uk‍kantā nāma hauti| sau attanau mūlakammaṭṭhānaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā nikantimaiva assādaintau nisīdatīti|

735. aittha ca aubhāsādayau upak‍kilaisavatthutāya upak‍kilaisāti vuttā, na akusalattā| nikanti pana upak‍kilaisau caiva upak‍kilaisavatthu ca| vatthuvasainaiva caitai dasa| gāhavasaina pana samatiṃsa haunti| kathaṃ? ‘‘mama aubhāsau uppan‍nau’’ti gaṇhatau hi diṭṭhigāhau hauti, ‘‘manāpau vata aubhāsau uppan‍nau’’ti gaṇhatau mānagāhau, aubhāsaṃ assādayatau taṇhāgāhau, iti aubhāsai diṭṭhimānataṇhāvasaina tayau gāhā| tathā saisaisupīti aivaṃ gāhavasaina samatiṃsa upak‍kilaisā haunti| taisaṃ vasaina akusalau abyattau yaugāvacarau aubhāsādīsu kampati vikkhipati| aubhāsādīsu aikaikaṃ ‘‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.241) samanupassati| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘aubhāsai caiva ñāṇai ca, pītiyā ca vikampati|

passaddhiyā sukhai caiva, yaihi cittaṃ pavaidhati||

‘‘adhimaukkhai  ca paggāhai, upaṭṭhānai ca kampati|

upaikkhāvaj‍janāyañ‍ca, upaikkhāya nikantiyā’’ti|| (paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.7)|

maggāmaggavavatthānakathā

736. kusalau pana paṇḍitau byattau buddhisampan‍nau yaugāvacarau aubhāsādīsu uppan‍naisu ‘‘ayaṃ khau mai aubhāsau uppan‍nau, sau khau panāyaṃ anic‍cau saṅkhatau paṭic‍casamuppan‍nau khayadhammau vayadhammau virāgadhammau niraudhadhammau’’ti iti vā taṃ pañ‍ñāya paricchindati upaparikkhati| atha vā panassa aivaṃ hauti, ‘‘sacai aubhāsau attā bhavaiyya, ‘attā’ti gahaituṃ  vaṭṭaiyya| anattā ca panāyaṃ ‘attā’ti gahitau| tasmā sau avasavattanaṭṭhaina anattā, hutvā abhāvaṭṭhaina anic‍cau, uppādavayapaṭipīḷanaṭṭhaina dukkhau’’ti sabbaṃ arūpasattakai vuttanayaina vitthāraitabbaṃ| yathā ca aubhāsai, aivaṃ saisaisupi|

sau aivaṃ upaparikkhitvā aubhāsaṃ ‘‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.241) samanupassati| ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… nikantiṃ ‘‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.241) samanupassati| aivaṃ samanupassantau aubhāsādīsu na kampati na vaidhati| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘imāni dasa ṭhānāni, pañ‍ñāyassa paric‍citā|

dhammuddhac‍cakusalau hauti, na ca vikkhaipaṃ gacchatī’’ti|| (paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.7)|

sau aivaṃ vikkhaipaṃ agacchantau taṃ samatiṃsavidhaṃ upak‍kilaisajaṭaṃ vijaṭaitvā aubhāsādayau dhammā na maggau| upak‍kilaisavimuttaṃ pana vīthipaṭipan‍naṃ vipassanāñāṇaṃ maggauti maggañ‍ca amaggañ‍ca vavatthapaiti| tassaivaṃ ‘‘ayaṃ maggau, ayaṃ na maggau’’ti maggañ‍ca amaggañ‍ca ñatvā ṭhitaṃ ñāṇaṃ maggāmaggañāṇadassanavisuddhīti vaiditabbaṃ|

aittāvatā ca pana taina tiṇṇaṃ sac‍cānaṃ vavatthānaṃ kataṃ hauti| kathaṃ? diṭṭhivisuddhiyaṃ tāva nāmarūpassa vavatthāpanaina dukkhasac‍cassa vavatthānaṃ kataṃ| kaṅkhāvitaraṇavisuddhiyaṃ pac‍cayapariggahaṇaina samudayasac‍cassa vavatthānaṃ| imissaṃ maggāmaggañāṇadassanavisuddhiyaṃ sammāmaggassa avadhāraṇaina maggasac‍cassa vavatthānaṃ  katanti, aivaṃ laukiyainaiva tāva ñāṇaina tiṇṇaṃ sac‍cānaṃ vavatthānaṃ kataṃ hauti|

iti sādhujanapāmauj‍jatthāya katai visuddhimaggai

pañ‍ñābhāvanādhikārai

maggāmaggañāṇadassanavisuddhiniddaisau nāma

vīsatimau paricchaidau|

21. paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhiniddaisau

upak‍kilaisavimuttaudayabbayañāṇakathā

737. aṭṭhan‍naṃ  pana ñāṇānaṃ vasaina sikhāppattā vipassanā, navamañ‍ca sac‍cānulaumikañāṇanti ayaṃ paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhi nāma| aṭṭhan‍nanti caittha upak‍kilaisavimuttaṃ vīthipaṭipan‍navipassanāsaṅkhātaṃ udayabbayānupassanāñāṇaṃ, bhaṅgānupassanāñāṇaṃ, bhayatupaṭṭhānañāṇaṃ, ādīnavānupassanāñāṇaṃ, nibbidānupassanāñāṇaṃ, muñ‍citukamyatāñāṇaṃ, paṭisaṅkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ, saṅkhārupaikkhāñāṇanti imāni aṭṭha ñāṇāni vaiditabbāni| navamaṃ sac‍cānulaumikañāṇanti anulaumassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| tasmā taṃ sampādaitukāmaina upak‍kilaisavimuttaṃ udayabbayañāṇaṃ ādiṃ katvā aitaisu ñāṇaisu yaugau karaṇīyau|

738. puna udayabbayañāṇai yaugau kimatthiyauti cai? lakkhaṇasal‍lakkhaṇatthau| udayabbayañāṇaṃ hi haiṭṭhā dasahi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ hutvā yāthāvasarasatau tilakkhaṇaṃ sal‍lakkhaituṃ nāsakkhi| upak‍kilaisavimuttaṃ pana sak‍kauti| tasmā lakkhaṇasal‍lakkhaṇatthamaittha puna yaugau karaṇīyau|

739. lakkhaṇāni pana kissa amanasikārā kaina paṭicchan‍nattā na upaṭṭhahanti? anic‍calakkhaṇaṃ tāva udayabbayānaṃ amanasikārā santatiyā paṭicchan‍nattā na upaṭṭhāti| dukkhalakkhaṇaṃ abhiṇhasampaṭipīḷanassa amanasikārā iriyāpathaihi paṭicchan‍nattā na upaṭṭhāti| anattalakkhaṇaṃ nānādhātuvinibbhaugassa amanasikārā ghanaina paṭicchan‍nattā na upaṭṭhāti| udayabbayampana pariggahaitvā santatiyā vikaupitāya anic‍calakkhaṇaṃ yāthāvasarasatau upaṭṭhāti| abhiṇhasampaṭipīḷanaṃ manasikatvā iriyāpathai ugghāṭitai dukkhalakkhaṇaṃ yāthāvasarasatau upaṭṭhāti| nānādhātuyau vinibbhujitvā ghanavinibbhaugai katai anattalakkhaṇaṃ yāthāvasarasatau upaṭṭhāti|

740. aittha  ca anic‍caṃ, anic‍calakkhaṇaṃ, dukkhaṃ, dukkhalakkhaṇaṃ, anattā, anattalakkhaṇanti ayaṃ vibhāgau vaiditabbau| tattha anic‍canti khandhapañ‍cakaṃ| kasmā? uppādavayañ‍ñathattabhāvā, hutvā abhāvatau vā| uppādavayañ‍ñathattaṃ anic‍calakkhaṇaṃ hutvā abhāvasaṅkhātau vā ākāravikārau|

‘‘yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkha’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.15) vacanatau pana tadaiva khandhapañ‍cakaṃ dukkhaṃ| kasmā? abhiṇhapaṭipīḷanā, abhiṇhapaṭipīḷanākārau dukkhalakkhaṇaṃ|

‘‘yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.15) pana vacanatau tadaiva khandhapañ‍cakaṃ anattā| kasmā? avasavattanatau, avasavattanākārau anattalakkhaṇaṃ|

tayidaṃ sabbampi ayaṃ yaugāvacarau upak‍kilaisavimuttaina vīthipaṭipan‍navipassanāsaṅkhātaina udayabbayānupassanāñāṇaina yāthāvasarasatau sal‍lakkhaiti|

upak‍kilaisavimuttaudayabbayañāṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

bhaṅgānupassanāñāṇakathā

741. tassaivaṃ sal‍lakkhaitvā punappunaṃ ‘‘anic‍caṃ dukkhamanattā’’ti rūpārūpadhammai tulayatau tīrayatau taṃ ñāṇaṃ tikkhaṃ hutvā vahati, saṅkhārā lahuṃ upaṭṭhahanti, ñāṇai tikkhai vahantai saṅkhāraisu lahuṃ upaṭṭhahantaisu uppādaṃ vā ṭhitiṃ vā pavattaṃ vā nimittaṃ vā na sampāpuṇāti| khayavayabhaidaniraudhaiyaiva sati santiṭṭhati| tassa ‘‘aivaṃ uppaj‍jitvā aivaṃ nāma saṅkhāragataṃ nirujjhatī’’ti passatau aitasmiṃ ṭhānai bhaṅgānupassanaṃ nāma vipassanāñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ –

‘‘kathaṃ ārammaṇapaṭisaṅkhā bhaṅgānupassanai pañ‍ñā vipassanai ñāṇaṃ? rūpārammaṇatā cittaṃ uppaj‍jitvā bhij‍jati, taṃ ārammaṇaṃ paṭisaṅkhā tassa cittassa bhaṅgaṃ anupassati| anupassatīti kathaṃ anupassati? anic‍catau anupassati nau nic‍catau, dukkhatau anupassati nau sukhatau, anattatau anupassati nau attatau, nibbindati nau nandati, viraj‍jati nau raj‍jati, niraudhaiti nau samudaiti, paṭinissaj‍jati nau ādiyati|

‘‘anic‍catau anupassantau nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pajahati| dukkhatau anupassantau sukhasañ‍ñaṃ, anattatau anupassantau attasañ‍ñaṃ, nibbindantau  nandiṃ, viraj‍jantau rāgaṃ, niraudhaintau samudayaṃ paṭinissaj‍jantau ādānaṃ pajahati|

‘‘vaidanārammaṇatā…pai॰… sañ‍ñārammaṇatā… saṅkhārārammaṇatā… viñ‍ñāṇārammaṇatā… cakkhārammaṇatā…pai॰… jarāmaraṇārammaṇatā cittaṃ uppaj‍jitvā bhij‍jati…pai॰… paṭinissaj‍jantau ādānaṃ pajahati|

‘‘vatthusaṅkamanā caiva, pañ‍ñāya ca vivaṭṭanā|

āvaj‍janābalañ‍caiva, paṭisaṅkhāvipassanā||

‘‘ārammaṇaanvayaina, ubhau aikavavatthanā|

niraudhai adhimuttatā, vayalakkhaṇavipassanā||

‘‘ārammaṇañ‍ca paṭisaṅkhā, bhaṅgañ‍ca anupassati|

suñ‍ñatau ca upaṭṭhānaṃ, adhipañ‍ñāvipassanā||

‘‘kusalau tīsu anupassanāsu, catassau ca vipassanāsu|

tayau upaṭṭhānai kusalatā, nānādiṭṭhīsu na kampatī’’ti||

‘‘taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā, taina vuc‍cati ‘ārammaṇapaṭisaṅkhā bhaṅgānupassanai pañ‍ñā vipassanai ñāṇa’’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.51-52)|

742. tattha  ārammaṇapaṭisaṅkhāti yaṃkiñ‍ci ārammaṇaṃ paṭisaṅkhāya jānitvā, khayatau vayatau disvāti atthau| bhaṅgānupassanai pañ‍ñāti tassa, ārammaṇaṃ khayatau vayatau paṭisaṅkhāya uppan‍nassa ñāṇassa bhaṅgaṃ anupassanai yā pañ‍ñā, idaṃ vipassanai ñāṇanti vuttaṃ| taṃ kathaṃ hautīti ayaṃ tāva kathaitukamyatāpucchāya atthau| tatau yathā taṃ hauti, taṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘rūpārammaṇatā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

tattha rūpārammaṇatā cittaṃ uppaj‍jitvā bhij‍jatīti rūpārammaṇaṃ cittaṃ uppaj‍jitvā bhij‍jati| atha vā rūpārammaṇabhāvai cittaṃ uppaj‍jitvā bhij‍jatīti atthau| taṃ ārammaṇaṃ paṭisaṅkhāti taṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ paṭisaṅkhāya jānitvā, khayatau vayatau disvāti atthau| tassa cittassa bhaṅgaṃ anupassatīti yaina cittaina taṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ khayatau vayatau diṭṭhaṃ, tassa cittassa aparaina cittaina bhaṅgaṃ anupassatīti atthau| taināhu paurāṇā ‘‘ñātañ‍ca ñāṇañ‍ca ubhaupi vipassatī’’ti|

aittha  ca anupassatīti anu anu passati, anaikaihi ākāraihi punappunaṃ passatīti atthau| taināha – ‘‘anupassatīti kathaṃ anupassati| anic‍catau anupassatī’’tiādi|

tattha yasmā bhaṅgau nāma anic‍catāya paramā kauṭi, tasmā sau bhaṅgānupassakau yaugāvacarau sabbaṃ saṅkhāragataṃ anic‍catau anupassati, nau nic‍catau| tatau anic‍cassa dukkhattā, dukkhassa ca anattattā tadaiva dukkhatau anupassati, nau sukhatau| anattatau anupassati nau attatau|

yasmā pana yaṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhamanattā, na taṃ abhinanditabbaṃ| yañ‍ca anabhinanditabbaṃ, na tattha raj‍jitabbaṃ| tasmā aitasmiṃ bhaṅgānupassanānusāraina ‘‘anic‍caṃ dukkhamanattā’’ti diṭṭhai saṅkhāragatai nibbindati, nau nandati| viraj‍jati, nau raj‍jati| sau aivaṃ araj‍jantau laukikainaiva tāva ñāṇaina rāgaṃ niraudhaiti, nau samudaiti| samudayaṃ na karautīti atthau|

atha vā sau aivaṃ virattau yathā diṭṭhaṃ saṅkhāragataṃ, tathā adiṭṭhampi anvayañāṇavasaina niraudhaiti, nau samudaiti| niraudhatauva manasikarauti| niraudhamaivassa passati, nau samudayanti atthau|

sau aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau paṭinissaj‍jati, nau ādiyati| kiṃ vuttaṃ hauti? ayampi anic‍cādianupassanā tadaṅgavasaina saddhiṃ khandhābhisaṅkhāraihi kilaisānaṃ paric‍cajanatau, saṅkhatadausadassanaina ca tabbiparītai nibbānai tan‍nin‍natāya pakkhandanatau paric‍cāgapaṭinissaggau caiva pakkhandanapaṭinissaggau cāti vuc‍cati| tasmā tāya saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu yathāvuttaina nayaina kilaisai paric‍cajati, nibbānai ca pakkhandati| nāpi nibbattanavasaina kilaisai ādiyati, na adausadassitāvasaina saṅkhatārammaṇaṃ| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘paṭinissaj‍jati nau ādiyatī’’ti|

743. idānissa taihi ñāṇaihi yaisaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānaṃ hauti, taṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘anic‍catau anupassantau nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pajahatī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha nandinti sappītikaṃ taṇhaṃ| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

744. gāthāsu  pana vatthusaṅkamanāti rūpassa bhaṅgaṃ disvā puna yaina cittaina bhaṅgau diṭṭhau, tassāpi bhaṅgadassanavasaina purimavatthutau añ‍ñavatthusaṅkamanā| pañ‍ñāya ca vivaṭṭanāti udayaṃ pahāya vayai santiṭṭhanā| āvaj‍janābalañ‍caivāti rūpassa bhaṅgaṃ disvā puna bhaṅgārammaṇassa cittassa bhaṅgadassanatthaṃ anantaramaiva āvaj‍janasamatthatā| paṭisaṅkhāvipassanāti aisā ārammaṇapaṭisaṅkhābhaṅgānupassanā nāma|

745. ārammaṇaanvayaina ubhau aikavavatthanāti pac‍cakkhatau diṭṭhassa ārammaṇassa anvayaina anugamanaina yathā idaṃ, tathā atītaipi saṅkhāragataṃ bhij‍jittha, anāgataipi bhij‍jissatīti aivaṃ ubhin‍naṃ aikasabhāvainaiva vavatthāpananti atthau|

vuttampi caitaṃ paurāṇaihi –

‘‘saṃvij‍jamānamhi visuddhadassanau,

tadanvayaṃ naiti atītanāgatai|

sabbaipi  saṅkhāragatā palaukinau,

ussāvabindū sūriyaiva uggatai’’ti||

niraudhai adhimuttatāti aivaṃ ubhin‍naṃ bhaṅgavasaina aikavavatthānaṃ katvā tasmiñ‍ñaiva bhaṅgasaṅkhātai niraudhai adhimuttatā taggarutā tan‍nin‍natā tappauṇatā tappabbhāratāti atthau| vayalakkhaṇavipassanāti aisā vayalakkhaṇavipassanā nāmāti vuttaṃ hauti|

746. ārammaṇañ‍ca paṭisaṅkhāti purimañ‍ca rūpādiārammaṇaṃ jānitvā| bhaṅgañ‍ca anupassatīti tassārammaṇassa bhaṅgaṃ disvā tadārammaṇassa cittassa bhaṅgaṃ anupassati| suñ‍ñatau ca upaṭṭhānanti tassaivaṃ bhaṅgaṃ anupassatau ‘‘saṅkhārāva bhij‍janti, taisaṃ bhaidau maraṇaṃ, na añ‍ñau kauci atthī’’ti suñ‍ñatau upaṭṭhānaṃ ijjhati|

taināhu  paurāṇā –

‘‘khandhā nirujjhanti na catthi añ‍ñau,

khandhāna bhaidau maraṇanti vuc‍cati|

taisaṃ khayaṃ passati appamattau,

maṇiṃva vijjhaṃ vajiraina yaunisau’’ti||

adhipañ‍ñāvipassanāti yā ca ārammaṇapaṭisaṅkhā yā ca bhaṅgānupassanā yañ‍ca suñ‍ñatau upaṭṭhānaṃ, ayaṃ adhipañ‍ñāvipassanā nāmāti vuttaṃ hauti|

747. kusalau tīsu anupassanāsūti anic‍cānupassanādīsu tīsu chaikau bhikkhu| catassau ca vipassanāsūti nibbidādīsu ca catūsu vipassanāsu| tayau upaṭṭhānai kusalatāti khayatau vayatau suñ‍ñatauti imasmiñ‍ca tividhai upaṭṭhānai kusalatāya| nānādiṭṭhīsu na kampatīti sassatadiṭṭhiādīsu nānappakārāsu diṭṭhīsu na vaidhati|

748. sau aivaṃ avaidhamānau ‘‘aniruddhamaiva nirujjhati, abhin‍namaiva bhij‍jatī’’ti pavattamanasikārau  dubbalabhājanassa viya bhij‍jamānassa, sukhumarajassaiva vippakiriyamānassa, tilānaṃ viya bhaj‍jiyamānānaṃ sabbasaṅkhārānaṃ uppādaṭṭhitipavattanimittaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā bhaidamaiva passati| sau yathā nāma cakkhumā purisau paukkharaṇītīrai vā nadītīrai vā ṭhitau thūlaphusitakai daivai vassantai udakapiṭṭhai mahantamahantāni udakabubbuḷakāni uppaj‍jitvā uppaj‍jitvā sīghaṃ sīghaṃ bhij‍jamānāni passaiyya, aivamaiva sabbai saṅkhārā bhij‍janti bhij‍jantīti passati| aivarūpaṃ hi yaugāvacaraṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘yathā bubbuḷakaṃ passai, yathā passai marīcikaṃ|

aivaṃ laukaṃ avaikkhantaṃ, mac‍curājā na passatī’’ti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 170)|

749. tassaivaṃ ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā bhij‍janti bhij‍jantī’’ti abhiṇhaṃ passatau aṭṭhānisaṃsaparivāraṃ bhaṅgānupassanāñāṇaṃ balappattaṃ hauti| tatrimai aṭṭhānisaṃsā – bhavadiṭṭhippahānaṃ, jīvitanikantiparic‍cāgau, sadāyuttapayuttatā, visuddhājīvitā, ussuk‍kappahānaṃ, vigatabhayatā, khantisaurac‍capaṭilābhau, aratiratisahanatāti|

taināhu  paurāṇā –

‘‘imāni aṭṭhagguṇamuttamāni,

disvā tahiṃ sammasatai punappunaṃ|

ādittacailassirasūpamau muni,

bhaṅgānupassī amatassa pattiyā’’ti||

bhaṅgānupassanāñāṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

bhayatupaṭṭhānañāṇakathā

750. tassaivaṃ sabbasaṅkhārānaṃ khayavayabhaidaniraudhārammaṇaṃ bhaṅgānupassanaṃ āsaivantassa bhāvaintassa  bahulīkarauntassa sabbabhavayaunigatiṭhitisattāvāsaisu pabhaidakā saṅkhārā sukhaina jīvitukāmassa bhīrukapurisassa sīhabyagghadīpiacchataracchayakkharakkhasacaṇḍagauṇacaṇḍakuk‍kurapabhin‍namadacaṇḍahatthighauraāsīvisaasanivicak‍kasusānaraṇabhūmijalitaaṅgārakāsuādayau viya mahābhayaṃ hutvā upaṭṭhahanti| tassa ‘‘atītā saṅkhārā niruddhā, pac‍cuppan‍nā nirujjhanti, anāgatai nibbattanakasaṅkhārāpi aivamaiva nirujjhissantī’’ti passatau aitasmiṃ ṭhānai bhayatupaṭṭhānañāṇaṃ nāma uppaj‍jati|

tatrāyaṃ upamā – aikissā kira itthiyā tayau puttā rājaparādhikā, taisaṃ rājā sīsacchaidaṃ āṇāpaisi| sā puttaihi saddhiṃ āghātanaṃ agamāsi| athassā jaiṭṭhaputtassa sīsaṃ chinditvā majjhimassa chindituṃ ārabhiṃsu| sā jaiṭṭhassa sīsaṃ chin‍naṃ majjhimassa ca chij‍jamānaṃ disvā kaniṭṭhamhi ālayaṃ vissaj‍ji ‘‘ayampi aitaisañ‍ñaiva sadisau bhavissatī’’ti| tattha tassā itthiyā jaiṭṭhaputtassa chin‍nasīsadassanaṃ viya yauginau atītasaṅkhārānaṃ niraudhadassanaṃ, majjhimassa chij‍jamānasīsadassanaṃ viya pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ niraudhadassanaṃ, ‘‘ayampi aitaisañ‍ñaiva sadisau bhavissatī’’ti kaniṭṭhaputtamhi ālayavissaj‍janaṃ viya ‘‘anāgataipi nibbattanakasaṅkhārā bhij‍jissantī’’ti anāgatānaṃ niraudhadassanaṃ| tassaivaṃ passatau aitasmiṃ ṭhānai uppaj‍jati bhayatupaṭṭhānañāṇaṃ|

aparāpi upamā – aikā kira pūtipajā itthī dasa dārakai vijāyi| taisu nava matā, aikau hatthagatau marati, aparau kucchiyaṃ| sā nava dārakai  matai dasamañ‍ca mīyamānaṃ disvā kucchigatai ālayaṃ vissaj‍ji ‘‘ayampi aitaisañ‍ñaiva sadisau bhavissatī’’ti| tattha tassā itthiyā navan‍naṃ dārakānaṃ maraṇānussaraṇaṃ viya yauginau atītasaṅkhārānaṃ niraudhadassanaṃ, hatthagatassa mīyamānabhāvadassanaṃ viya yauginau pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ niraudhadassanaṃ, kucchigatai ālayavissaj‍janaṃ viya anāgatānaṃ niraudhadassanaṃ| tassaivaṃ passatau aitasmiṃ khaṇai uppaj‍jati bhayatupaṭṭhānañāṇaṃ|

751. bhayatupaṭṭhānañāṇaṃ pana bhāyati na bhāyatīti? na bhāyati| tañhi atītā saṅkhārā niruddhā, pac‍cuppan‍nā nirujjhanti, anāgatā nirujjhissantīti tīraṇamattamaiva hauti| tasmā yathā nāma cakkhumā purisau nagaradvārai tissau aṅgārakāsuyau aulaukayamānau sayaṃ na bhāyati, kaivalaṃ hissa ‘‘yai yai aittha nipatissanti, sabbai anappakaṃ dukkhamanubhavissantī’’ti tīraṇamattamaiva hauti| yathā vā pana cakkhumā purisau khadirasūlaṃ ayausūlaṃ suvaṇṇasūlanti paṭipāṭiyā  ṭhapitaṃ sūlattayaṃ aulaukayamānau sayaṃ na bhāyati, kaivalaṃ hissa ‘‘yai yai imaisu sūlaisu nipatissanti, sabbai anappakaṃ dukkhamanubhavissantī’’ti tīraṇamattamaiva hauti, aivamaiva bhayatupaṭṭhānañāṇaṃ sayaṃ na bhāyati, kaivalaṃ hissa aṅgārakāsuttayasadisaisu, sūlattayasadisaisu ca tīsu bhavaisu ‘‘atītā saṅkhārā niruddhā, pac‍cuppan‍nā nirujjhanti, anāgatā nirujjhissantī’’ti tīraṇamattamaiva hauti| yasmā panassa kaivalaṃ sabbabhavayaunigatiṭhitinivāsagatā saṅkhārā byasanāpan‍nā sappaṭibhayā hutvā bhayatau upaṭṭhahanti, tasmā bhayatupaṭṭhānanti vuc‍cati|

aivaṃ bhayatau upaṭṭhānai panassa ayaṃ pāḷi –

‘‘anic‍catau manasikarautau kiṃ bhayatau upaṭṭhāti? dukkhatau| anattatau manasikarautau kiṃ bhayatau upaṭṭhātīti? anic‍catau manasikarautau nimittaṃ bhayatau upaṭṭhāti| dukkhatau manasikarautau pavattaṃ bhayatau upaṭṭhāti| anattatau manasikarautau nimittañ‍ca pavattañ‍ca bhayatau upaṭṭhātī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.227)|

tattha  nimittanti saṅkhāranimittaṃ| atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ saṅkhārānamaivaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| anic‍catau manasikarauntau hi saṅkhārānaṃ maraṇamaiva passati, tainassa nimittaṃ bhayatau upaṭṭhāti| pavattanti rūpārūpabhavapavatti| dukkhatau manasikarauntau hi sukhasammatāyapi pavattiyā abhiṇhapaṭipīḷanabhāvamaiva passati, tainassa pavattaṃ bhayatau upaṭṭhāti| anattatau manasikarauntau pana ubhayampaitaṃ suñ‍ñagāmaṃ viya marīcigandhabbanagarādīni viya ca rittaṃ tucchaṃ suñ‍ñaṃ assāmikaṃ apariṇāyakaṃ passati| tainassa nimittañ‍ca pavattañ‍ca ubhayaṃ bhayatau upaṭṭhātīti|

bhayatupaṭṭhānañāṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

ādīnavānupassanāñāṇakathā

752. tassa taṃ bhayatupaṭṭhānañāṇaṃ āsaivantassa bhāvaintassa bahulīkarauntassa sabbabhavayaunigatiṭhitisattāvāsaisu  naiva tāṇaṃ, na laiṇaṃ, na gati, nappaṭisaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| sabbabhavayaunigatiṭhitinivāsagataisu saṅkhāraisu aikasaṅkhāraipi patthanā vā parāmāsau vā na hauti| tayau bhavā vītac‍cikaṅgārapuṇṇaaṅgārakāsuyau viya, cattārau mahābhūtā ghauravisaāsīvisā viya, pañ‍cakkhandhā ukkhittāsikavadhakā viya, cha ajjhattikāyatanāni suñ‍ñagāmau viya, cha bāhirāyatanāni gāmaghātakacaurā viya, satta viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau, nava ca sattāvāsā aikādasahi aggīhi ādittā sampaj‍jalitā sajautibhūtā viya ca, sabbai saṅkhārā gaṇḍabhūtā raugabhūtā sal‍labhūtā aghabhūtā ābādhabhūtā viya ca nirassādā nirasā mahāādīnavarāsibhūtā hutvā upaṭṭhahanti|

kathaṃ? sukhaina jīvitukāmassa bhīrukapurisassa ramaṇīyākārasaṇṭhitampi savāḷakamiva vanagahanaṃ, sasaddūlā viya guhā, sagāharakkhasaṃ viya udakaṃ, samussitakhaggā viya pac‍catthikā, savisaṃ viya bhaujanaṃ, sacaurau viya maggau, ādittamiva agāraṃ, uyyuttasainā viya raṇabhūmi| yathā hi sau purisau aitāni savāḷakavanagahanādīni āgamma bhītau saṃviggau laumahaṭṭhajātau samantatau ādīnavamaiva passati, aivamaivāyaṃ yaugāvacarau  bhaṅgānupassanāvasaina sabbasaṅkhāraisu bhayatau upaṭṭhitaisu samantatau nirasaṃ nirassādaṃ ādīnavamaiva passati| tassaivaṃ passatau ādīnavañāṇaṃ nāma uppan‍naṃ hauti| yaṃ sandhāya idaṃ vuttaṃ –

‘‘kathaṃ bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ? uppādau bhayanti bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ| pavattaṃ bhayanti… nimittaṃ bhayanti… āyūhanā bhayanti… paṭisandhi bhayanti… gati bhayanti… nibbatti bhayanti… upapatti bhayanti… jāti bhayanti… jarā bhayanti… byādhi bhayanti… maraṇaṃ bhayanti… saukau bhayanti… paridaivau bhayanti… upāyāsau bhayanti bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ| anuppādau khaimanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ| appavattaṃ…pai॰… anupāyāsau khaimanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ| uppādau bhayaṃ, anuppādau khaimanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ| pavattaṃ…pai॰… upāyāsau bhayaṃ, anupāyāsau khaimanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘uppādau dukkhanti bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ| pavattaṃ…pai॰… upāyāsau dukkhanti bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ| anuppādau sukhanti santipadai  ñāṇaṃ| appavattaṃ…pai॰… anupāyāsau sukhanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ| uppādau dukkhaṃ, anuppādau sukhanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ| pavattaṃ…pai॰… upāyāsau dukkhaṃ, anupāyāsau sukhanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘uppādau sāmisanti bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ| pavattaṃ…pai॰… upāyāsau sāmisanti bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ| anuppādau nirāmisanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ| appavattaṃ…pai॰… anupāyāsau nirāmisanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ| uppādau sāmisaṃ, anuppādau nirāmisanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ| pavattaṃ…pai॰… upāyāsau sāmisaṃ, anupāyāsau nirāmisanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ|

uppādau  ‘‘saṅkhārāti bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ| pavattaṃ…pai॰… upāyāsau saṅkhārāti bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ| anuppādau nibbānanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ| appavattaṃ…pai॰… anupāyāsau nibbānanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ| uppādau saṅkhārā, anuppādau nibbānanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ| pavattaṃ…pai॰… upāyāsau saṅkhārā, anupāyāsau nibbānanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘uppādañ‍ca pavattañ‍ca, nimittaṃ dukkhanti passati|

āyūhanaṃ paṭisandhiṃ, ñāṇaṃ ādīnavai idaṃ||

‘‘anuppādaṃ appavattaṃ, animittaṃ sukhanti ca|

anāyūhanā appaṭisandhi, ñāṇaṃ santipadai idaṃ||

‘‘idaṃ ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ, pañ‍caṭhānaisu jāyati|

pañ‍caṭhānai santipadai, dasa ñāṇai pajānāti|

dvin‍naṃ ñāṇānaṃ kusalatā, nānādiṭṭhīsu na kampatī’’ti||

‘‘taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ| pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā ādīnavai ñāṇa’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.53)|

753. tattha  uppādauti purimakammapac‍cayā idha uppatti| pavattanti tathā uppan‍nassa pavatti| nimittanti sabbampi saṅkhāranimittaṃ| āyūhanāti āyatiṃ paṭisandhihaitubhūtaṃ kammaṃ| paṭisandhīti āyatiṃ uppatti| gatīti yāya gatiyā sā paṭisandhi hauti| nibbattīti khandhānaṃ nibbattanaṃ| upapattīti ‘‘samāpan‍nassa vā upapan‍nassa vā’’ti (dha॰ sa॰ 1289, 1291) aivaṃ vuttā vipākappavatti| jātīti jarādīnaṃ pac‍cayabhūtā bhavapac‍cayā jāti| jarāmaraṇādayau pākaṭā aiva| aittha ca uppādādayau pañ‍caiva ādīnavañāṇassa vatthuvasaina vuttā| saisā taisaṃ vaivacanavasaina| nibbatti jātīti idañhi dvayaṃ uppādassa caiva paṭisandhiyā ca vaivacanaṃ| gati upapattīti idaṃ dvayaṃ pavattassa| jarādayau nimittassāti| taināha –

‘‘uppādañ‍ca pavattañ‍ca, nimittaṃ dukkhanti passati|

āyūhanaṃ paṭisandhiṃ, ñāṇaṃ ādīnavai ida’’nti ca||

‘‘idaṃ ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ, pañ‍caṭhānaisu jāyatī’’ti ca||

anuppādau  khaimanti santipadai ñāṇantiādi pana ādīnavañāṇassa paṭipakkhañāṇadassanatthaṃ vuttaṃ| bhayatupaṭṭhānaina vā ādīnavaṃ disvā ubbiggahadayānaṃ abhayampi atthi khaimaṃ nirādīnavanti assāsajananatthampi aitaṃ vuttaṃ| yasmā vā panassa uppādādayau bhayatau sūpaṭṭhitā haunti, tassa tappaṭipakkhanin‍naṃ cittaṃ hauti, tasmā bhayatupaṭṭhānavasaina siddhassa ādīnavañāṇassa ānisaṃsadassanatthampaitaṃ vuttanti vaiditabbaṃ|

aittha ca yaṃ bhayaṃ, taṃ yasmā niyamatau dukkhaṃ| taṃ vaṭṭāmisalaukāmisakilaisāmisaihi avippamuttattā sāmisamaiva| yañ‍ca sāmisaṃ, taṃ saṅkhāramattamaiva| tasmā ‘‘uppādau dukkhanti bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā ādīnavai ñāṇa’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| aivaṃ santaipi bhayākāraina dukkhākāraina sāmisākāraināti aivaṃ ākāranānattatau pavattivasainaivaittha nānattaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

dasañāṇai pajānātīti ādīnavañāṇaṃ pajānantau uppādādivatthukāni pañ‍ca, anuppādādivatthukāni pañ‍cāti dasa ñāṇāni pajānāti paṭivijjhati sacchikarauti| dvin‍naṃ ñāṇānaṃ kusalatāti ādīnavañāṇassa caiva santipadañāṇassa cāti imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ kusalatāya| nānādiṭṭhīsu  na kampatīti paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānādivasaina pavattāsu diṭṭhīsu na vaidhati| saisamaittha uttānamaivāti|

ādīnavānupassanāñāṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

nibbidānupassanāñāṇakathā

754. sau aivaṃ sabbasaṅkhārai ādīnavatau passantau sabbabhavayaunigativiñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitisattāvāsagatai sabhaidakai saṅkhāragatai nibbindati uk‍kaṇṭhati nābhiramati|

saiyyathāpi nāma, cittakūṭapabbatapādābhiratau suvaṇṇarājahaṃsau asucimhi caṇḍālagāmadvāraāvāṭai nābhiramati, sattasu mahāsaraisuyaiva abhiramati, aivamaiva ayampi yaugīrājahaṃsau suparidiṭṭhādīnavai sabhaidakai saṅkhāragatai nābhiramati| bhāvanārāmatāya pana bhāvanāratiyā saman‍nāgatattā sattasu anupassanāsuyaiva ramati|

yathā  ca suvaṇṇapañ‍jarai pakkhittau sīhau migarājā nābhiramati, tiyaujanasahassavitthatai pana himavantaiyaiva ramati, aivamayaṃ yaugīsīhau tividhai sugatibhavaipi nābhiramati, tīsu pana anupassanāsuyaiva ramati|

yathā ca sabbasaitau sattapatiṭṭhau iddhimā vaihāsaṅgamau chaddantau nāgarājā nagaramajjhai nābhiramati, himavati chaddantadahagahanaiyaiva abhiramati, aivamayaṃ yaugīvaravāraṇau sabbasmimpi saṅkhāragatai nābhiramati, anuppādau khaimantiādinā nayaina diṭṭhai santipadaiyaiva abhiramati, tan‍nin‍natappauṇatappabbhāramānasau hautīti|

nibbidānupassanāñāṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

755. taṃ panaitaṃ purimaina ñāṇadvayaina atthatau aikaṃ| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘bhayatupaṭṭhānaṃ  aikamaiva tīṇi nāmāni labhati, sabbasaṅkhārai bhayatau addasāti bhayatupaṭṭhānaṃ nāma jātaṃ| taisuyaiva saṅkhāraisu ādīnavaṃ uppādaitīti ādīnavānupassanā nāma jātaṃ| taisuyaiva saṅkhāraisu nibbindamānaṃ uppan‍nanti nibbidānupassanā nāma jāta’’nti|

pāḷiyampi vuttaṃ – ‘‘yā ca bhayatupaṭṭhānai pañ‍ñā, yañ‍ca ādīnavai ñāṇaṃ, yā ca nibbidā, imai dhammā aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.227)|

muñ‍citukamyatāñāṇakathā

756. iminā pana nibbidāñāṇaina imassa kulaputtassa nibbindantassa uk‍kaṇṭhantassa anabhiramantassa sabbabhavayaunigativiñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitisattāvāsagataisu sabhaidakaisu saṅkhāraisu aikasaṅkhāraipi cittaṃ na saj‍jati, na laggati, na bajjhati, sabbasmā saṅkhāragatā muc‍citukāmaṃ nissaritukāmaṃ hauti| yathā kiṃ? yathā nāma jālabbhantaragatau macchau, sappamukhagatau maṇḍūkau, pañ‍jarapakkhittau vanakuk‍kuṭau, daḷhapāsavasagatau migau, ahituṇḍikahatthagatau sappau, mahāpaṅkapakkhandau kuñ‍jarau, supaṇṇamukhagatau  nāgarājā, rāhumukhappaviṭṭhau candau, sapattaparivāritau purisauti aivamādayau tatau tatau muc‍citukāmā nissaritukāmāva haunti, aivaṃ tassa yauginau cittaṃ sabbasmā saṅkhāragatā muc‍citukāmaṃ nissaritukāmaṃ hauti| athassa aivaṃ sabbasaṅkhāraisu vigatālayassa sabbasmā saṅkhāragatā muc‍citukāmassa uppaj‍jati muñ‍citukamyatā ñāṇanti|

muñ‍citukamyatāñāṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

paṭisaṅkhānupassanāñāṇakathā

757. sau aivaṃ sabbabhavayaunigatiṭṭhitinivāsagataihi sabhaidakaihi saṅkhāraihi muc‍citukāmau sabbasmā saṅkhāragatā muc‍cituṃ puna tai aivaṃ saṅkhārai paṭisaṅkhānupassanāñāṇaina tilakkhaṇaṃ āraupaitvā pariggaṇhāti|

sau  sabbasaṅkhārai anac‍cantikatau, tāvakālikatau, uppādavayaparicchin‍natau, palaukatau, calatau, pabhaṅgutau, addhuvatau, vipariṇāmadhammatau, assārakatau, vibhavatau, saṅkhatatau, maraṇadhammatautiādīhi kāraṇaihi anic‍cāti passati|

abhiṇhapaṭipīḷanatau, dukkhamatau, dukkhavatthutau, raugatau, gaṇḍatau, sal‍latau, aghatau, ābādhatau, ītitau, upaddavatau, bhayatau, upasaggatau, atāṇatau, alaiṇatau, asaraṇatau, ādīnavatau, aghamūlatau, vadhakatau, sāsavatau, mārāmisatau, jātidhammatau, jarādhammatau, byādhidhammatau, saukadhammatau, paridaivadhammatau, upāyāsadhammatau, saṃkilaisikadhammatautiādīhi kāraṇaihi dukkhāti passati|

ajañ‍ñatau, duggandhatau, jaigucchatau, paṭik‍kūlatau, amaṇḍanārahatau, virūpatau, bībhacchatautiādīhi kāraṇaihi dukkhalakkhaṇassa parivārabhūtatau asubhatau passati|

paratau, rittatau, tucchatau, suñ‍ñatau, assāmikatau, anissaratau, avasavattitautiādīhi kāraṇaihi anattatau passati|

758. aivañhi  passatānaina tilakkhaṇaṃ āraupaitvā saṅkhārā pariggahitā nāma haunti| kasmā panāyamaitai aivaṃ pariggaṇhātīti? muñ‍canassa upāyasampādanatthaṃ|

tatrāyaṃ upamā – aikau kira purisau ‘‘macchai gahaissāmī’’ti macchakhippaṃ gahaitvā udakai auḍḍāpaisi sau khippamukhaina hatthaṃ autāraitvā antauudakai sappaṃ gīvāya gahaitvā ‘‘macchau mai gahitau’’ti attamanau ahausi| sau ‘‘mahā vata mayā macchau laddhau’’ti ukkhipitvā passantau sauvatthikattayadassanaina sappauti sañ‍jānitvā bhītau ādīnavaṃ disvā gahaṇai nibbin‍nau muñ‍citukāmau hutvā muñ‍canassa upāyaṃ karauntau agganaṅguṭṭhatau paṭṭhāya hatthaṃ nibbaiṭhaitvā bāhuṃ ukkhipitvā uparisīsai dvai tayau vārai āvijjhitvā sappaṃ dubbalaṃ katvā ‘‘gaccha duṭṭha sappā’’ti nissaj‍jitvā vaigaina taḷākapāḷiṃ āruyha ‘‘mahantassa vata bhau sappassa mukhatau muttaumhī’’ti āgatamaggaṃ aulaukayamānau aṭṭhāsi|

tattha  tassa purisassa ‘‘macchau’’ti sappaṃ gīvāya gahaitvā tuṭṭhakālau viya imassāpi yauginau āditauva attabhāvaṃ paṭilabhitvā tuṭṭhakālau, tassa khippamukhatau sīsaṃ nīharitvā sauvatthikattayadassanaṃ viya imassa ghanavinibbhaugaṃ katvā saṅkhāraisu tilakkhaṇadassanaṃ, tassa bhītakālau viya imassa bhayatupaṭṭhānañāṇaṃ| tatau ādīnavadassanaṃ viya ādīnavānupassanāñāṇaṃ, gahaṇai nibbindanaṃ viya nibbidānupassanāñāṇaṃ| sappaṃ muñ‍citukāmatā viya muñ‍citukamyatāñāṇaṃ, muñ‍canassa upāyakaraṇaṃ viya paṭisaṅkhānupassanāñāṇaina saṅkhāraisu tilakkhaṇāraupanaṃ| yathā hi sau purisau sappaṃ āvijjhitvā dubbalaṃ katvā nivattaitvā ḍaṃsituṃ asamatthabhāvaṃ pāpaitvā sumuttaṃ muñ‍cati, aivamayaṃ yaugāvacarau tilakkhaṇāraupanaina saṅkhārai āvijjhitvā dubbalai katvā puna nic‍casukhasubhaattākāraina upaṭṭhātuṃ asamatthataṃ pāpaitvā sumuttaṃ muñ‍cati| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘muñ‍canassa upāyasampādanatthaṃ aivaṃ pariggaṇhātī’’ti|

759. aittāvatā tassa uppan‍naṃ hauti paṭisaṅkhāñāṇaṃ| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ –

‘‘anic‍catau  manasikarautau kiṃ paṭisaṅkhā ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati? dukkhatau| anattatau manasikarautau kiṃ paṭisaṅkhā ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati? anic‍catau manasikarautau nimittaṃ paṭisaṅkhā ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati| dukkhatau manasikarautau pavattaṃ paṭisaṅkhā ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati| anattatau manasikarautau nimittañ‍ca pavattañ‍ca paṭisaṅkhā ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jatī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.227)|

aittha ca nimittaṃ paṭisaṅkhāti saṅkhāranimittaṃ ‘‘addhuvaṃ tāvakālika’’nti anic‍calakkhaṇavasaina jānitvā| kāmañ‍ca na paṭhamaṃ jānitvā pacchā ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati, vauhāravasaina pana ‘‘manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇa’’ntiādīni (ma॰ ni॰ 3.421) viya aivaṃ vuc‍cati| aikattanayaina vā purimañ‍ca pacchimañ‍ca aikaṃ katvā aivaṃ vuttanti vaiditabbaṃ| iminā nayaina itarasmimpi padadvayai atthau vaiditabbauti|

paṭisaṅkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

saṅkhārupaikkhāñāṇakathā

760. sau  aivaṃ paṭisaṅkhānupassanāñāṇaina ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā suñ‍ñā’’ti pariggahaitvā puna ‘‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ attaina vā attaniyaina vā’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 3.69) dvikauṭikaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ pariggaṇhāti| sau aivaṃ naiva attānaṃ, na paraṃ kiñ‍ci attanau parikkhārabhāvai ṭhitaṃ disvā puna ‘‘nāhaṃ k‍vacani, kassaci kiñ‍canatasmiṃ, na ca mama k‍vacani, kismiñ‍ci kiñ‍canatatthī’’ti yā aittha catukauṭikā suñ‍ñatā kathitā, taṃ pariggaṇhāti|

kathaṃ? ayañhi nāhaṃ k‍vacanīti k‍vaci attānaṃ na passati| kassaci kiñ‍canatasminti attanau attānaṃ kassaci parassa kiñ‍canabhāvai upanaitabbaṃ na passati| bhātiṭṭhānaivā bhātaraṃ, sahāyaṭṭhānai vā sahāyaṃ, parikkhāraṭṭhānai vā parikkhāraṃ mañ‍ñitvā upanaitabbaṃ na passatīti atthau| na ca mama k‍vacanīti aittha mama-saddaṃ tāva ṭhapaitvā na ca k‍vacanīti parassa ca attānaṃ k‍vaci napassatīti  ayamatthau| idāni mama-saddaṃ āharitvā mama kismiñ‍ci kiñ‍canatatthīti sau parassa attā mama kismiñ‍ci kiñ‍canabhāvai atthīti na passatīti| attanau bhātiṭṭhānai vā bhātaraṃ, sahāyaṭṭhānai vā sahāyaṃ parikkhāraṭṭhānai vā parikkhāranti kismiñ‍ci ṭhānai parassa attānaṃ iminā kiñ‍canabhāvaina upanaitabbaṃ na passatīti atthau| aivamayaṃ yasmā naiva katthaci attānaṃ passati, na taṃ parassa kiñ‍canabhāvai upanaitabbaṃ passati, na parassa attānaṃ passati, na parassa attānaṃ attanau kiñ‍canabhāvai upanaitabbaṃ passati| tasmānaina catukauṭikā suñ‍ñatā pariggahitā hautīti|

761. aivaṃ catukauṭikaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ pariggahaitvā puna chahākāraihi suñ‍ñataṃ pariggaṇhāti| kathaṃ? cakkhu suñ‍ñaṃ attaina vā attaniyaina vā nic‍caina vā dhuvaina vā sassataina vā avipariṇāmadhammaina vā…pai॰… manau suñ‍ñau| rūpā suñ‍ñā…pai॰… dhammā suñ‍ñā| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| cakkhusamphassauti aivaṃ yāva jarāmaraṇā nayau naitabbau|

762. aivaṃ chahākāraihi suñ‍ñataṃ pariggahaitvā puna aṭṭhahākāraihi pariggaṇhāti| saiyyathidaṃ – rūpaṃ asāraṃ nissāraṃ sārāpagataṃ nic‍casārasāraina vā dhuvasārasāraina vā sukhasārasāraina  vā attasārasāraina vā nic‍caina vā dhuvaina vā sassataina vā avipariṇāmadhammaina vā| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhu…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ asāraṃ nissāraṃ sārāpagataṃ nic‍casārasāraina vā dhuvasārasāraina vā sukhasārasāraina vā attasārasāraina vā nic‍caina vā dhuvaina vā sassataina vā avipariṇāmadhammaina vā| yathā naḷau asārau nissārau sārāpagatau| yathā airaṇḍau… yathā udumbarau… yathā saitavacchau… yathā pāḷibhaddakau… yathā phaiṇapiṇḍau… yathā udakabubbuḷaṃ… yathā marīci… yathā kadalikkhandhau… yathā māyā asārā nissārā sārāpagatā, aivamaiva rūpaṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ asāraṃ nissāraṃ sārāpagataṃ nic‍casārasāraina vā…pai॰… avipariṇāmadhammaina vāti (cūḷani॰ maugharājamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 88)|

763. sau  aivaṃ aṭṭhahākāraihi suñ‍ñataṃ pariggahaitvā puna dasahākāraihi pariggaṇhāti, rūpaṃ rittatau passati| tucchatau… suñ‍ñatau… anattatau… anissariyatau… akāmakāriyatau… alabbhanīyatau… avasavattakatau… paratau… vivittatau passati| vaidanaṃ…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ rittatau…pai॰… vivittatau passatīti|

764. aivaṃ dasahākāraihi suñ‍ñataṃ pariggahaitvā puna dvādasahākāraihi pariggaṇhāti| saiyyathidaṃ – rūpaṃ na sattau, na jīvau, na narau, na māṇavau, na itthī, na purisau, na attā, na attaniyaṃ| nāhaṃ, na mama, na añ‍ñassa, na kassaci| vaidanā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na kassacīti (cūḷani॰ maugharājamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 88)|

765. aivaṃ dvādasahākāraihi suñ‍ñataṃ pariggaṇhitvā puna tīraṇapariñ‍ñāvasaina dvācattālīsāya ākāraihi suñ‍ñataṃ pariggaṇhāti, rūpaṃ anic‍catau… dukkhatau… raugatau… gaṇḍatau… sal‍latau… aghatau… ābādhatau… paratau… palaukatau… ītitau… upaddavatau… bhayatau… upasaggatau… calatau… pabhaṅgutau… addhuvatau… atāṇatau… alaiṇatau… asaraṇatau… asaraṇībhūtatau… rittatau… tucchatau… suñ‍ñatau… anattatau… anassādatau… ādīnavatau… vipariṇāmadhammatau… assārakatau… aghamūlatau… vadhakatau… vibhavatau… sāsavatau… saṅkhatatau… mārāmisatau… jātidhammatau… jarādhammatau… byādhidhammatau… maraṇadhammatau… saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassaupāyāsadhammatau… samudayatau… atthaṅgamatau… anassādatau … ādīnavatau… nissaraṇatau passati| vaidanaṃ…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… nissaraṇatau passati|

vuttampi caitaṃ – ‘‘rūpaṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… nissaraṇatau passantau suñ‍ñatau laukaṃ avaikkhati| vaidanaṃ…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… nissaraṇatau passantau suñ‍ñatau laukaṃ avaikkhati’’|

‘‘suñ‍ñatau laukaṃ avaikkhassu, maugharāja sadā satau|

attānudiṭṭhiṃ ūhac‍ca, aivaṃ mac‍cutarau siyā|

aivaṃ laukaṃ avaikkhantaṃ, mac‍curājā na passatī’’ti|| (su॰ ni॰ 1125; cūḷani॰ maugharājamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 88)|

766. aivaṃ  suñ‍ñatau disvā tilakkhaṇaṃ āraupaitvā saṅkhārai pariggaṇhantau bhayañ‍ca nandiñ‍ca vippahāya saṅkhāraisu udāsīnau ahausi majjhattau, ahanti vā mamanti vā na gaṇhāti vissaṭṭhabhariyau viya purisau|

yathā nāma purisassa bhariyā bhavaiyya iṭṭhā kantā manāpā, sau tāya vinā muhuttampi adhivāsaituṃ na sak‍kuṇaiyya, ativiya naṃ mamāyaiyya, sau taṃ itthiṃ añ‍ñaina purisaina saddhiṃ ṭhitaṃ vā nisin‍naṃ vā kathaintiṃ vā hasantiṃ vā disvā kupitau assa anattamanau, adhimattaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiyya| sau aparaina samayaina tassā itthiyā dausaṃ disvā muñ‍citukāmau hutvā taṃ vissaj‍jaiyya, na naṃ mamāti gaṇhaiyya| tatau paṭṭhāya taṃ yainakainaci saddhiṃ yaṃkiñ‍ci kurumānaṃ disvāpi naiva kuppaiyya, na daumanassaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, añ‍ñadatthu udāsīnauva bhavaiyya majjhattau| aivamaivāyaṃ sabbasaṅkhāraihi muñ‍citukāmau hutvā paṭisaṅkhānupassanāya saṅkhārai pariggaṇhantau ahaṃ mamāti gahaitabbaṃ adisvā bhayañ‍ca nandiñ‍ca vippahāya sabbasaṅkhāraisu udāsīnau hauti majjhattau|

tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau tīsu bhavaisu catūsu yaunīsu pañ‍casu gatīsu sattasu viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitīsu  navasu sattāvāsaisu cittaṃ patilīyati patikuṭati pativattati na sampasāriyati, upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti|

saiyyathāpi nāma padumapalāsai īsakapauṇai udakaphusitāni patilīyanti patikuṭanti pativattanti na sampasāriyanti, aivamaiva…pai॰… saiyyathāpi nāma kuk‍kuṭapattaṃ vā nahārudaddulaṃ vā aggimhi pakkhittaṃ patilīyati patikuṭati pativattati na sampasāriyati (a॰ ni॰ 7.49), aivamaiva tassa tīsu bhavaisu cittaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti| ic‍cassa saṅkhārupaikkhāñāṇaṃ nāma uppan‍naṃ hauti|

767. taṃ panaitaṃ sacai santipadaṃ nibbānaṃ santatau passati, sabbaṃ saṅkhārappavattaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā nibbānamaiva pakkhandati| nau cai nibbānaṃ santatau  passati, punappunaṃ saṅkhārārammaṇamaiva hutvā pavattati sāmuddikānaṃ disākākau viya| sāmuddikā kira vāṇijakā nāvaṃ ārauhantā disākākaṃ nāma gaṇhanti, tai yadā nāvā vātakkhittā vidaisaṃ pakkhandati, tīraṃ na pañ‍ñāyati, tadā disākākaṃ vissaj‍jainti| sau kūpakayaṭṭhitau ākāsaṃ laṅghitvā sabbā disā ca vidisā ca anugantvā sacai tīraṃ passati, tadabhimukhauva gacchati| nau cai passati, punappunaṃ āgantvā kūpakayaṭṭhiṃyaiva al‍līyati| aivamaiva sacai saṅkhārupaikkhāñāṇaṃ santipadaṃ nibbānaṃ santatau passati, sabbaṃ saṅkhārappavattaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā nibbānamaiva pakkhandati| nau cai passati, punappunaṃ saṅkhārārammaṇamaiva hutvā pavattati|

tadidaṃ suppaggai piṭṭhaṃ vaṭṭayamānaṃ viya| nibbaṭṭitakappāsaṃ vihanamānaṃ viya nānappakāratau saṅkhārai pariggahaitvā bhayañ‍ca nandiñ‍ca pahāya saṅkhāravicinanai majjhattaṃ hutvā tividhānupassanāvasaina tiṭṭhati| aivaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tividhavimaukkhamukhabhāvaṃ āpaj‍jitvā sattaariyapuggalavibhāgāya pac‍cayau hauti|

768. tatridaṃ tividhānupassanāvasaina pavattanatau tiṇṇaṃ indriyānaṃ ādhipataiyyavasaina tividhavimaukkhamukhabhāvaṃ āpaj‍jati nāma| tissau hi anupassanā tīṇi vimaukkhamukhānīti vuc‍canti| yathāha –

‘‘tīṇi  khau panimāni vimaukkhamukhāni laukaniyyānāya saṃvattanti, sabbasaṅkhārai paricchaidaparivaṭumatau samanupassanatāya, animittāya ca dhātuyā cittasampakkhandanatāya, sabbasaṅkhāraisu manausamuttaijanatāya, appaṇihitāya ca dhātuyā cittasampakkhandanatāya, sabbadhammai paratau samanupassanatāya, suñ‍ñatāya ca dhātuyā cittasampakkhandanatāya, imāni tīṇi vimaukkhamukhāni laukaniyyānāya saṃvattantī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.219)|

tattha paricchaidaparivaṭumatauti udayabbayavasaina paricchaidatau caiva parivaṭumatau ca| anic‍cānupassanaṃ hi ‘‘udayatau pubbai saṅkhārā natthī’’ti paricchinditvā taisaṃ gatiṃ saman‍naisamānaṃ ‘‘vayatau paraṃ na gacchanti, aitthaiva antaradhāyantī’’ti parivaṭumatau samanupassati| manausamuttaijanatāyāti cittasaṃvaijanatāya| dukkhānupassanaina hi saṅkhāraisu cittaṃ saṃvaijaiti| paratau samanupassanatāyāti ‘‘nāhaṃ, na mamā’’ti aivaṃ anattatau samanupassanatāya| iti imāni tīṇi padāni anic‍cānupassanādīnaṃ vasaina vuttānīti vaiditabbāni| tainaiva tadanantarai pañhavissaj‍janai vuttaṃ – ‘‘anic‍catau manasikarautau khayatau saṅkhārā upaṭṭhahanti| dukkhatau manasikarautau bhayatau saṅkhārā upaṭṭhahanti| anattatau manasikarautau suñ‍ñatau saṅkhārā upaṭṭhahantī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.219)|

769. katamai pana tai vimaukkhā, yaisaṃ imāni anupassanāni mukhānīti? animittau, appaṇihitau, suñ‍ñatauti aitai tayau| vuttaṃ haitaṃ ‘‘anic‍catau manasikarauntau adhimaukkhabahulau animittaṃ vimaukkhaṃ paṭilabhati| dukkhatau manasikarauntau passaddhibahulau appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhaṃ paṭilabhati| anattatau manasikarauntau vaidabahulau suñ‍ñatavimaukkhaṃ paṭilabhatī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.223)|

aittha ca animittau vimaukkhauti animittākāraina nibbānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā pavattau ariyamaggau| sau hi animittāya dhātuyā uppan‍nattā animittau| kilaisaihi ca vimuttattā vimaukkhau| aitainaiva nayaina appaṇihitākāraina nibbānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā pavattau appaṇihitau| suñ‍ñatākāraina nibbānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā pavattau suñ‍ñatauti vaiditabbau|

770. yaṃ pana abhidhammai ‘‘yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ  diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati appaṇihitaṃ suñ‍ñata’’nti (dha॰ sa॰ 343 ādayau) aivaṃ vimaukkhadvayamaiva vuttaṃ, taṃ nippariyāyatau vipassanāgamanaṃ sandhāya| vipassanāñāṇaṃ hi kiñ‍cāpi paṭisambhidāmaggai –

‘‘anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau abhinivaisaṃ muñ‍catīti suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| dukkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ sukhatau abhinivaisaṃ| anattānupassanāñāṇaṃ attatau abhinivaisaṃ muñ‍catīti  suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.229) aivaṃ abhinivaisaṃ muñ‍canavasaina suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhauti ca,

‘‘anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau nimittaṃ muñ‍catīti animittau vimaukkhau| dukkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ sukhatau nimittaṃ, anattānupassanāñāṇaṃ attatau nimittaṃ muñ‍catīti animittau vimaukkhau’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.229) aivaṃ nimittaṃ muñ‍canavasaina animittau vimaukkhauti ca,

‘‘anic‍cānupassanāñāṇaṃ nic‍catau paṇidhiṃ muñ‍catīti appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| dukkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ sukhatau paṇidhiṃ| anattānupassanāñāṇaṃ attatau paṇidhiṃ muñ‍catīti appaṇihitau vimaukkhau’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.229) aivaṃ paṇidhiṃ muñ‍canavasaina appaṇihitau vimaukkhauti ca –

vuttaṃ| tathāpi taṃ saṅkhāranimittassa avijahanatau na nippariyāyaina animittaṃ| nippariyāyaina pana suñ‍ñatañ‍caiva appaṇihitañ‍ca| tassa ca āgamanavasaina ariyamaggakkhaṇai vimaukkhau uddhaṭau| tasmā appaṇihitaṃ suñ‍ñatanti vimaukkhadvayamaiva vuttanti vaiditabbaṃ| ayaṃ tāvaittha vimaukkhakathā|

771. yaṃ pana vuttaṃ ‘‘sattaariyapuggalavibhāgāya pac‍cayau hautī’’ti, tattha saddhānusārī, saddhāvimuttau, kāyasakkhi, ubhataubhāgavimuttau, dhammānusārī, diṭṭhippattau, pañ‍ñāvimuttauti  imai tāva satta ariyapuggalā, taisaṃ vibhāgāya idaṃ saṅkhārupaikkhāñāṇaṃ pac‍cayau hauti|

772. yau hi anic‍catau manasikarauntau adhimaukkhabahulau saddhindriyaṃ paṭilabhati, sau sautāpattimaggakkhaṇai saddhānusārī hauti| saisaisu sattasu ṭhānaisu saddhāvimuttau|

773. yau pana dukkhatau manasikarauntau passaddhibahulau samādhindriyaṃ paṭilabhati, sau sabbattha kāyasakkhi nāma hauti| arūpajjhānaṃ pana patvā aggaphalappattau ubhataubhāgavimuttau nāma hauti|

774. yau  pana anattatau manasikarauntau vaidabahulau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ paṭilabhati, sau sautāpattimaggakkhaṇai dhammānusārī hauti| chasu ṭhānaisu diṭṭhippattau aggaphalai pañ‍ñāvimuttauti|

775. vuttaṃ haitaṃ –

‘‘anic‍catau manasikarautau saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti| saddhindriyassa adhimattattā sautāpattimaggaṃ paṭilabhati, taina vuc‍cati saddhānusārī’’ti|

tathā ‘‘anic‍catau manasikarautau saddhindriyaṃ adhimattaṃ hauti, saddhindriyassa adhimattattā sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti, taina vuc‍cati saddhāvimuttau’’tiādi (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.221)|

776. aparampi vuttaṃ –

‘‘saddahantau vimuttauti saddhāvimuttau| phuṭṭhantaṃ sacchikatauti kāyasakkhi| diṭṭhantaṃ pattauti diṭṭhippattau| saddahantau vimuc‍catīti saddhāvimuttau| jhānaphassaṃ paṭhamaṃ phusati pacchā niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikarautīti kāyasakkhi| ‘dukkhā saṅkhārā, sukhau niraudhau’ti ñātaṃ hauti diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phusitaṃ pañ‍ñāyāti diṭṭhippattau’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.221)|

777. itaraisu  pana catūsu saddhaṃ anusarati, saddhāya vā anusarati gacchatīti saddhānusārī| tathā pañ‍ñāsaṅkhātaṃ dhammaṃ anusarati, dhammaina vā anusaratīti dhammānusārī| arūpajjhānaina caiva ariyamaggaina cāti ubhataubhāgaina vimuttauti ubhataubhāgavimuttau| pajānantau vimuttauti pañ‍ñāvimuttauti aivaṃ vacanatthau vaiditabbauti|

saṅkhārupaikkhāñāṇaṃ|

778. taṃ panaitaṃ purimaina ñāṇadvayaina atthatau aikaṃ| taināhu paurāṇā – ‘‘idaṃ saṅkhārupaikkhāñāṇaṃ aikamaiva tīṇi nāmāni labhati, haiṭṭhā  muñ‍citukamyatāñāṇaṃ nāma jātaṃ, majjhai paṭisaṅkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ nāma, antai ca sikhāppattaṃ saṅkhārupaikkhāñāṇaṃ nāma’’|

779. pāḷiyampi vuttaṃ –

‘‘kathaṃ muñ‍citukamyatā-paṭisaṅkhā-santiṭṭhanā pañ‍ñā saṅkhārupaikkhāsu ñāṇaṃ? uppādaṃ muñ‍citukamyatā-paṭisaṅkhā-santiṭṭhanā pañ‍ñā saṅkhārupaikkhāsu ñāṇaṃ| pavattaṃ…pai॰… nimittaṃ…pai॰… upāyāsaṃ muñ‍citukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhā-santiṭṭhanā pañ‍ñā saṅkhārupaikkhāsu ñāṇaṃ| uppādau dukkhanti…pai॰… bhayanti…pai॰… sāmisanti…pai॰… uppādau saṅkhārāti…pai॰… upāyāsau saṅkhārāti muñ‍citukamyatā-paṭisaṅkhā-santiṭṭhanā pañ‍ñā saṅkhārupaikkhāsu ñāṇa’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.54)|

780. tattha muñ‍citukamyatā ca sā paṭisaṅkhā ca santiṭṭhanā cāti muñ‍citukamyatā-paṭisaṅkhā-santiṭṭhanā| iti pubbabhāgai nibbidāñāṇaina nibbin‍nassa uppādādīni paric‍cajitukāmatā muñ‍citukāmatā| muñ‍canassa upāyakaraṇatthaṃ majjhai paṭisaṅkhānaṃ paṭisaṅkhā| muñ‍citvā avasānai ajjhupaikkhanaṃ santiṭṭhanā| yaṃ sandhāya ‘‘uppādau saṅkhārā, tai saṅkhārai ajjhupaikkhatīti saṅkhārupaikkhā’’tiādi (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.54) vuttaṃ| aivaṃ aikamaividaṃ ñāṇaṃ|

781. apica imāyapi pāḷiyā idaṃ aikamaivāti vaiditabbaṃ| vuttaṃ haitaṃ – ‘‘yā ca muñ‍citukamyatā, yā ca paṭisaṅkhānupassanā, yā ca saṅkhārupaikkhā, imai dhammā aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.227)|

782. aivaṃ  adhigatasaṅkhārupaikkhassa pana imassa kulaputtassa vipassanā sikhāppattā vuṭṭhānagāminī hauti| sikhāppattā vipassanāti vā vuṭṭhānagāminīti vā saṅkhārupaikkhādiñāṇattayassaiva aitaṃ nāmaṃ| sā hi sikhaṃ uttamabhāvaṃ pattattā sikhāppattā| vuṭṭhānaṃ gacchatīti vuṭṭhānagāminī| vuṭṭhānaṃ vuc‍cati bahiddhānimittabhūtatau abhiniviṭṭhavatthutau caiva ajjhattapavattatau ca vuṭṭhahanatau maggau, taṃ gacchatīti vuṭṭhānagāminī, maggaina saddhiṃ ghaṭiyatīti atthau|

783. tatrāyaṃ abhinivaisavuṭṭhānānaṃ āvibhāvatthāya mātikā – ajjhattaṃ abhinivisitvā ajjhattā vuṭṭhāti, ajjhattaṃ abhinivisitvā bahiddhā vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā abhinivisitvā bahiddhā vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā abhinivisitvā ajjhattā vuṭṭhāti, rūpai abhinivisitvā rūpā vuṭṭhāti, rūpai abhinivisitvā arūpā vuṭṭhāti, arūpai abhinivisitvā arūpā vuṭṭhāti, arūpai abhinivisitvā rūpā vuṭṭhāti, aikappahāraina pañ‍cahi khandhaihi vuṭṭhāti, anic‍catau abhinivisitvā anic‍catau vuṭṭhāti, anic‍catau abhinivisitvā dukkhatau, anattatau vuṭṭhāti, dukkhatau abhinivisitvā dukkhatau, anic‍catau, anattatau vuṭṭhāti, anattatau abhinivisitvā anattatau, anic‍catau, dukkhatau vuṭṭhāti|

784. kathaṃ? idhaikac‍cau āditauva ajjhattasaṅkhāraisu abhinivisati, abhinivisitvā tai passati| yasmā pana na suddhaajjhattadassanamattainaiva maggavuṭṭhānaṃ hauti, bahiddhāpi daṭṭhabbamaiva, tasmā parassa khandhaipi anupādiṇṇasaṅkhāraipi anic‍caṃ dukkhamanattāti passati| sau kālaina ajjhattaṃ sammasati, kālaina bahiddhā| tassaivaṃ sammasatau ajjhattaṃ sammasanakālai vipassanā maggaina saddhiṃ ghaṭiyati| ayaṃ ajjhattaṃ abhinivisitvā ajjhattā vuṭṭhāti nāma|

sacai panassa bahiddhā sammasanakālai vipassanā maggaina saddhiṃ ghaṭiyati, ayaṃ ajjhattaṃ abhinivisitvā bahiddhā vuṭṭhāti nāma| aisa nayau bahiddhā abhinivisitvā bahiddhā ca ajjhattā ca vuṭṭhānaipi|

785. aparau āditauva rūpai abhinivisati, abhinivisitvā bhūtarūpañ‍ca upādārūpañ‍ca rāsiṃ katvā passati| yasmā pana na suddharūpadassanamattainaiva vuṭṭhānaṃ hauti, arūpampi daṭṭhabbamaiva| tasmā taṃ rūpaṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā uppan‍naṃ vaidanaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ saṅkhārai viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca ‘‘idaṃ arūpa’’nti arūpaṃ passati| sau kālaina rūpaṃ sammasati, kālaina arūpaṃ| tassaivaṃ sammasatau rūpasammasanakālai vipassanā maggaina saddhiṃ ghaṭiyati, ayaṃ rūpai abhinivisitvā rūpā vuṭṭhāti nāma|

sacai panassa arūpasammasanakālai vipassanā maggaina saddhiṃ ghaṭiyati, ayaṃ arūpai abhinivisitvā arūpā vuṭṭhāti nāma| aisa nayau arūpai abhinivisitvā arūpā ca rūpā ca vuṭṭhānaipi|

786. ‘‘yaṃkiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti (dī॰ ni॰ 1.298) aivaṃ abhinivisitvā aivamaiva vuṭṭhānakālai pana aikappahāraina pañ‍cahi khandhaihi vuṭṭhāti nāma|

787. aikau  āditauva anic‍catau saṅkhārai sammasati| yasmā pana na anic‍catau sammasanamattainaiva vuṭṭhānaṃ hauti, dukkhataupi anattataupi sammasitabbamaiva, tasmā dukkhataupi anattataupi sammasati| tassaivaṃ paṭipan‍nassa anic‍catau sammasanakālai vuṭṭhānaṃ hauti, ayaṃ anic‍catau abhinivisitvā anic‍catau vuṭṭhāti nāma|

sacai panassa dukkhatau anattatau sammasanakālai vuṭṭhānaṃ hauti, ayaṃ anic‍catau abhinivisitvā dukkhatau, anattatau vuṭṭhāti nāma| aisa nayau dukkhatau anattatau abhinivisitvā saisavuṭṭhānaisupi|

788. aittha ca yaupi anic‍catau abhiniviṭṭhau, yaupi dukkhatau, yaupi anattatau, vuṭṭhānakālai ca anic‍catau vuṭṭhānaṃ hauti| tayaupi janā adhimaukkhabahulā haunti, saddhindriyaṃ paṭilabhanti, animittavimaukkhaina vimuc‍canti, paṭhamamaggakkhaṇai saddhānusārinau haunti, sattasu ṭhānaisu saddhāvimuttā| sacai pana dukkhatau vuṭṭhānaṃ hauti, tayaupi janā passaddhibahulā haunti, samādhindriyaṃ paṭilabhanti, appaṇihitavimaukkhaina vimuc‍canti, sabbattha kāyasakkhinau haunti| yassa panaittha arūpajjhānaṃ pādakaṃ, sau aggaphalai ubhataubhāgavimuttau hauti| atha naisaṃ anattatau vuṭṭhānaṃ hauti, tayaupi janā vaidabahulā haunti, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ paṭilabhanti, suñ‍ñatavimaukkhaina vimuc‍canti, paṭhamamaggakkhaṇai dhammānusārinau haunti, chasu ṭhānaisu diṭṭhippattā aggaphalai pañ‍ñāvimuttāti|

789. idāni  saddhiṃ purimapacchimañāṇaihi imissā vuṭṭhānagāminiyā vipassanāya āvibhāvatthaṃ dvādasa upamā vaiditabbā| tāsaṃ idaṃ uddānaṃ –

‘‘vaggulī kaṇhasappau ca, gharaṃ gau yakkhi dārakau|

khuddaṃ pipāsaṃ sītuṇhaṃ, andhakāraṃ visaina cā’’ti||

imā ca upamā bhayatupaṭṭhānatau pabhuti yattha katthaci ñāṇai ṭhatvā āharituṃ vaṭṭaiyyuṃ| imasmiṃ pana ṭhānai āhariyamānāsu bhayatupaṭṭhānatau yāva phalañāṇaṃ sabbaṃ pākaṭaṃ hauti, tasmā idhaiva āharitabbāti vuttā|

790. vaggulīti  aikā kira vaggulī ‘‘aittha pupphaṃ vā phalaṃ vā labhissāmī’’ti pañ‍casākhai madhukarukkhai nilīyitvā aikaṃ sākhaṃ parāmasitvā na tattha kiñ‍ci pupphaṃ phalaṃ vā gayhupagaṃ addasa| yathā ca aikaṃ, aivaṃ dutiyaṃ, tatiyaṃ, catutthaṃ| pañ‍camampi sākhaṃ parāmasitvā nāddasa| sā ‘‘aphalau vatāyaṃ rukkhau, natthaittha kiñ‍ci gayhupaga’’nti tasmiṃ rukkhai ālayaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā ujukāya sākhāya āruyha viṭapantaraina sīsaṃ nīharitvā uddhaṃ ul‍laukaitvā ākāsai uppatitvā añ‍ñasmiṃ phalarukkhai nilīyati|

tattha vagguli viya yaugāvacarau daṭṭhabbau, pañ‍casākhau madhukarukkhau viya pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā, tattha vagguliyā nilīyanaṃ viya yauginau khandhapañ‍cakai abhinivaisau, tassā aikaikaṃ sākhaṃ parāmasitvā kiñ‍ci gayhupagaṃ adisvā avasaisasākhāparāmasanaṃ viya yauginau rūpakkhandhaṃ sammasitvā tattha kiñ‍ci gayhupagaṃ adisvā avasaisakkhandhasammasanaṃ, tassā ‘‘aphalau vatāyaṃ rukkhau’’ti rukkhai ālayavissaj‍janaṃ viya yauginau pañ‍casupi khandhaisu anic‍calakkhaṇādidassanavasaina nibbin‍nassa muñ‍citukamyatādiñāṇattayaṃ, tassā ujukāya sākhāya upari ārauhanaṃ viya yauginau anulaumaṃ, sīsaṃ nīharitvā uddhaṃ ul‍laukanaṃ viya gautrabhuñāṇaṃ, ākāsai uppatanaṃ viya maggañāṇaṃ, añ‍ñasmiṃ phalarukkhai nilīyanaṃ viya phalañāṇaṃ|

791. kaṇhasappupamā paṭisaṅkhāñāṇai vuttāva| upamāsaṃsandanai panaittha sappavissaj‍janaṃ viya  gautrabhuñāṇaṃ, muñ‍citvā āgatamaggaṃ aulaukaintassa ṭhānaṃ viya maggañāṇaṃ, gantvā abhayaṭṭhānai ṭhānaṃ viya phalañāṇanti ayaṃ visaisau|

792. gharanti gharasāmikai kira sāyaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā sayanaṃ āruyha niddaṃ auk‍kantai gharaṃ ādittaṃ, sau pabujjhitvā aggiṃ disvā ‘‘bhītau sādhu vatassa sacai aḍayhamānau nikkhamaiyya’’nti aulaukayamānau maggaṃ disvā nikkhamitvā vaigaina khaimaṭṭhānaṃ gantvā ṭhitau| tattha gharasāmikassa bhuñ‍jitvā sayanaṃ āruyha niddauk‍kamanaṃ viya bālaputhuj‍janassa khandhapañ‍cakai ‘‘ahaṃ mamā’’ti gahaṇaṃ| pabujjhitvā aggiṃ disvā bhītakālau viya sammāpaṭipadaṃ paṭipaj‍jitvā lakkhaṇaṃ disvā bhayatupaṭṭhānañāṇaṃ, nikkhamanamaggaṃ aulaukanaṃ viya muñ‍citukamyatāñāṇaṃ, maggadassanaṃ viya anulaumaṃ, nikkhamanaṃ viya gautrabhuñāṇaṃ, vaigaina gamanaṃ viya maggañāṇaṃ, khaimaṭṭhānai ṭhānaṃ viya phalañāṇaṃ|

793. gauti aikassa kira kassakassa rattibhāgai niddaṃ auk‍kantassa vajaṃ bhinditvā gauṇā palātā, sau pac‍cūsasamayai tattha gantvā aulaukaintau taisaṃ palātabhāvaṃ ñatvā anupadaṃ gantvā rañ‍ñau gauṇai addasa| tai ‘‘mayhaṃ gauṇā’’ti sal‍lakkhaitvā āharantau pabhātakālai ‘‘na imai mayhaṃ gauṇā, rañ‍ñau gauṇā’’ti sañ‍jānitvā ‘‘yāva maṃ ‘caurau aya’nti gahaitvā rājapurisā na anayabyasanaṃ pāpainti, tāvadaiva palāyissāmī’’ti bhītau gauṇai pahāya vaigaina palāyitvā nibbhayaṭṭhānai aṭṭhāsi| tattha ‘‘mayhaṃ gauṇā’’ti rājagauṇānaṃ gahaṇaṃ viya bālaputhuj‍janassa ‘‘ahaṃ mamā’’ti khandhānaṃ gahaṇaṃ, pabhātai ‘‘rājagauṇā’’ti sañ‍jānanaṃ viya yauginau tilakkhaṇavasaina khandhānaṃ ‘‘anic‍cā dukkhā anattā’’ti sañ‍jānanaṃ, bhītakālau viya bhayatupaṭṭhānañāṇaṃ, vissaj‍jitvā gantukāmatā viya muñ‍citukamyatā, vissaj‍janaṃ viya gautrabhu, palāyanaṃ viya maggau, palāyitvā abhayadaisai ṭhānaṃ viya phalaṃ|

794. yakkhīti aikau kira purisau yakkhiniyā saddhiṃ saṃvāsaṃ kappaisi, sā rattibhāgai ‘‘suttau aya’’nti mantvā āmakasusānaṃ gantvā manussamaṃsaṃ khādati| sau ‘‘kuhiṃ aisā gacchatī’’ti anubandhitvā manussamaṃsaṃ khādamānaṃ disvā tassā amanussibhāvaṃ ñatvā ‘‘yāva maṃ na khādati, tāva palāyissāmī’’ti bhītau vaigaina palāyitvā khaimaṭṭhānai aṭṭhāsi| tattha yakkhiniyā saddhiṃ saṃvāsau viya khandhānaṃ ‘‘ahaṃ mamā’’ti gahaṇaṃ, susānai manussamaṃsaṃ khādamānaṃ  disvā ‘‘yakkhinī aya’’nti jānanaṃ viya khandhānaṃ tilakkhaṇaṃ disvā anic‍cādibhāvajānanaṃ, bhītakālau viya bhayatupaṭṭhānaṃ, palāyitukāmatā viya muñ‍citukamyatā, susānavijahanaṃ viya gautrabhu, vaigaina palāyanaṃ viya maggau, abhayadaisai ṭhānaṃ viya phalaṃ|

795. dārakauti aikā kira puttagiddhinī itthī, sā uparipāsādai nisin‍nāva antaravīthiyaṃ dārakasaddaṃ sutvā ‘‘puttau nu khau mai kainaci vihaiṭhiyatī’’ti vaigasā gantvā ‘‘attanau puttau’’ti sañ‍ñāya paraputtaṃ aggahaisi| sā ‘‘paraputtau aya’’nti sañ‍jānitvā auttappamānā itau citau  ca aulaukaitvā ‘‘mā haiva maṃ kauci ‘dārakacaurī aya’nti vadaiyyā’’ti dārakaṃ tatthaiva auraupaitvā puna vaigasā pāsādaṃ āruyha nisīdi| tattha attanau puttasañ‍ñāya paraputtassa gahaṇaṃ viya ‘‘ahaṃ mamā’’ti pañ‍cakkhandhagahaṇaṃ, ‘‘paraputtau aya’’nti sañ‍jānanaṃ viya tilakkhaṇavasaina ‘‘nāhaṃ, na mamā’’ti sañ‍jānanaṃ, auttappanaṃ viya bhayatupaṭṭhānaṃ, itau citau ca aulaukanaṃ viya muñ‍citukamyatāñāṇaṃ, tatthaiva dārakassa auraupanaṃ viya anulaumaṃ, auraupaitvā antaravīthiyaṃ ṭhitakālau viya gautrabhu, pāsādārūhanaṃ viya maggau, āruyha nisīdanaṃ viya phalaṃ|

796. khuddaṃ pipāsaṃ sītuṇhaṃ, andhakāraṃ visaina cāti imā pana cha upamā vuṭṭhānagāminiyā vipassanāya ṭhitassa laukuttaradhammābhimukhanin‍napauṇapabbhārabhāvadassanatthaṃ vuttā| yathā hi khuddāya abhibhūtau sujighacchitau purisau sādurasaṃ bhaujanaṃ patthaiti, aivamaivāyaṃ saṃsāravaṭṭajighacchāya phuṭṭhau yaugāvacarau amatarasaṃ kāyagatāsatibhaujanaṃ patthaiti|

yathā ca pipāsitau purisau parisussamānakaṇṭhamukhau anaikaṅgasambhāraṃ pānakaṃ patthaiti, aivamaivāyaṃ saṃsāravaṭṭapipāsāya phuṭṭhau yaugāvacarau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikamaggapānakaṃ patthaiti|

yathā pana sītasamphuṭṭhau purisau uṇhaṃ patthaiti, aivamaivāyaṃ saṃsāravaṭṭai taṇhāsinaihasītaina phuṭṭhau yaugāvacarau kilaisasantāpakaṃ maggataijaṃ patthaiti|

yathā ca uṇhasamphuṭṭhau purisau sītaṃ patthaiti, aivamaivāyaṃ saṃsāravaṭṭai aikādasaggisantāpasantattau yaugāvacarau aikādasaggivūpasamaṃ nibbānaṃ patthaiti|

yathā  pana andhakāraparaitau purisau ālaukaṃ patthaiti, aivamaivāyaṃ avij‍jandhakāraina aunaddhapariyaunaddhau yaugāvacarau ñāṇālaukaṃ maggabhāvanaṃ patthaiti|

yathā ca visasamphuṭṭhau purisau visaghātanaṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ patthaiti, aivamaivāyaṃ kilaisavisasamphuṭṭhau yaugāvacarau kilaisavisanimmathanaṃ amatausadhaṃ nibbānaṃ patthaiti| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘tassaivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau  tīsu bhavaisu…pai॰… navasu sattāvāsaisu cittaṃ patilīyati patikuṭati pativattati na sampasāriyati| upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti| saiyyathāpi nāma padumapalāsai īsakapauṇai’’ti sabbaṃ pubbai vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ|

797. aittāvatā ca panaisa patilīnacarau nāma hauti, yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ –

‘‘patilīnacarassa bhikkhunau,

bhajamānassa vivittamāsanaṃ|

sāmaggiyamāhu tassa taṃ,

yau attānaṃ bhavanai na dassayai’’ti|| (su॰ ni॰ 816; mahāni॰ 45)|

aivamidaṃ saṅkhārupaikkhāñāṇaṃ yauginau patilīnacarabhāvaṃ niyamaitvā uttari ariyamaggassāpi baujjhaṅgamaggaṅgajhānaṅgapaṭipadāvimaukkhavisaisaṃ niyamaiti| kaici hi thairā baujjhaṅgamaggaṅgajhānaṅgānaṃ visaisaṃ pādakajjhānaṃ niyamaitīti vadanti| kaici vipassanāya ārammaṇabhūtā khandhā niyamaintīti vadanti| kaici puggalajjhāsayau niyamaitīti vadanti| taisampi vādaisu ayaṃ pubbabhāgavuṭṭhānagāminivipassanāva niyamaitīti vaiditabbā|

798. tatrāyaṃ anupubbikathā – vipassanāniyamaina hi sukkhavipassakassa uppan‍namaggaupi, samāpattilābhinau jhānaṃ pādakaṃ akatvā uppan‍namaggaupi, paṭhamajjhānaṃ pādakaṃ katvā pakiṇṇakasaṅkhārai sammasitvā uppāditamaggaupi paṭhamajjhānikāva haunti| sabbaisu satta baujjhaṅgāni aṭṭha maggaṅgāni pañ‍ca jhānaṅgāni haunti| taisaṃ hi pubbabhāgavipassanā saumanassasahagatāpi upaikkhāsahagatāpi hutvā vuṭṭhānakālai saṅkhārupaikkhābhāvaṃ patvā saumanassasahagatā  hauti| pañ‍cakanayai dutiyatatiyacatutthajjhānāni pādakāni katvā uppāditamaggaisu yathāk‍kamainaiva jhānaṃ caturaṅgikaṃ tivaṅgikaṃ duvaṅgikañ‍ca hauti| sabbaisu pana satta maggaṅgāni haunti| catutthai cha baujjhaṅgāni| ayaṃ visaisau pādakajjhānaniyamaina caiva vipassanāniyamaina ca hauti| taisampi hi pubbabhāgavipassanā saumanassasahagatāpi upaikkhāsahagatāpi hauti| vuṭṭhānagāminī saumanassasahagatāva| pañ‍camajjhānaṃ  pādakaṃ katvā nibbattitamaggai pana upaikkhācittaikaggatāvasaina dvai jhānaṅgāni baujjhaṅgamaggaṅgāni cha satta caiva| ayampi visaisau ubhayaniyamavasaina hauti| imasmiṃ hi nayai pubbabhāgavipassanā saumanassasahagatā vā upaikkhāsahagatā vā hauti| vuṭṭhānagāminī upaikkhāsahagatāva| arūpajjhānāni pādakaṃ katvā uppāditamaggaipi aisaiva nayau| aivaṃ pādakajjhānatau vuṭṭhāya yaikaici saṅkhārai sammasitvā nibbattitamaggassa āsan‍napadaisai vuṭṭhitasamāpatti attanau sadisabhāvaṃ karauti bhūmivaṇṇau viya gaudhāvaṇṇassa|

799. dutiyatthairavādai pana yatau yatau samāpattitau vuṭṭhāya yai yai samāpattidhammai sammasitvā maggau nibbattitau hauti, taṃtaṃsamāpattisadisauva hauti| tatrāpi ca vipassanāniyamau vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbau|

800. tatiyatthairavādai attanau attanau ajjhāsayānurūpaina yaṃ yaṃ jhānaṃ pādakaṃ katvā yai yai jhānadhammai sammasitvā maggau nibbattitau, taṃtaṃjhānasadisauva hauti| pādakajjhānaṃ pana sammasitajjhānaṃ vā vinā ajjhāsayamattainaiva taṃ na ijjhati| svāyamatthau nandakauvādasuttaina (ma॰ ni॰ 3.398 ādayau) dīpaitabbau| aitthāpi ca vipassanāniyamau vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbau| aivaṃ tāva saṅkhārupaikkhā baujjhaṅgamaggaṅgajhānaṅgāni niyamaitīti vaiditabbā|

801. sacai panāyaṃ āditau kilaisai vikkhambhayamānā dukkhaina sappayaugaina sasaṅkhāraina vikkhambhaituṃ asakkhi, dukkhāpaṭipadā nāma hauti| vipariyāyaina sukhāpaṭipadā| kilaisai pana vikkhambhaitvā vipassanāparivāsaṃ maggapātubhāvaṃ saṇikaṃ kurumānā dandhābhiñ‍ñā nāma hauti| vipariyāyaina khippābhiñ‍ñā| iti ayaṃ saṅkhārupaikkhā āgamanīyaṭṭhānai ṭhatvā attanau attanau maggassa nāmaṃ daiti| taina maggau cattāri nāmāni labhati|

sā  panāyaṃ paṭipadā kassaci bhikkhunau nānā hauti, kassaci catūsupi maggaisu aikāva| buddhānaṃ pana cattāraupi maggā sukhāpaṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñāva ahaisuṃ| tathā dhammasaināpatissa| mahāmauggal‍lānatthairassa pana paṭhamamaggau sukhāpaṭipadau khippābhiñ‍ñau ahausi| upari tayau dukkhāpaṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā| yathā ca paṭipadā, aivaṃ adhipatayaupi kassaci bhikkhunau catūsu maggaisu nānā haunti| kassaci catūsupi aikāva| aivaṃ saṅkhārupaikkhā paṭipadāvisaisaṃ niyamaiti| yathā pana vimaukkhavisaisaṃ niyamaiti, taṃ pubbai vuttamaiva|

802. apica maggau nāma pañ‍cahi kāraṇaihi nāmaṃ labhati sarasaina vā pac‍canīkaina vā saguṇaina vā ārammaṇaina vā āgamanaina vā| sacai hi saṅkhārupaikkhā anic‍catau saṅkhārai sammasitvā vuṭṭhāti, animittavimaukkhaina vimuc‍cati| sacai dukkhatau sammasitvā vuṭṭhāti, appaṇihitavimaukkhaina vimuc‍cati| sacai anattatau sammasitvā vuṭṭhāti, suñ‍ñatavimaukkhaina vimuc‍cati| idaṃ sarasatau nāmaṃ nāma|

yasmā panaisa anic‍cānupassanāya saṅkhārānaṃ ghanavinibbhaugaṃ katvā nic‍canimittadhuvanimittasassatanimittāni pajahantau āgatau, tasmā animittau| dukkhānupassanāya pana sukhasañ‍ñaṃ pahāya paṇidhiṃ patthanaṃ sukkhāpaitvā āgatattā appaṇihitau| anattānupassanāya attasattapuggalasañ‍ñaṃ pahāya saṅkhārānaṃ suñ‍ñatau diṭṭhattā suñ‍ñatauti idaṃ pac‍canīkatau nāmaṃ nāma|

rāgādīhi panaisa suñ‍ñattā suñ‍ñatau, rūpanimittādīnaṃ rāganimittādīnañ‍ñaiva vā abhāvaina animittau, rāgapaṇidhiādīnaṃ abhāvatau appaṇihitauti idamassa saguṇatau nāmaṃ|

svāyaṃ suñ‍ñaṃ animittaṃ appaṇihitañ‍ca nibbānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ karautītipi suñ‍ñatau animittau appaṇihitauti vuc‍cati| idamassa ārammaṇatau nāmaṃ|

803. āgamanaṃ pana duvidhaṃ vipassanāgamanaṃ maggāgamanañ‍ca| tattha maggai vipassanāgamanaṃ labhati, phalai maggāgamanaṃ| anattānupassanā hi suñ‍ñatā nāma, suñ‍ñatavipassanāya maggau suñ‍ñatau, anic‍cānupassanā animittā nāma, animittavipassanāya  maggau animittau| idaṃ pana nāmaṃ na abhidhammapariyāyaina labbhati, suttantapariyāyaina labbhati| tatra hi gautrabhuñāṇaṃ animittaṃ nibbānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā animittanāmakaṃ hutvā sayaṃ āgamanīyaṭṭhānai ṭhatvā maggassa nāmaṃ daitīti vadanti| taina maggau animittauti vuttau| maggāgamanaina pana phalaṃ animittanti yuj‍jatiyaiva| dukkhānupassanā saṅkhāraisu paṇidhiṃ sukkhāpaitvā āgatattā appaṇihitā nāma, appaṇihitavipassanāya maggau appaṇihitau, appaṇihitamaggassa phalaṃ appaṇihitaṃ| aivaṃ vipassanā attanau nāmaṃ maggassa daiti, maggau phalassāti idaṃ āgamanatau nāmaṃ| aivamayaṃ saṅkhārupaikkhā vimaukkhavisaisaṃ niyamaitīti|

saṅkhārupaikkhāñāṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

anulaumañāṇakathā

804. tassa taṃ saṅkhārupaikkhāñāṇaṃ āsaivantassa bhāvaintassa bahulīkarauntassa adhimaukkhasaddhā balavatarā nibbattati, vīriyaṃ supaggahitaṃ hauti, sati sūpaṭṭhitā, cittaṃ susamāhitaṃ, tikkhatarā saṅkhārupaikkhā uppaj‍jati| tassa ‘‘dāni maggau uppaj‍jissatī’’ti saṅkhārupaikkhā saṅkhārai anic‍cāti vā dukkhāti vā anattāti vā sammasitvā bhavaṅgaṃ autarati| bhavaṅgānantaraṃ saṅkhārupaikkhāya katanayainaiva saṅkhārai anic‍cāti vā dukkhāti vā anattāti vā ārammaṇaṃ kurumānaṃ uppaj‍jati manaudvārāvaj‍janaṃ| tatau bhavaṅgaṃ āvaṭṭaitvā uppan‍nassa tassa kiriyacittassānantaraṃ avīcikaṃ cittasantatiṃ anuppabandhamānaṃ tathaiva saṅkhārai ārammaṇaṃ katvā uppaj‍jati paṭhamaṃ javanacittaṃ, yaṃ parikammanti vuc‍cati| tadanantaraṃ tathaiva saṅkhārai ārammaṇaṃ katvā uppaj‍jati dutiyaṃ javanacittaṃ, yaṃ upacāranti vuc‍cati| tadanantarampi tathaiva saṅkhārai ārammaṇaṃ katvā uppaj‍jati tatiyaṃ javanacittaṃ, yaṃ anulaumanti vuc‍cati| idaṃ naisaṃ pāṭiyaik‍kaṃ nāmaṃ|

avisaisaina pana tividhampaitaṃ āsaivanantipi parikammantipi upacārantipi anulaumantipi vattuṃ vaṭṭati| kissānulaumaṃ? purimabhāgapacchimabhāgānaṃ| tañhi purimānaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ  vipassanāñāṇānaṃ tathakic‍catāya ca anulaumaiti, upari ca sattatiṃsāya baudhipakkhiyadhammānaṃ| tañhi anic‍calakkhaṇādivasaina saṅkhārai ārabbha  pavattattā, ‘‘udayabbayavantānaṃyaiva vata dhammānaṃ udayabbayañāṇaṃ uppādavayai addasā’’ti ca, ‘‘bhaṅgavantānaṃyaiva vata bhaṅgānupassanaṃ bhaṅgaṃ addasā’’ti ca, ‘‘sabhayaṃyaiva vata bhayatupaṭṭhānassa bhayatau upaṭṭhita’’nti ca, ‘‘sādīnavaiyaiva vata ādīnavānupassanaṃ ādīnavaṃ addasā’’ti ca, ‘‘nibbinditabbaiyaiva vata nibbidāñāṇaṃ nibbin‍na’’nti ca, ‘‘muñ‍citabbamhiyaiva vata muñ‍citukamyatāñāṇaṃ muñ‍citukāmaṃ jāta’’nti ca, ‘‘paṭisaṅkhātabbaṃyaiva vata paṭisaṅkhāñāṇaina paṭisaṅkhāta’’nti ca, ‘‘upaikkhitabbaṃyaiva vata saṅkhārupaikkhāya upaikkhita’’nti ca atthatau vadamānaṃ viya imaisañ‍ca aṭṭhan‍naṃ ñāṇānaṃ tathakic‍catāya anulaumaiti, upari ca sattatiṃsāya baudhipakkhiyadhammānaṃ tāya paṭipattiyā pattabbattā|

yathā hi dhammikau rājā vinicchayaṭṭhānai nisin‍nau vauhārikamahāmattānaṃ vinicchayaṃ sutvā agatigamanaṃ pahāya majjhattau hutvā ‘‘aivaṃ hautū’’ti anumaudamānau taisañ‍ca vinicchayassa anulaumaiti, paurāṇassa ca rājadhammassa, aivaṃsampadamidaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| rājā viya hi anulaumañāṇaṃ, aṭṭha vauhārikamahāmattā viya aṭṭha ñāṇāni, paurāṇau rājadhammau viya sattatiṃsa baudhipakkhiyā| tattha yathā rājā ‘‘aivaṃ hautū’’ti vadamānau vauhārikānañ‍ca vinicchayassa, rājadhammassa ca anulaumaiti, aivamidaṃ anic‍cādivasaina saṅkhārai ārabbha uppaj‍jamānaṃ aṭṭhan‍nañ‍ca ñāṇānaṃ tathakic‍catāya anulaumaiti, upari ca sattatiṃsāya baudhipakkhiyadhammānaṃ| tainaiva sac‍cānulaumikañāṇanti vuc‍catīti|

anulaumañāṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

vuṭṭhānagāminīvipassanākathā

805. idañ‍ca pana anulaumañāṇaṃ saṅkhārārammaṇāya vuṭṭhānagāminiyā vipassanāya pariyausānaṃ hauti| sabbaina sabbaṃ pana gautrabhuñāṇaṃ vuṭṭhānagāminiyā vipassanāya pariyausānaṃ| idāni tassāyaiva vuṭṭhānagāminiyā vipassanāya asammauhatthaṃ ayaṃ suttasaṃsandanā vaiditabbā|

saiyyathidaṃ  –

ayañhi vuṭṭhānagāminī vipassanā saḷāyatanavibhaṅgasuttai ‘‘atammayataṃ, bhikkhavai, nissāya atammayataṃ āgamma yāyaṃ upaikkhā aikattā aikattasitā, taṃ pajahatha taṃ samatik‍kamathā’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 3.310) aivaṃ atammayatāti vuttā|

alagaddasuttantai ‘‘nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati, virāgā vimuc‍catī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.245) aivaṃ nibbidāti vuttā|

susimasuttantai ‘‘pubbai khau, susima, dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṃ, pacchā nibbānai ñāṇa’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.70) aivaṃ dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇanti vuttā|

pauṭṭhapādasuttantai ‘‘sañ‍ñā khau, pauṭṭhapāda, paṭhamaṃ uppaj‍jati, pacchā ñāṇa’’nti (dī॰ ni॰ 1.416) aivaṃ sañ‍ñagganti vuttā|

dasuttarasuttantai ‘‘paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhi pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅga’’nti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.359) aivaṃ pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅganti vuttā|

paṭisambhidāmaggai ‘‘yā ca muñ‍citukamyatā yā ca paṭisaṅkhānupassanā yā ca saṅkhārupaikkhā, imai dhammā aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.227) aivaṃ tīhi nāmaihi vuttā|

paṭṭhānai ‘‘anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa, anulaumaṃ vaudānassā’’ti (paṭṭhā॰ 1.1.417) aivaṃ tīhi nāmaihi vuttā|

rathavinītasuttantai ‘‘kiṃ panāvusau, paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhatthaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.257) aivaṃ paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhīti vuttā|

itinaikaihi  nāmaihi, kittitā yā mahaisinā|

vuṭṭhānagāminī santā, parisuddhā vipassanā||

vuṭṭhātukāmau saṃsāra-dukkhapaṅkā mahabbhayā|

karaiyya satataṃ tattha, yaugaṃ paṇḍitajātikauti||

iti sādhujanapāmauj‍jatthāya katai visuddhimaggai

pañ‍ñābhāvanādhikārai

paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhiniddaisau nāma

aikavīsatimau paricchaidau|

22. ñāṇadassanavisuddhiniddaisau

paṭhamamaggañāṇakathā

806. itau  paraṃ gautrabhuñāṇaṃ hauti, taṃ maggassa āvaj‍janaṭṭhāniyattā naiva paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhiṃ na ñāṇadassanavisuddhiṃ bhajati, antarā abbauhārikamaiva hauti| vipassanāsautai patitattā pana vipassanāti saṅkhaṃ gacchati| sautāpattimaggau sakadāgāmimaggau anāgāmimaggau arahattamaggauti imaisu pana catūsu maggaisu ñāṇaṃ ñāṇadassanavisuddhi nāma|

tattha paṭhamamaggañāṇaṃ tāva sampādaitukāmaina añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍ci kātabbaṃ nāma natthi| yañhi anaina kātabbaṃ siyā, taṃ anulaumāvasānaṃ vipassanaṃ uppādaintaina katamaiva| aivaṃ uppan‍naanulaumañāṇassa panassa taihi tīhipi anulaumañāṇaihi attanau balānurūpaina thūlathūlai sac‍capaṭicchādakai tamamhi antaradhāpitai sabbasaṅkhāragataisu cittaṃ na pakkhandati, na santiṭṭhati, nādhimuc‍cati, na saj‍jati, na laggati, na bajjhati| padumapalāsatau udakaṃ viya patilīyati patikuṭati pativattati| sabbaṃ nimittārammaṇampi sabbaṃ pavattārammaṇampi palibaudhatau upaṭṭhāti| athassa sabbasmiṃ nimittapavattārammaṇai palibaudhatau upaṭṭhitai anulaumañāṇassa āsaivanantai animittaṃ appavattaṃ visaṅkhāraṃ niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ kurumānaṃ puthuj‍janagauttaṃ puthuj‍janasaṅkhaṃ puthuj‍janabhūmiṃ atik‍kamamānaṃ ariyagauttaṃ ariyasaṅkhaṃ ariyabhūmiṃ auk‍kamamānaṃ nibbānārammaṇai paṭhamāvaṭṭanapaṭhamābhaugapaṭhamasaman‍nāhārabhūtaṃ maggassa anantarasamanantarāsaivanaupanissayanatthivigatavasaina chahi ākāraihi pac‍cayabhāvaṃ sādhayamānaṃ sikhāppattaṃ vipassanāya muddhabhūtaṃ apunarāvaṭṭakaṃ uppaj‍jati gautrabhuñāṇaṃ|

yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ –

‘‘kathaṃ  bahiddhā vuṭṭhānavivaṭṭanai pañ‍ñā gautrabhuñāṇaṃ? uppādaṃ abhibhuyyatīti gautrabhu| pavattaṃ…pai॰… upāyāsaṃ abhibhuyyatīti gautrabhu| bahiddhā saṅkhāranimittaṃ abhibhuyyatīti gautrabhu| anuppādaṃ pakkhandatīti gautrabhu| appavattaṃ…pai॰… anupāyāsaṃ niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ pakkhandatīti  gautrabhu| uppādaṃ abhibhuyyitvā anuppādaṃ pakkhandatīti gautrabhū’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.59) sabbaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|
