807. tatrāyaṃ aikāvaj‍janaina aikavīthiyaṃ pavattamānānampi anulaumagautrabhūnaṃ nānārammaṇai pavattanākāradīpikā upamā – yathā hi mahāmātikaṃ laṅghitvā paratīrai patiṭṭhātukāmau purisau vaigaina dhāvitvā mātikāya aurimatīrai rukkhasākhāya bandhitvā aulambitaṃ raj‍juṃ vā yaṭṭhiṃ vā gahaitvā ul‍laṅghitvā paratīranin‍napauṇapabbhārakāyau hutvā paratīrassa uparibhāgaṃ pattau taṃ muñ‍citvā vaidhamānau paratīrai patitvā saṇikaṃ patiṭṭhāti, aivamaivāyaṃ yaugāvacaraupi bhavayaunigatiṭṭhitinivāsānaṃ paratīrabhūtai nibbānai patiṭṭhātukāmau udayabbayānupassanādinā vaigaina dhāvitvā attabhāvarukkhasākhāya bandhitvā aulambitaṃ rūparaj‍juṃ vā vaidanādīsu añ‍ñataradaṇḍaṃ vā anic‍canti vā dukkhanti vā anattāti vāti anulaumāvaj‍janaina gahaitvā taṃ amuñ‍camānauva paṭhamaina anulaumacittaina ul‍laṅghitvā dutiyaina paratīranin‍napauṇapabbhārakāyau viya nibbānanin‍napauṇapabbhāramānasau hutvā tatiyaina paratīrassa uparibhāgaṃ pattau viya idāni pattabbassa nibbānassa āsan‍nau hutvā tassa cittassa niraudhaina taṃ saṅkhārārammaṇaṃ muñ‍citvā gautrabhucittaina visaṅkhārai paratīrabhūtai nibbānai patati| aikārammaṇai pana aladdhāsaivanatāya vaidhamānau sau purisau viya na tāva suppatiṭṭhitau hauti, tatau maggañāṇaina patiṭṭhātīti|

808. tattha anulaumaṃ sac‍capaṭicchādakaṃ kilaisatamaṃ vinaudaituṃ sak‍kauti, na nibbānamārammaṇaṃ kātuṃ| gautrabhu nibbānamaiva ārammaṇaṃ kātuṃ sak‍kauti, na sac‍capaṭicchādakaṃ tamaṃ vinaudaituṃ| tatrāyaṃ upamā – aikau kira cakkhumā purisau ‘‘nakkhattayaugaṃ jānissāmī’’ti rattibhāgai nikkhamitvā candaṃ passituṃ uddhaṃ ul‍laukaisi, tassa valāhakaihi paṭicchan‍nattā candau na pañ‍ñāyittha| athaikau vātau uṭṭhahitvā thūlathūlai valāhakai viddhaṃsaiti| aparau majjhimai, aparau sukhumaiti| tatau sau purisau vigatavalāhakai nabhai candaṃ disvā nakkhattayaugaṃ añ‍ñāsi|

tattha  tayau valāhakā viya sac‍capaṭicchādakathūlamajjhimasukhumaṃ kilaisandhakāraṃ, tayau vātā viya tīṇi anulaumacittāni, cakkhumā purisau viya gautrabhuñāṇaṃ, candau viya nibbānaṃ, aikaikassa vātassa yathāk‍kamaina valāhakaviddhaṃsanaṃ viya aikaikassa anulaumacittassa sac‍capaṭicchādakatamavinaudanaṃ, vigatavalāhakai nabhai tassa purisassa visuddhacandadassanaṃ viya vigatai sac‍capaṭicchādakai tamai gautrabhuñāṇassa visuddhanibbānadassanaṃ|

yathaiva hi tayau vātā candapaṭicchādakai valāhakaiyaiva viddhaṃsaituṃ sak‍kaunti, na candaṃ daṭṭhuṃ, aivaṃ anulaumāni sac‍capaṭicchādakaṃ tamañ‍ñaiva vinaudaituṃ sak‍kaunti, na nibbānaṃ daṭṭhuṃ| yathā sau purisau candamaiva daṭṭhuṃ sak‍kauti, na valāhakai viddhaṃsaituṃ, aivaṃ gautrabhuñāṇaṃ nibbānamaiva daṭṭhuṃ sak‍kauti, na kilaisatamaṃ vinaudaituṃ| tainaiva caitaṃ maggassa āvaj‍jananti vuc‍cati| tañhi anāvaj‍janampi samānaṃ āvaj‍janaṭṭhānai ṭhatvā ‘‘aivaṃ nibbattāhī’’ti maggassa sañ‍ñaṃ datvā viya nirujjhati| maggaupi taina din‍nasañ‍ñaṃ amuñ‍citvāva avīcisantativasaina taṃ ñāṇaṃ anuppabandhamānau anibbiddhapubbaṃ apadālitapubbaṃ laubhakkhandhaṃ dausakkhandhaṃ mauhakkhandhaṃ nibbijjhamānauva padālayamānauva nibbattati|

809. tatrāyaṃ upamā – aikau kira issāsau aṭṭhausabhamattai padaisai phalakasataṃ ṭhapāpaitvā vatthaina mukhaṃ vaiṭhaitvā saraṃ san‍nahitvā cak‍kayantai aṭṭhāsi| añ‍ñau purisau cak‍kayantaṃ āvijjhitvā yadā issāsassa phalakaṃ abhimukhaṃ hauti, tadā tattha daṇḍakaina sañ‍ñaṃ daiti| issāsau daṇḍakasañ‍ñaṃ amuñ‍citvāva saraṃ khipitvā phalakasataṃ nibbijjhati| tattha daṇḍakasañ‍ñaṃ viya gautrabhuñāṇaṃ, issāsau viya maggañāṇaṃ| issāsassa daṇḍakasañ‍ñaṃ amuñ‍citvāva phalakasatanibbijjhanaṃ viya maggañāṇassa gautrabhuñāṇaina din‍nasañ‍ñaṃ amuñ‍citvāva nibbānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā anibbiddhapubbānaṃ apadālitapubbānaṃ laubhadausamauhakkhandhānaṃ nibbijjhanapadālanaṃ|

810. na kaivalañ‍caisa maggau laubhakkhandhādīnaṃ nibbijjhanamaiva karauti, apica khau anamataggasaṃsāravaṭṭadukkhasamuddaṃ sausaiti, sabbaapāyadvārāni pidahati, sattan‍naṃ ariyadhanānaṃ sammukhībhāvaṃ karauti, aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ micchāmaggaṃ pajahati, sabbavairabhayāni vūpasamaiti, sammāsambuddhassa aurasaputtabhāvaṃ upanaiti, añ‍ñaisañ‍ca anaikasatānaṃ ānisaṃsānaṃ paṭilābhāya saṃvattatīti aivaṃ anaikānisaṃsadāyakaina sautāpattimaggaina sampayuttaṃ ñāṇaṃ sautāpattimaggai ñāṇanti|

paṭhamamaggañāṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

sautāpan‍napuggalakathā

811. imassa pana ñāṇassa anantaraṃ tassaiva vipākabhūtāni dvai tīṇi vā phalacittāni uppaj‍janti| anantaravipākattāyaiva hi laukuttarakusalānaṃ ‘‘samādhimānantarikañ‍ñamāhū’’ti (khu॰ pā॰ 6.5) ca ‘‘dandhaṃ ānantarikaṃ pāpuṇāti āsavānaṃ khayāyā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.162) ca ādi vuttaṃ| kaici pana aikaṃ dvai tīṇi cattāri vā phalacittānīti vadanti, taṃ na gahaitabbaṃ|

anulaumassa hi āsaivanantai gautrabhuñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati| tasmā sabbantimaina paricchaidaina dvīhi anulaumacittaihi bhavitabbaṃ| na hi aikaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaṃ labhati, sattacittaparamā ca aikāvaj‍janavīthi| tasmā yassa dvai anulaumāni, tassa tatiyaṃ gautrabhu catutthaṃ maggacittaṃ tīṇi phalacittāni haunti| yassa tīṇi anulaumāni, tassa catutthaṃ gautrabhu pañ‍camaṃ maggacittaṃ dvai phalacittāni haunti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘dvai tīṇi vā phalacittāni uppaj‍jantī’’ti|

kaici pana yassa cattāri anulaumāni, tassa pañ‍camaṃ gautrabhu chaṭṭhaṃ maggacittaṃ aikaṃ phalacittanti vadanti, taṃ pana yasmā catutthaṃ pañ‍camaṃ vā appaiti, na tatau paraṃ āsan‍nabhavaṅgattāti paṭikkhittaṃ| tasmā na sāratau pac‍caitabbaṃ|

812. aittāvatā ca panaisa sautāpan‍nau nāma dutiyau ariyapuggalau hauti| bhusaṃ pamattaupi hutvā sattakkhattuṃ daivaisu ca manussaisu ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantassa karaṇasamatthau hauti| phalapariyausānai panassa cittaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ autarati, tatau bhavaṅgaṃ upacchinditvā maggapac‍cavaikkhaṇatthāya uppaj‍jati manaudvārāvaj‍janaṃ, tasmiṃ niruddhai paṭipāṭiyā satta maggapac‍cavaikkhaṇajavanānīti| puna bhavaṅgaṃ autaritvā tainaiva nayaina  phalādīnaṃ pac‍cavaikkhaṇatthāya āvaj‍janādīni  uppaj‍janti| yaisaṃ uppattiyā aisa maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pahīnakilaisai pac‍cavaikkhati, avasiṭṭhakilaisai pac‍cavaikkhati, nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati|

sau hi ‘‘iminā vatāhaṃ maggaina āgatau’’ti maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, tatau ‘‘ayaṃ mai ānisaṃsau laddhau’’ti phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| tatau ‘‘imai nāma mai kilaisā pahīnā’’ti pahīnakilaisai pac‍cavaikkhati| tatau ‘‘imai nāma mai kilaisā avasiṭṭhā’’ti uparimaggattayavajjhai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhati| avasānai ca ‘‘ayaṃ mai dhammau ārammaṇatau paṭividdhau’’ti amataṃ nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| iti sautāpan‍nassa ariyasāvakassa pañ‍ca pac‍cavaikkhaṇāni haunti| yathā ca sautāpan‍nassa, aivaṃ sakadāgāmianāgāmīnampi| arahatau pana avasiṭṭhakilaisapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ nāma natthīti| aivaṃ sabbānipi aikūnavīsati pac‍cavaikkhaṇāni nāma|

uk‍kaṭṭhaparicchaidauyaiva caisau| pahīnāvasiṭṭhakilaisapac‍cavaikkhaṇañhi saikkhānampi hauti vā na vā| tassa hi pac‍cavaikkhaṇassa abhāvainaiva mahānāmau bhagavantaṃ pucchi ‘‘kausu nāma mai dhammau ajjhattaṃ appahīnau, yaina mai aikadā laubhadhammāpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhantī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.175) sabbaṃ vitthāratau vaiditabbaṃ|

dutiyamaggañāṇakathā

813. aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā pana sau sautāpan‍nau ariyasāvakau tasmiñ‍ñaiva vā āsanai nisin‍nau, aparaina vā samayaina kāmarāgabyāpādānaṃ tanubhāvāya dutiyāya bhūmiyā pattiyā yaugaṃ karauti| sau indriyabalabaujjhaṅgāni samaudhānaitvā tadaiva rūpavaidanāsañ‍ñāsaṅkhāraviñ‍ñāṇabhaidaṃ saṅkhāragataṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhamanattāti ñāṇaina parimaddati, parivattaiti, vipassanāvīthiṃ augāhati| tassaivaṃ paṭipan‍nassa vuttanayainaiva saṅkhārupaikkhāvasānai aikāvaj‍janaina anulaumagautrabhuñāṇaisu uppan‍naisu gautrabhuanantaraṃ sakadāgāmimaggau uppaj‍jati| taina sampayuttaṃ ñāṇaṃ sakadāgāmimaggai ñāṇanti|

dutiyañāṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tatiyamaggañāṇakathā

814. imassāpi  ñāṇassa anantaraṃ vuttanayainaiva phalacittāni vaiditabbāni| aittāvatā caisa sakadāgāmī nāma catutthau ariyapuggalau hauti sakiṃdaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantakaraṇasamatthau| tatau paraṃ pac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā ca sau sakadāgāmī ariyasāvakau tasmiñ‍ñaiva vā āsanai nisin‍nau aparaina vā samayaina kāmarāgabyāpādānaṃ anavasaisappahānāya tatiyāya bhūmiyā pattiyā yaugaṃ karauti, sau indriyabalabaujjhaṅgāni samaudhānaitvā tadaiva saṅkhāragataṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhamanattāti ñāṇaina parimaddati, parivattaiti, vipassanāvīthiṃ augāhati| tassaivaṃ paṭipan‍nassa vuttanayainaiva saṅkhārupaikkhāvasānai aikāvaj‍janaina anulaumagautrabhuñāṇaisu uppan‍naisu gautrabhuanantaraṃ anāgāmimaggau uppaj‍jati, taina sampayuttaṃ ñāṇaṃ anāgāmimaggai ñāṇanti|

tatiyañāṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

catutthamaggañāṇakathā

815. imassapi ñāṇassa anantaraṃ vuttanayainaiva phalacittāni vaiditabbāni| aittāvatā caisa anāgāmī nāma chaṭṭhau ariyapuggalau hauti aupapātikau tatthaparinibbāyī anāvattidhammau paṭisandhivasaina imaṃ laukaṃ puna anāgantā| tatau paraṃ pac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā ca sau anāgāmī ariyasāvakau tasmiñ‍ñaiva vā āsanai nisin‍nau, aparaina vā samayaina rūpārūparāgamānauddhac‍caavij‍jānaṃ anavasaisappahānāya catutthāya bhūmiyā pattiyā yaugaṃ karauti, sau indriyabalabaujjhaṅgāni samaudhānaitvā tadaiva saṅkhāragataṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhamanattāti ñāṇaina parimaddati, parivattaiti, vipassanāvīthiṃ augāhati| tassaivaṃ paṭipan‍nassa vuttanayainaiva saṅkhārupaikkhāvasānai aikāvaj‍janaina anulaumagautrabhuñāṇaisu  uppan‍naisu gautrabhuanantaraṃ arahattamaggau uppaj‍jati, taina sampayuttaṃ ñāṇaṃ arahattamaggai ñāṇanti|

catutthañāṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

arahantapuggalakathā

816. imassapi  ñāṇassa anantaraṃ vuttanayainaiva phalacittāni vaiditabbāni| aittāvatā caisa arahā nāma aṭṭhamau ariyapuggalau hauti mahākhīṇāsavau antimadaihadhārī auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammādañ‍ñā vimuttau sadaivakassa laukassa aggadakkhiṇaiyyauti|

iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘sautāpattimaggau sakadāgāmimaggau anāgāmimaggau arahattamaggauti imaisu pana catūsu maggaisu ñāṇaṃ ñāṇadassanavisuddhi nāmā’’ti, taṃ imāni iminā anuk‍kamaina pattabbāni cattāri ñāṇāni sandhāya vuttaṃ|

baudhipakkhiyakathā

817. idāni imissāyaiva catuñāṇāya ñāṇadassanavisuddhiyā ānubhāvavijānanatthaṃ –

paripuṇṇabaudhipakkhiya, bhāvau vuṭṭhānabalasamāyaugau|

yai yaina pahātabbā, dhammā taisaṃ pahānañ‍ca||

kic‍cāni pariñ‍ñādīni, yāni vuttāni abhisamayakālai|

tāni ca yathāsabhāvaina, jānitabbāni sabbānīti||

818. tattha paripuṇṇabaudhipakkhiya, bhāvauti baudhipakkhiyānaṃ paripuṇṇabhāvau| cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau iddhipādā, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, satta baujjhaṅgā, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggauti hi imai sattatiṃsa dhammā bujjhanaṭṭhaina baudhauti laddhanāmassa ariyamaggassa pakkhai bhavattā baudhipakkhiyā nāma| pakkhai bhavattāti upakārabhāvai ṭhitattā|

819. taisu  taisu ārammaṇaisu aukkhanditvā pakkhanditvā upaṭṭhānatau paṭṭhānaṃ| satiyaiva paṭṭhānaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ| kāyavaidanācittadhammaisu panassā asubha-dukkha-anic‍ca-anattākāragahaṇavasaina  subha-sukha-nic‍ca-atta-sañ‍ñāpahānakic‍casādhanavasaina ca pavattitau catudhā bhaidau hauti| tasmā cattārau satipaṭṭhānāti vuc‍canti|

820. padahanti aitaināti padhānaṃ| saubhanaṃ padhānaṃ sammappadhānaṃ| sammā vā padahanti aitaināti sammappadhānaṃ| saubhanaṃ vā taṃ kilaisavirūpattavirahatau padhānañ‍ca hitasukhanipphādakattaina saiṭṭhabhāvāvahanatau padhānabhāvakāraṇatau cāti sammappadhānaṃ| vīriyassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| tayidaṃ uppan‍nānuppan‍nānaṃ akusalānaṃ pahānānuppattikic‍caṃ anuppan‍nuppan‍nānañ‍ca kusalānaṃ uppattiṭṭhitikic‍caṃ sādhayatīti catubbidhaṃ hauti, tasmā cattārau sammappadhānāti vuc‍canti|

821. pubbai vuttaina ijjhanaṭṭhaina iddhi| tassā sampayuttāya pubbaṅgamaṭṭhaina phalabhūtāya pubbabhāgakāraṇaṭṭhaina ca iddhiyā pādauti iddhipādau| sau chandādivasaina catubbidhau hauti, tasmā cattārau iddhipādāti vuc‍canti| yathāha – ‘‘cattārau iddhipādā chandiddhipādau cittiddhipādau vīriyiddhipādau vīmaṃsiddhipādau’’ti (vibha॰ 457)| imai laukuttarāva| laukiyā pana ‘‘chandañ‍cai bhikkhu adhipatiṃ karitvā labhati samādhiṃ, labhati cittassa aikaggataṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati chandasamādhī’’tiādivacanatau (vibha॰ 432) chandādiadhipativasaina paṭiladdhadhammāpi haunti|

822. assaddhiyakausaj‍japamādavikkhaipasammauhānaṃ abhibhavanatau abhibhavanasaṅkhātaina adhipatiyaṭṭhaina indriyaṃ| assaddhiyādīhi ca anabhibhavanīyatau akampiyaṭṭhaina balaṃ| tadubhayampi saddhādivasaina pañ‍cavidhaṃ hauti, tasmā pañ‍cindriyāni pañ‍ca balānīti vuc‍canti|

823. bujjhanakasattassa pana aṅgabhāvaina satiādayau satta baujjhaṅgā| niyyānikaṭṭhaina ca sammādiṭṭhiādayau aṭṭha maggaṅgā haunti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘satta baujjhaṅgā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau’’ti|

824. iti  imai sattatiṃsa baudhipakkhiyadhammā pubbabhāgai laukiyavipassanāya vattamānāya cuddasavidhaina kāyaṃ pariggaṇhatau ca kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānaṃ, navavidhaina vaidanaṃ pariggaṇhatau ca vaidanānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānaṃ, sauḷasavidhaina cittaṃ pariggaṇhatau ca cittānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānaṃ, pañ‍cavidhaina dhammai pariggaṇhatau ca dhammānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānaṃ| imasmiṃ attabhāvai  anuppan‍napubbaṃ parassa uppan‍naṃ akusalaṃ disvā ‘‘yathā paṭipan‍nassaitaṃ uppan‍naṃ, na tathā paṭipaj‍jissāmi aivaṃ mai aitaṃ nuppaj‍jissatī’’ti, tassa anuppādāya vāyamanakālai paṭhamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ| attanau samudācārappattaṃ akusalaṃ disvā tassa pahānāya vāyamanakālai dutiyaṃ| imasmiṃ attabhāvai anuppan‍napubbaṃ jhānaṃ vā vipassanaṃ vā uppādaituṃ vāyamantassa tatiyaṃ| uppan‍naṃ yathā na parihāyati, aivaṃ punappunaṃ uppādaintassa catutthaṃ sammappadhānaṃ| chandaṃ dhuraṃ katvā kusaluppādanakālai chandiddhipādau| micchāvācāya viramaṇakālai sammāvācāti aivaṃ nānācittaisu labbhanti| imaisaṃ pana catun‍naṃ ñāṇānaṃ uppattikālai aikacittai labbhanti| phalakkhaṇai ṭhapaitvā cattārau sammappadhānai avasaisā taittiṃsa labbhanti|

825. aivaṃ aikacittai labbhamānaisu caitaisu aikāva nibbānārammaṇā sati kāyādīsu subhasañ‍ñādippahānakic‍casādhanavasaina cattārau satipaṭṭhānāti vuc‍cati| aikamaiva ca vīriyaṃ anuppan‍nānaṃ anuppādādikic‍casādhanavasaina cattārau sammappadhānāti vuc‍cati| saisaisu pana hāpanavaḍḍhanaṃ natthi|

826. apica taisu –

nava aikavidhā aikau, dvaidhātha catu pañ‍cadhā|

aṭṭhadhā navadhā caiva, iti chaddhā bhavanti tai||

nava aikavidhāti chandau, cittaṃ, pīti, passaddhi, upaikkhā, saṅkappau, vācā, kammantau, ājīvauti imai nava chandiddhipādādivasaina aikavidhāva haunti, na añ‍ñaṃ kauṭṭhāsaṃ bhajanti| aikau dvaidhāti saddhā indriya, balavasaina dvaidhā ṭhitā| atha catu pañ‍cadhāti athañ‍ñau aikau catudhā, añ‍ñau pañ‍cadhā ṭhitauti atthau| tattha samādhi aikau indriya, bala, baujjhaṅga, maggaṅgavasaina catudhā ṭhitau| pañ‍ñā taisañ‍ca catun‍naṃ iddhipādakauṭṭhāsassa ca  vasaina pañ‍cadhā| aṭṭhadhā navadhā caivāti aparau aikau aṭṭhadhā, aikau navadhā ṭhitauti atthau| catusatipaṭṭhāna, indriya, bala, baujjhaṅga, maggaṅgavasaina sati aṭṭhadhā ṭhitā| catusammappadhāna, iddhipāda, indriya, bala, baujjhaṅga, maggaṅgavasaina vīriyaṃ navadhāti| aivaṃ –

cuddasaiva  asambhin‍nā, hauntaitai baudhipakkhiyā|

kauṭṭhāsatau sattavidhā, sattatiṃsappabhaidatau||

sakic‍canipphādanatau, sarūpaina ca vuttitau|

sabbaiva ariyamaggassa, sambhavai sambhavanti taiti||

aivaṃ tāvaittha paripuṇṇabaudhipakkhiyabhāvau jānitabbau|

vuṭṭhānabalasamāyaugakathā

827. vuṭṭhānabalasamāyaugauti vuṭṭhānañ‍caiva balasamāyaugau ca| laukiyavipassanā hi nimittārammaṇattā caiva pavattikāraṇassa ca samudayassa asamucchindanatau naiva nimittā na pavattā vuṭṭhāti| gautrabhuñāṇaṃ samudayassa asamucchindanatau pavattā na vuṭṭhāti| nibbānārammaṇatau pana nimittā vuṭṭhātīti aikatau vuṭṭhānaṃ hauti| taināha ‘‘bahiddhāvuṭṭhānavivaṭṭanai pañ‍ñā gautrabhuñāṇa’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ mātikā 1.10)| tathā ‘‘uppādā vivaṭṭitvā anuppādaṃ pakkhandatīti gautrabhu, pavattā vivaṭṭitvā’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.59) sabbaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| imāni pana cattāripi ñāṇāni animittārammaṇattā nimittatau vuṭṭhahanti, samudayassa samucchindanatau pavattā vuṭṭhahantīti dubhatau vuṭṭhānāni haunti|

taina vuttaṃ –

‘‘kathaṃ dubhatau vuṭṭhānavivaṭṭanai pañ‍ñā maggai ñāṇaṃ?

‘‘sautāpattimaggakkhaṇai dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhiyā vuṭṭhāti, tadanuvattakakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi vuṭṭhāti| taina vuc‍cati dubhatau vuṭṭhānavivaṭṭanai pañ‍ñā maggai ñāṇaṃ| abhiniraupanaṭṭhaina sammāsaṅkappau micchāsaṅkappā…pai॰… pariggahaṭṭhaina sammāvācā micchāvācāya| samuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina sammākammantau| vaudānaṭṭhaina sammāājīvau| paggahaṭṭhaina sammāvāyāmau| upaṭṭhānaṭṭhaina sammāsati| avikkhaipaṭṭhaina sammāsamādhi micchāsamādhitau vuṭṭhāti, tadanuvattakakilaisaihi ca  khandhaihi ca vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi vuṭṭhāti| taina vuc‍cati ‘dubhatau vuṭṭhānavivaṭṭanai pañ‍ñā maggai ñāṇa’nti|

‘‘sakadāgāmimaggakkhaṇai dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… avikkhaipaṭṭhaina sammāsamādhi auḷārikā kāmarāgasaṃyaujanā paṭighasaṃyaujanā auḷārikā kāmarāgānusayā paṭighānusayā vuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

‘‘anāgāmimaggakkhaṇai dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… avikkhaipaṭṭhaina sammāsamādhi anusahagatā kāmarāgasaṃyaujanā paṭighasaṃyaujanā anusahagatā kāmarāgānusayā paṭighānusayā vuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

‘‘arahattamaggakkhaṇai dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… avikkhaipaṭṭhaina sammāsamādhi rūparāgā arūparāgā mānā uddhac‍cā avij‍jāya mānānusayā bhavarāgānusayā avij‍jānusayā vuṭṭhāti, tadanuvattakakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi vuṭṭhāti| taina vuc‍cati ‘dubhatau vuṭṭhānavivaṭṭanai pañ‍ñā maggai ñāṇa’’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.61)|

828. laukiyānañ‍ca aṭṭhan‍naṃ samāpattīnaṃ bhāvanākālai samathabalaṃ adhikaṃ hauti| anic‍cānupassanādīnaṃ bhāvanākālai vipassanābalaṃ| ariyamaggakkhaṇai pana yuganaddhā tai dhammā pavattanti añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ anativattanaṭṭhaina| tasmā imaisu catūsupi ñāṇaisu ubhayabalasamāyaugau hauti| yathāha –

‘‘uddhac‍casahagatakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca vuṭṭhahatau cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samādhi niraudhagaucarau, avij‍jāsahagatakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca vuṭṭhahatau anupassanaṭṭhaina vipassanā niraudhagaucarā| iti vuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina samathavipassanā aikarasā  haunti, yuganaddhā haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nātivattantīti| taina vuc‍cati vuṭṭhānaṭṭhaina samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.5)|

aivamaittha vuṭṭhānabalasamāyaugau vaiditabbau|

pahātabbadhammapahānakathā

829. yai  yaina pahātabbā dhammā, taisaṃ pahānañ‍cāti imaisu pana catūsu ñāṇaisu yai dhammā yaina ñāṇaina pahātabbā, taisaṃ pahānañ‍ca jānitabbaṃ| aitāni hi yathāyaugaṃ saṃyaujanakilaisamicchattalaukadhammamacchariyavipal‍lāsaganthaagatiāsavaaughayauganīvaraṇaparāmāsaupādānaanusayamalaakusalakammapathacittuppādasaṅkhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānakarāni|

tattha saṃyaujanānīti khandhaihi khandhānaṃ phalaina kammassa dukkhaina vā sattānaṃ saṃyaujakattā rūparāgādayau dasa dhammā vuc‍canti| yāvañhi tai, tāva aitaisaṃ anuparamauti| tatrāpi rūparāgau arūparāgau mānau uddhac‍caṃ avij‍jāti imai pañ‍ca uddhaṃnibbattanakakhandhādisaṃyaujakattā uddhaṃbhāgiyasaṃyaujanāni nāma| sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi vicikicchā sīlabbataparāmāsau kāmarāgau paṭighauti imai pañ‍ca adhaunibbattanakakhandhādisaṃyaujakattā adhaubhāgiyasaṃyaujanāni nāma|

kilaisāti sayaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhattā sampayuttadhammānañ‍ca saṃkilaisikattā laubhau dausau mauhau mānau diṭṭhi vicikicchā thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappanti imai dasa dhammā|

micchattāti micchāpavattanatau micchādiṭṭhi micchāsaṅkappau micchāvācā micchākammantau micchāājīvau micchāvāyāmau micchāsati micchāsamādhīti imai aṭṭha dhammā| micchāvimuttimicchāñāṇaihi vā saddhiṃ dasa|

laukadhammāti laukappavattiyā sati anuparamadhammakattā lābhau alābhau yasau ayasau sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ nindā pasaṃsāti imai aṭṭha| idha pana kāraṇaupacāraina lābhādivatthukassa anunayassa alābhādivatthukassa paṭighassa caitaṃ laukadhammaggahaṇaina gahaṇaṃ katanti vaiditabbaṃ|

macchariyānīti  āvāsamacchariyaṃ kulamacchariyaṃ lābhamacchariyaṃ dhammamacchariyaṃ vaṇṇamacchariyanti imāsu āvāsādīsu añ‍ñaisaṃ sādhāraṇabhāvaṃ asahanākāraina pavattāni pañ‍ca macchariyāni|

vipal‍lāsāti  anic‍cadukkhaanattaasubhaisuyaiva vatthūsu ‘‘nic‍caṃ sukhaṃ attā subha’’nti aivaṃ pavattau sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsauti imai tayau|

ganthāti nāmakāyassa caiva rūpakāyassa ca ganthanatau abhijjhādayau cattārau| tathā hi tai abhijjhā kāyaganthau, byāpādau kāyaganthau, sīlabbataparāmāsau kāyaganthau, idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisau kāyaganthau ic‍caiva vuttā|

agatīti chandadausamauhabhayaihi akattabbakaraṇassa, kattabbākaraṇassa ca adhivacanaṃ| tañhi ariyaihi agantabbattā agatīti vuc‍cati|

āsavāti ārammaṇavasaina āgautrabhutau, ābhavaggatau ca savanā, asaṃvutaihi vā dvāraihi ghaṭachiddaihi udakaṃ viya savanatau nic‍capaggharaṇaṭṭhaina saṃsāradukkhassa vā savanatau kāmarāgabhavarāgamicchādiṭṭhiavij‍jānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

bhavasāgarai ākaḍḍhanaṭṭhaina duruttaraṇaṭṭhaina ca aughātipi, ārammaṇaviyaugassa caiva dukkhaviyaugassa ca appadānatau yaugātipi taisañ‍ñaiva adhivacanaṃ|

nīvaraṇānīti cittassa āvaraṇanīvaraṇapaṭicchādanaṭṭhaina kāmacchandādayau pañ‍ca|

parāmāsauti tassa tassa dhammassa sabhāvaṃ atik‍kamma paratau abhūtaṃ sabhāvaṃ āmasanākāraina pavattanatau micchādiṭṭhiyā aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

upādānānīti sabbākāraina paṭic‍casamuppādaniddaisai vuttāni kāmupādānādīni cattāri|

anusayāti thāmagataṭṭhaina kāmarāgānusayau, paṭigha, māna, diṭṭhi, vicikicchā, bhavarāga, avij‍jānusayauti aivaṃ vuttā kāmarāgādayau satta| tai hi thāmagatattā punappunaṃ kāmarāgādīnaṃ uppattihaitubhāvaina anusaintiyaivāti anusayā|

malāti  tailañ‍janakalalaṃ viya sayañ‍ca asuddhattā, añ‍ñaisañ‍ca asuddhabhāvakaraṇatau laubhadausamauhā tayau|

akusalakammapathāti akusalakammabhāvaina caiva duggatīnañ‍ca pathabhāvaina pāṇātipātau adin‍nādānaṃ kāmaisumicchācārau musāvādau pisuṇavācā pharusavācā samphappalāpau abhijjhā byāpādau micchādiṭṭhīti imai dasa|

akusalacittuppādāti laubhamūlā aṭṭha dausamūlā dvai mauhamūlā dvaiti imai dvādasa|

830. iti aitaisaṃ saṃyaujanādīnaṃ dhammānaṃ aitāni yathāyaugaṃ pahānakarāni| kathaṃ? saṃyaujanaisu tāva sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi vicikicchā sīlabbataparāmāsau apāyagamanīyā ca kāmarāgapaṭighāti aitai pañ‍ca dhammā paṭhamañāṇavajjhā, saisā kāmarāgapaṭighā auḷārikā dutiyañāṇavajjhā, sukhumā tatiyañāṇavajjhā, rūparāgādayau pañ‍capi catutthañāṇavajjhā aiva| parataupi ca yattha yattha aivasaddaina niyamaṃ na karissāma| tattha tattha yaṃ yaṃ ‘‘upariñāṇavajjhau’’ti vakkhāma, sau sau purimañāṇaihi hatāpāyagamanīyādibhāvauva hutvā upariñāṇavajjhau hautīti vaiditabbau|

kilaisaisu diṭṭhivicikicchā paṭhamañāṇavajjhā, dausau tatiyañāṇavajjhau, laubhamauhamānathinauddhac‍caahirikaanauttappāni catutthañāṇavajjhāni|

micchattaisu micchādiṭṭhi musāvādau micchākammantau micchāājīvauti imai paṭhamañāṇavajjhā, micchāsaṅkappau pisuṇavācā pharusavācāti imai tatiyañāṇavajjhā, caitanāyaiva caittha vācāti vaiditabbā| samphappalāpamicchāvāyāmasatisamādhivimuttiñāṇāni catutthañāṇavajjhāni|

laukadhammaisu paṭighau tatiyañāṇavajjhau, anunayau catutthañāṇavajjhau, yasai ca pasaṃsāya ca anunayau catutthañāṇavajjhauti aikai| macchariyāni paṭhamañāṇavajjhānaiva|

vipal‍lāsaisu  anic‍cai nic‍caṃ, anattani attāti ca sañ‍ñācittadiṭṭhivipal‍lāsā, dukkhai sukhaṃ, asubhai subhanti diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau cāti imai paṭhamañāṇavajjhā, asubhai subhanti sañ‍ñācittavipal‍lāsā tatiyañāṇavajjhā, dukkhai sukhanti sañ‍ñācittavipal‍lāsā catutthañāṇavajjhā|

ganthaisu sīlabbataparāmasaidaṃsac‍cābhinivaisakāyaganthā paṭhamañāṇavajjhā, byāpādakāyaganthau tatiyañāṇavajjhau, itarau catutthañāṇavajjhau|

agati paṭhamañāṇavajjhāva|

āsavaisu diṭṭhāsavau paṭhamañāṇavajjhau, kāmāsavau tatiyañāṇavajjhau, itarai dvai catutthañāṇavajjhā| aughayaugaisupi aisaiva nayau|

nīvaraṇaisu vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ paṭhamañāṇavajjhaṃ, kāmacchandau byāpādau kuk‍kuc‍canti tīṇi tatiyañāṇavajjhāni, thinamiddhauddhac‍cāni catutthañāṇavajjhāni|

parāmāsau paṭhamañāṇavajjhauva|

upādānaisu sabbaisampi laukiyadhammānaṃ vatthukāmavasaina kāmāti āgatattā rūpārūparāgaupi kāmupādānai patati, tasmā taṃ catutthañāṇavajjhaṃ, saisāni paṭhamañāṇavajjhāni|

anusayaisu diṭṭhivicikicchānusayā paṭhamañāṇavajjhāva, kāmarāgapaṭighānusayā tatiyañāṇavajjhā, mānabhavarāgāvij‍jānusayā catutthañāṇavajjhā|

malaisu dausamalaṃ tatiyañāṇavajjhaṃ, itarāni catutthañāṇavajjhāni|

akusalakammapathaisu pāṇātipātau adin‍nādānaṃ micchācārau musāvādau micchādiṭṭhīti imai paṭhamañāṇavajjhā, pisuṇavācā pharusavācā byāpādauti tayau tatiyañāṇavajjhā, samphappalāpābhijjhā catutthañāṇavajjhā|

akusalacittuppādaisu cattārau diṭṭhigatasampayuttā vicikicchāsampayuttau cāti pañ‍ca paṭhamañāṇavajjhāva, dvai paṭighasampayuttā tatiyañāṇavajjhā, saisā catutthañāṇavajjhāti|

yañ‍ca yaina vajjhaṃ, taṃ taina pahātabbaṃ nāma| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘iti aitaisaṃ saṃyaujanādīnaṃ dhammānaṃ aitāni yathāyaugaṃ pahānakarānī’’ti|

831. kiṃ  panaitāni aitai dhammai atītānāgatai pajahanti udāhu pac‍cuppan‍naiti| kiṃ panaittha yadi tāva atītānāgatai, aphalau vāyāmau āpaj‍jati| kasmā? pahātabbānaṃ natthitāya| atha pac‍cuppan‍nai, tathāpi aphalau, vāyāmaina saddhiṃ pahātabbānaṃ atthitāya, saṃkilaisikā ca maggabhāvanā āpaj‍jati, vippayuttatā vā kilaisānaṃ, na ca pac‍cuppan‍nakilaisau cittavippayuttau nāma atthīti| nāyaṃ āvaiṇikā caudanā| pāḷiyaṃyaiva hi ‘‘svāyaṃ kilaisai pajahati, atītai kilaisai pajahati, anāgatai kilaisai pajahati, pac‍cuppan‍nai kilaisai pajahatī’’ti vatvā, puna ‘‘hañ‍ci atītai kilaisai pajahati, tainahi khīṇaṃ khaipaiti, niruddhaṃ niraudhaiti, vigataṃ vigamaiti, atthaṅgataṃ atthaṅgamaiti| atītaṃ yaṃ natthi, taṃ pajahatī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 3.21) ca vatvā, ‘‘na atītai kilaisai pajahatī’’ti paṭikkhittaṃ|

tathā ‘‘hañ‍ci anāgatai kilaisai pajahati, tainahi ajātaṃ pajahati, anibbattaṃ pajahati, anuppan‍naṃ pajahati, apātubhūtaṃ pajahati| anāgataṃ yaṃ natthi, taṃ pajahatī’’ti ca vatvā, ‘‘na anāgatai kilaisai pajahatī’’ti paṭikkhittaṃ|

tathā ‘‘hañ‍ci pac‍cuppan‍nai kilaisai pajahati, tainahi rattau rāgaṃ pajahati| duṭṭhau dausaṃ, mūḷhau mauhaṃ, vinibaddhau mānaṃ, parāmaṭṭhau diṭṭhiṃ, vikkhaipagatau uddhac‍caṃ, aniṭṭhaṅgatau vicikicchaṃ, thāmagatau anusayaṃ pajahati| kaṇhasuk‍kā dhammā yuganaddhāva vattanti| saṃkilaisikā maggabhāvanā hautī’’ti ca vatvā, ‘‘na atītai kilaisai pajahati, na anāgatai, na pac‍cuppan‍nai kilaisai pajahatī’’ti sabbaṃ paṭikkhipitvā, ‘‘tainahi natthi maggabhāvanā, natthi phalasacchikiriyā, natthi kilaisappahānaṃ, natthi dhammābhisamayau’’ti pañhāpariyausānai ‘‘na hi natthi maggabhāvanā…pai॰… natthi dhammābhisamayau’’ti paṭijānitvā ‘‘yathā kathaṃ viyā’’ti vuttai idaṃ vuttaṃ –

‘‘saiyyathāpi taruṇau rukkhau ajātaphalau, tamainaṃ purisau mūlai chindaiyya, yai tassa rukkhassa ajātaphalā, tai ajātāyaiva na jāyanti, anibbattāyaiva na nibbattanti, anuppan‍nāyaiva na uppaj‍janti, apātubhūtāyaiva na pātubhavanti, aivamaiva  uppādau haitu uppādau pac‍cayau kilaisānaṃ nibbattiyāti uppādai ādīnavaṃ disvā anuppādai cittaṃ pakkhandati, anuppādai cittassa pakkhandattā yai āyūhanapac‍cayā kilaisā nibbattaiyyuṃ, tai ajātāyaiva na jāyanti…pai॰… apātubhūtāyaiva na pātubhavanti, aivaṃ haituniraudhā dukkhaniraudhau| pavattaṃ haitu…pai॰… nimittaṃ haitu…pai॰… āyūhanā haitu…pai॰… anāyūhanai cittassa pakkhandattā yai āyūhanapac‍cayā kilaisā nibbattaiyyuṃ, tai ajātāyaiva…pai॰… apātubhūtāyaiva na pātubhavanti, aivaṃ haituniraudhā dukkhaniraudhau| aivaṃ atthi maggabhāvanā, atthi phalasacchikiriyā, atthi kilaisappahānaṃ, atthi dhammābhisamayau’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 3.21)|

832. aitaina kiṃ dīpitaṃ hauti? bhūmiladdhānaṃ kilaisānaṃ pahānaṃ dīpitaṃ hauti| bhūmiladdhā pana kiṃ atītānāgatā udāhu pac‍cuppan‍nāti| bhūmiladdhuppan‍nā aiva nāma tai|

833. uppan‍naṃ hi vattamānabhūtāpagataukāsakatabhūmiladdhavasaina anaikappabhaidaṃ| tattha sabbampi uppādajarābhaṅgasamaṅgisaṅkhātaṃ vattamānuppan‍naṃ nāma| ārammaṇarasaṃ anubhavitvā niruddhaṃ anubhūtāpagatasaṅkhātaṃ kusalākusalaṃ uppādādittayaṃ anuppatvā niruddhaṃ bhūtāpagatasaṅkhātaṃ saisasaṅkhatañ‍ca bhūtāpagatuppan‍naṃ nāma| ‘‘yānissa tāni pubbaikatāni kammānī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 3.248) aivamādinā nayaina vuttaṃ kammaṃ atītampi samānaṃ añ‍ñaṃ vipākaṃ paṭibāhitvā attanau vipākassaukāsaṃ katvā ṭhitattā tathā kataukāsañ‍ca vipākaṃ anuppan‍nampi samānaṃ aivaṃ katai aukāsai aikantaina uppaj‍janatau aukāsakatuppan‍naṃ nāma| tāsu tāsu bhūmīsu asamūhataṃ akusalaṃ bhūmiladdhuppan‍naṃ nāma|

834. aittha  ca bhūmiyā bhūmiladdhassa ca nānattaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| bhūmīti hi vipassanāya ārammaṇabhūtā taibhūmakā pañ‍cakkhandhā| bhūmiladdhaṃ nāma taisu khandhaisu uppattirahaṃ kilaisajātaṃ| tainahi sā bhūmi laddhā nāma hautīti tasmā bhūmiladdhanti vuc‍cati, sā ca khau na ārammaṇavasaina| ārammaṇavasaina  hi sabbaipi atītānāgatai pariñ‍ñātaipi ca khīṇāsavānaṃ khandhai ārabbha kilaisā uppaj‍janti mahākac‍cānauppalavaṇṇādīnaṃ khandhai ārabbha sauraiyyasaiṭṭhi nandamāṇavakādīnaṃ viya| yadi ca taṃ bhūmiladdhaṃ nāma siyā, tassa appahaiyyatau na kauci bhavamūlaṃ pajahaiyya| vatthuvasaina pana bhūmiladdhaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| yattha yattha hi vipassanāya apariñ‍ñātā khandhā uppaj‍janti, tattha tattha uppādatau pabhuti taisu vaṭṭamūlaṃ kilaisajātaṃ anusaiti| taṃ appahīnaṭṭhaina bhūmiladdhanti vaiditabbaṃ|

835. tattha ca yassa yaisu khandhaisu appahīnaṭṭhaina anusayitā kilaisā, tassa tai aiva khandhā taisaṃ kilaisānaṃ vatthu, na añ‍ñaisaṃ santakā khandhā| atītakkhandhaisu ca appahīnānusayitānaṃ kilaisānaṃ atītakkhandhāva vatthu, na itarai| aisa nayau anāgatādīsu| tathā kāmāvacarakkhandhaisu appahīnānusayitānaṃ kilaisānaṃ kāmāvacarakkhandhāva vatthu, na itarai| aisa nayau rūpārūpāvacaraisu| sautāpan‍nādīsu pana yassa yassa ariyapuggalassa khandhaisu taṃ taṃ vaṭṭamūlaṃ kilaisajātaṃ taina taina maggaina pahīnaṃ, tassa tassa tai tai khandhā pahīnānaṃ taisaṃ taisaṃ vaṭṭamūlakilaisānaṃ avatthutau bhūmīti saṅkhaṃ na labhanti| puthuj‍janassa sabbasauva vaṭṭamūlakilaisānaṃ appahīnattā yaṃkiñ‍ci kariyamānaṃ kammaṃ kusalaṃ akusalaṃ vā hauti| ic‍cassa kammakilaisapac‍cayā vaṭṭaṃ vaṭṭati| tassaitaṃ vaṭṭamūlaṃ rūpakkhandhaiyaiva, na vaidanākkhandhādīsu| viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaiyaiva vā, na rūpakkhandhādīsūti na vattabbaṃ| kasmā? avisaisaina pañ‍casupi khandhaisu anusayitattā|

836. kathaṃ? pathavīrasādi viya rukkhai| yathā hi mahārukkhai pathavītalaṃ adhiṭṭhāya pathavīrasañ‍ca āpaurasañ‍ca nissāya tappac‍cayā mūlakhandhasākhapasākhapal‍lavapalāsapupphaphalaihi vaḍḍhitvā nabhaṃ pūraitvā yāva kappāvasānā bījaparamparāya rukkhapavaiṇiṃ santānayamānai ṭhitai taṃ pathavīrasādi mūlaiyaiva, na khandhādīsu…pai॰… phalaiyaiva vā, na mūlādīsūti na vattabbaṃ| kasmā? avisaisaina sabbaisu mūlādīsu anugatattāti|

yathā  pana tassaiva rukkhassa pupphaphalādīsu nibbin‍nau kauci purisau catūsu disāsu maṇḍūkakaṇṭakaṃ nāma visakaṇṭakaṃ ākauṭaiyya, atha sau  rukkhau taina visasamphassaina phuṭṭhau pathavīrasaāpaurasānaṃ pariyādiṇṇattā appasavanadhammataṃ āgamma puna santānaṃ nibbattaituṃ na sak‍kuṇaiyya, aivamaiva khandhapavattiyaṃ nibbin‍nau kulaputtau tassa purisassa catūsu disāsu rukkhai visayaujanaṃ viya attanau santānai catumaggabhāvanaṃ ārabhati| athassa sau khandhasantānau taina catumaggavisasamphassaina sabbasau vaṭṭamūlakakilaisānaṃ pariyādiṇṇattā kiriyabhāvamattaupagatakāyakammādisabbakammappabhaidau hutvā āyatiṃ punabbhavānabhinibbattanadhammataṃ āgamma bhavantarasantānaṃ nibbattaituṃ na sak‍kauti| kaivalaṃ carimaviñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaina nirindhanau viya jātavaidau anupādānau parinibbāyati, aivaṃ bhūmiyā bhūmiladdhassa ca nānattaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

837. apica aparampi samudācāraārammaṇādhiggahitaavikkhambhitaasamūhatavasaina catubbidhaṃ uppan‍naṃ| tattha vattamānuppan‍namaiva samudācāruppan‍naṃ| cakkhādīnaṃ pana āpāthagatai ārammaṇai pubbabhāgai anuppaj‍jamānampi kilaisajātaṃ ārammaṇassa adhiggahitattā aiva aparabhāgai aikantaina uppattitau ārammaṇādhiggahituppan‍nanti vuc‍cati, kalyāṇigāmai piṇḍāya caratau mahātissatthairassa visabhāgarūpadassanaina uppan‍nakilaisajātaṃ viya| samathavipassanānaṃ añ‍ñataravasaina avikkhambhitaṃ kilaisajātaṃ cittasantatimanārūḷhampi uppattinivārakassa haitunau abhāvā avikkhambhituppan‍naṃ nāma| samathavipassanāvasaina pana vikkhambhitampi ariyamaggaina asamūhatattā uppattidhammataṃ anatītatāya asamūhatuppan‍nanti vuc‍cati, ākāsaina gacchantassa aṭṭhasamāpattilābhinau thairassa kusumitarukkhai upavanai pupphāni uc‍cinantassa madhuraina saraina gāyatau mātugāmassa gītasavanaina uppan‍nakilaisajātaṃ viya| tividhampi caitaṃ ārammaṇādhiggahitāvikkhambhitaasamūhatuppan‍naṃ bhūmiladdhainaiva saṅgahaṃ gacchatīti vaiditabbaṃ|

838. ic‍caitasmiṃ vuttappabhaidai uppan‍nai yadaitaṃ vattamānabhūtāpagataukāsakatasamudācārasaṅkhātaṃ catubbidhaṃ uppan‍naṃ, taṃ amaggavajjhattā kainacipi ñāṇaina pahātabbaṃ na hauti| yaṃ panaitaṃ bhūmiladdhārammaṇādhiggahitaavikkhambhitaasamūhatasaṅkhātaṃ uppan‍naṃ, tassa taṃ uppan‍nabhāvaṃ vināsayamānaṃ yasmā taṃ taṃ laukiyalaukuttarañāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati, tasmā taṃ sabbampi pahātabbaṃ hautīti| aivamaittha yai yaina pahātabbā dhammā, taisaṃ pahānañ‍ca jānitabbaṃ|

pariñ‍ñādikic‍cakathā

839.

 kic‍cāni  pariñ‍ñādīni, yāni vuttāni abhisamayakālai|

tāni ca yathāsabhāvaina, jānitabbāni sabbānīti||

sac‍cābhisamayakālaṃ hi aitaisu catūsu ñāṇaisu aikaikassa aikakkhaṇai pariñ‍ñā pahānaṃ sacchikiriyā bhāvanāti aitāni pariñ‍ñādīni cattāri kic‍cāni vuttāni, tāni yathāsabhāvaina jānitabbāni| vuttaṃ haitaṃ paurāṇaihi –

‘‘yathā padīpau apubbaṃ acarimaṃ aikakkhaṇai cattāri kic‍cāni karauti, vaṭṭiṃ jhāpaiti, andhakāraṃ vidhamati, ālaukaṃ parividaṃsaiti, sinaihaṃ pariyādiyati, aivamaiva maggañāṇaṃ apubbaṃ acarimaṃ aikakkhaṇai cattāri sac‍cāni abhisamaiti, dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñābhisamayaina abhisamaiti, samudayaṃ pahānābhisamayaina abhisamaiti, maggaṃ bhāvanābhisamayaina abhisamaiti, niraudhaṃ sacchikiriyābhisamayaina abhisamaiti| kiṃ vuttaṃ hauti? niraudhaṃ ārammaṇaṃ karitvā cattāripi sac‍cāni pāpuṇāti passati paṭivijjhatī’’ti|

vuttampi caitaṃ ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ passati, dukkhasamudayampi sau passati, dukkhaniraudhampi passati, dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadampi passatī’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1100) sabbaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

aparampi vuttaṃ ‘‘maggasamaṅgissa ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaipaitaṃ ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayaipaitaṃ ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhaipaitaṃ ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāyapaitaṃ ñāṇa’’nti (vibha॰ 794; paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.109)|

tattha yathā padīpau vaṭṭiṃ jhāpaiti, aivaṃ maggañāṇaṃ dukkhaṃ parijānāti| yathā andhakāraṃ vidhamati, aivaṃ samudayaṃ pajahati| yathā ālaukaṃ parividaṃsaiti, aivaṃ sahajātādipac‍cayatāya sammāsaṅkappādidhammasaṅkhātaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti| yathā sinaihaṃ pariyādiyati, aivaṃ kilaisapariyādānaṃ niraudhaṃ sacchikarautīti aivaṃ upamāsaṃsandanaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

840. aparau  nayau – yathā sūriyau udayantau apubbaṃ acarimaṃ saha pātubhāvā cattāri kic‍cāni karauti, rūpagatāni aubhāsaiti, andhakāraṃ  vidhamati, ālaukaṃ dassaiti, sītaṃ paṭippassambhaiti, aivamaiva maggañāṇaṃ…pai॰… niraudhaṃ sacchikiriyābhisamayaina abhisamaiti| idhāpi yathā sūriyau rūpagatāni aubhāsaiti, aivaṃ maggañāṇaṃ dukkhaṃ parijānāti| yathā andhakāraṃ vidhamati, aivaṃ samudayaṃ pajahati| yathā ālaukaṃ dassaiti, aivaṃ sahajātādipac‍cayatāya maggaṃ bhāvaiti| yathā sītaṃ paṭippassambhaiti, aivaṃ kilaisapaṭippassaddhiṃ niraudhaṃ sacchikarautīti aivaṃ upamāsaṃsandanaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

841. aparau nayau – yathā nāvā apubbaṃ acarimaṃ aikakkhaṇai cattāri kic‍cāni karauti, aurimatīraṃ pajahati, sautaṃ chindati, bhaṇḍaṃ vahati, pārimatīraṃ appaiti, aivamaiva maggañāṇaṃ…pai॰… niraudhaṃ sacchikiriyābhisamayaina abhisamaiti| aitthāpi yathā nāvā aurimatīraṃ pajahati, aivaṃ maggañāṇaṃ dukkhaṃ parijānāti| yathā sautaṃ chindati, aivaṃ samudayaṃ pajahati| yathā bhaṇḍaṃ vahati, aivaṃ sahajātādipac‍cayatāya maggaṃ bhāvaiti| yathā pārimatīraṃ appaiti, aivaṃ pārimatīrabhūtaṃ niraudhaṃ sacchikarautīti aivaṃ upamāsaṃsandanaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

842. aivaṃ sac‍cābhisamayakālasmiṃ aikakkhaṇai catun‍naṃ kic‍cānaṃ vasaina pavattañāṇassa panassa sauḷasahākāraihi tathaṭṭhaina cattāri kic‍cāni aikapaṭivaidhāni haunti| yathāha –

‘‘kathaṃ tathaṭṭhaina cattāri kic‍cāni aikapaṭivaidhāni? sauḷasahi ākāraihi tathaṭṭhaina cattāri kic‍cāni aikapaṭivaidhāni| dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhau, saṅkhataṭṭhau, santāpaṭṭhau, vipariṇāmaṭṭhau, tathaṭṭhau| samudayassa āyūhanaṭṭhau, nidānaṭṭhau, saṃyaugaṭṭhau, palibaudhaṭṭhau, tathaṭṭhau| niraudhassa nissaraṇaṭṭhau, vivaikaṭṭhau, asaṅkhataṭṭhau, amataṭṭhau, tathaṭṭhau| maggassa niyyānaṭṭhau, haituṭṭhau, dassanaṭṭhau, adhipataiyyaṭṭhau, tathaṭṭhau| imaihi sauḷasahi ākāraihi tathaṭṭhaina cattāri sac‍cāni aikasaṅgahitāni| yaṃ aikasaṅgahitaṃ, taṃ aikattaṃ| yaṃ aikattaṃ, taṃ aikaina ñāṇaina paṭivijjhatīti cattāri sac‍cāni aikapaṭivaidhānī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.11)|

843. tattha  siyā yadā dukkhādīnaṃ añ‍ñaipi raugagaṇḍādayau atthā atthi, atha kasmā cattārauyaiva vuttāti| aittha vadāma, añ‍ñasac‍cadassanavasaina āvibhāvatau| ‘‘tattha katamaṃ  dukkhai ñāṇaṃ? dukkhaṃ ārabbha yā uppaj‍jati pañ‍ñā pajānanā’’tiādinā (vibha॰ 794; paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.109) hi nayaina aikaikasac‍cārammaṇavasaināpi sac‍cañāṇaṃ vuttaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ passati, samudayampi sau passatī’’tiādinā (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1100) nayaina aikaṃ sac‍caṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā saisaisupi kic‍canipphattivasaināpi vuttaṃ|

tattha yadā aikaikaṃ sac‍caṃ ārammaṇaṃ karauti, tadā samudayadassanaina tāva sabhāvatau pīḷanalakkhaṇassāpi dukkhassa, yasmā taṃ āyūhanalakkhaṇaina samudayaina āyūhitaṃ saṅkhataṃ rāsikataṃ, tasmāssa sau saṅkhataṭṭhau āvibhavati| yasmā pana maggau kilaisasantāpaharau susītalau, tasmāssa maggassa dassanaina santāpaṭṭhau āvibhavati āyasmatau nandassa accharādassanaina sundariyā anabhirūpabhāvau viya| avipariṇāmadhammassa pana niraudhassa dassanainassa vipariṇāmaṭṭhau āvibhavatīti vattabbamaivaittha natthi|

tathā sabhāvatau āyūhanalakkhaṇassāpi samudayassa, dukkhadassanaina nidānaṭṭhau āvibhavati asappāyabhaujanatau uppan‍nabyādhidassanaina bhaujanassa byādhinidānabhāvau viya| visaṃyaugabhūtassa niraudhassa dassanaina saṃyaugaṭṭhau| niyyānabhūtassa ca maggassa dassanaina palibaudhaṭṭhauti|

tathā nissaraṇalakkhaṇassāpi niraudhassa, avivaikabhūtassa samudayassa dassanaina vivaikaṭṭhau āvibhavati| maggadassanaina asaṅkhataṭṭhau, iminā hi anamataggasaṃsārai maggau nadiṭṭhapubbau, saupi ca sappac‍cayattā saṅkhatauyaivāti appac‍cayadhammassa asaṅkhatabhāvau ativiya pākaṭau hauti| dukkhadassanaina panassa amataṭṭhau āvibhavati, dukkhaṃ hi visaṃ, amataṃ nibbānanti|

tathā niyyānalakkhaṇassāpi maggassa, samudayadassanaina ‘‘nāyaṃ haitu nibbānassa pattiyā, ayaṃ haitū’’ti haituṭṭhau āvibhavati| niraudhadassanaina dassanaṭṭhau, paramasukhumāni rūpāni passatau ‘‘vippasan‍naṃ vata mai  cakkhū’’nti cakkhussa vippasan‍nabhāvau viya| dukkhadassanaina adhipataiyyaṭṭhau, anaikaraugāturakapaṇajanadassanaina issarajanassa uḷārabhāvau viyāti aivamaittha salakkhaṇavasaina aikaikassa, añ‍ñasac‍cadassanavasaina ca itaraisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ tiṇṇaṃ āvibhāvatau aikaikassa cattārau cattārau atthā vuttā| maggakkhaṇai pana sabbai caitai atthā aikainaiva dukkhādīsu  catukic‍caina ñāṇaina paṭivaidhaṃ gacchantīti| yai pana nānābhisamayaṃ icchanti, taisaṃ uttaraṃ abhidhammai kathāvatthusmiṃ vuttamaiva|

pariñ‍ñādippabhaidakathā

844. idāni yāni tāni pariñ‍ñādīni cattāri kic‍cāni vuttāni, taisu –

tividhā hauti pariñ‍ñā, tathā pahānampi sacchikiriyāpi|

dvai bhāvanā abhimatā, vinicchayau tattha ñātabbau||

845. tividhā hauti pariñ‍ñāti ñātapariñ‍ñā tīraṇapariñ‍ñā pahānapariñ‍ñāti aivaṃ pariñ‍ñā tividhā hauti| tattha ‘‘abhiñ‍ñāpañ‍ñā ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇa’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ mātikā 1.20) aivaṃ uddisitvā ‘‘yai yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñātā haunti, tai tai dhammā ñātā hauntī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.75) aivaṃ saṅkhaipatau, ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ? cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñaiyya’’ntiādinā (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.2) nayaina vitthāratau vuttā ñātapariñ‍ñā nāma| tassā sappac‍cayanāmarūpābhijānanā āvaiṇikā bhūmi|

846. ‘‘pariñ‍ñāpañ‍ñā tīraṇaṭṭhaina ñāṇa’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ mātikā 1.21) aivaṃ uddisitvā pana ‘‘yai yai dhammā pariñ‍ñātā haunti, tai tai dhammā tīritā hauntī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.75) aivaṃ saṅkhaipatau, ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ? cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñaiyya’’ntiādinā (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.21) nayaina vitthāratau vuttā tīraṇapariñ‍ñā nāma| tassā kalāpasammasanatau paṭṭhāya anic‍caṃ dukkhamanattāti tīraṇavasaina pavattamānāya yāva anulaumā āvaiṇikā bhūmi|

847. ‘‘pahānapañ‍ñā paric‍cāgaṭṭhaina ñāṇa’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ mātikā 1.22) aivaṃ pana uddisitvā ‘‘yai yai dhammā pahīnā haunti, tai tai dhammā paric‍cattā hauntī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.75) aivaṃ vitthāratau  vuttā ‘‘anic‍cānupassanāya nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pajahatī’’tiādinayappavattā  pahānapariñ‍ñā| tassā bhaṅgānupassanatau paṭṭhāya yāva maggañāṇā bhūmi, ayaṃ idha adhippaitā|

yasmā vā ñātatīraṇapariñ‍ñāyaupi tadatthāyaiva, yasmā ca yai dhammai pajahati, tai niyamatau ñātā caiva tīritā ca haunti, tasmā pariñ‍ñāttayampi iminā pariyāyaina maggañāṇassa kic‍canti vaiditabbaṃ|

848. tathā pahānampīti pahānampi hi vikkhambhanappahānaṃ tadaṅgappahānaṃ samucchaidappahānanti pariñ‍ñā viya tividhamaiva hauti| tattha yaṃ sasaivālai udakai pakkhittaina ghaṭaina saivālassa viya taina taina laukiyasamādhinā nīvaraṇādīnaṃ pac‍canīkadhammānaṃ vikkhambhanaṃ, idaṃ vikkhambhanappahānaṃ nāma| pāḷiyaṃ pana ‘‘vikkhambhanappahānañ‍ca nīvaraṇānaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ bhāvayatau’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.24) nīvaraṇānañ‍ñaiva vikkhambhanaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ pākaṭattā vuttanti vaiditabbaṃ| nīvaraṇāni hi jhānassa pubbabhāgaipi pacchābhāgaipi na sahasā cittaṃ ajjhauttharanti, vitak‍kādayau appitakkhaṇaiyaiva| tasmā nīvaraṇānaṃ vikkhambhanaṃ pākaṭaṃ|

849. yaṃ pana rattibhāgai samuj‍jalitaina padīpaina andhakārassa viya taina taina vipassanāya avayavabhūtaina ñāṇaṅgaina paṭipakkhavasainaiva tassa tassa pahātabbadhammassa pahānaṃ, idaṃ tadaṅgappahānaṃ nāma| saiyyathidaṃ – nāmarūpaparicchaidaina tāva sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā| pac‍cayapariggahaina ahaituvisamahaitudiṭṭhiyā caiva kaṅkhāmalassa ca| kalāpasammasanaina ‘‘ahaṃ mamā’’ti samūhagāhassa| maggāmaggavavatthānaina amaggai maggasañ‍ñāya| udayadassanaina ucchaidadiṭṭhiyā| vayadassanaina sassatadiṭṭhiyā| bhayatupaṭṭhānaina sabhayai abhayasañ‍ñāya| ādīnavadassanaina assādasañ‍ñāya| nibbidānupassanaina abhiratisañ‍ñāya| muñ‍citukamyatāya amuñ‍citukāmabhāvassa| paṭisaṅkhānaina appaṭisaṅkhānassa| upaikkhāya anupaikkhanassa| anulaumaina sac‍capaṭilaumagāhassa pahānaṃ|

yaṃ vā pana aṭṭhārasasu mahāvipassanāsu anic‍cānupassanāya nic‍casañ‍ñāya| dukkhānupassanāya sukhasañ‍ñāya| anattānupassanāya attasañ‍ñāya| nibbidānupassanāya nandiyā| virāgānupassanāya rāgassa| niraudhānupassanāya  samudayassa| paṭinissaggānupassanāya  ādānassa| khayānupassanāya ghanasañ‍ñāya| vayānupassanāya āyūhanassa| vipariṇāmānupassanāya dhuvasañ‍ñāya| animittānupassanāya nimittassa| appaṇihitānupassanāya paṇidhiyā| suñ‍ñatānupassanāya abhinivaisassa| adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya sārādānābhinivaisassa| yathābhūtañāṇadassanaina sammauhābhinivaisassa| ādīnavānupassanāya ālayābhinivaisassa| paṭisaṅkhānupassanāya appaṭisaṅkhāya| vivaṭṭānupassanāya saṃyaugābhinivaisassa pahānaṃ| idampi tadaṅgappahānamaiva|

850. tattha yathā anic‍cānupassanādīhi sattahi nic‍casañ‍ñādīnaṃ pahānaṃ hauti, taṃ bhaṅgānupassanai vuttamaiva|

khayānupassanāti pana ghanavinibbhaugaṃ katvā anic‍caṃ khayaṭṭhaināti aivaṃ khayaṃ passatau ñāṇaṃ| taina ghanasañ‍ñāya pahānaṃ hauti|

vayānupassanāti –

ārammaṇānvayaina, ubhau aikavavatthānā|

niraudhai adhimuttatā, vayalakkhaṇavipassanāti|| –

aivaṃ vuttā pac‍cakkhatau caiva anvayatau ca saṅkhārānaṃ bhaṅgaṃ disvā tasmiñ‍ñaiva bhaṅgasaṅkhātai niraudhai adhimuttatā, tāya āyūhanassa pahānaṃ hauti| yaisaṃ hi atthāya āyūhaiyya, ‘‘tai aivaṃ vayadhammā’’ti vipassatau āyūhanai cittaṃ na namati|

vipariṇāmānupassanāti rūpasattakādivasaina taṃ taṃ paricchaidaṃ atik‍kamma añ‍ñathāpavattidassanaṃ| uppan‍nassa vā jarāya caiva maraṇaina ca dvīhākāraihi vipariṇāmadassanaṃ, tāya dhuvasañ‍ñāya pahānaṃ hauti|

animittānupassanāti anic‍cānupassanāva, tāya nic‍canimittassa pahānaṃ hauti|

appaṇihitānupassanāti dukkhānupassanāva, tāya sukhapaṇidhisukhapatthanāpahānaṃ hauti|

suñ‍ñatānupassanāti anattānupassanāva, tāya ‘‘atthi attā’’ti abhinivaisassa pahānaṃ hauti|

adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāti  –

‘‘ārammaṇañ‍ca paṭisaṅkhā, bhaṅgañ‍ca anupassati|

suñ‍ñatau ca upaṭṭhānaṃ, adhipañ‍ñā vipassanā’’ti|| –

aivaṃ vuttā rūpādiārammaṇaṃ jānitvā tassa ca ārammaṇassa tadārammaṇassa ca cittassa bhaṅgaṃ disvā ‘‘saṅkhārāva bhij‍janti, saṅkhārānaṃ maraṇaṃ, na añ‍ñau kauci atthī’’ti bhaṅgavasaina suñ‍ñataṃ gahaitvā pavattā vipassanā| sā adhipañ‍ñā ca dhammaisu ca vipassanāti katvā adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāti vuc‍cati, tāya nic‍casārābhāvassa ca attasārābhāvassa ca suṭṭhu diṭṭhattā sārādānābhinivaisassa pahānaṃ hauti|

yathābhūtañāṇadassananti sappac‍cayanāmarūpapariggahau, taina ‘‘ahausiṃ nu khau ahaṃ atītamaddhāna’’ntiādivasaina caiva, ‘‘issaratau laukau sambhautī’’tiādivasaina ca pavattassa sammauhābhinivaisassa pahānaṃ hauti|

ādīnavānupassanāti bhayatupaṭṭhānavasaina uppan‍naṃ sabbabhavādīsu ādīnavadassanañāṇaṃ, taina ‘‘kiñ‍ci al‍līyitabbaṃ na dissatī’’ti ālayābhinivaisassa pahānaṃ hauti|

paṭisaṅkhānupassanāti muñ‍canassa upāyakaraṇaṃ paṭisaṅkhāñāṇaṃ, taina appaṭisaṅkhāya pahānaṃ hauti|

vivaṭṭānupassanāti saṅkhārupaikkhā caiva anulaumañ‍ca| tadā hissa cittaṃ īsakapauṇai padumapalāsai udakabindu viya sabbasmā saṅkhāragatā patilīyati, patikuṭati, pativattatīti vuttaṃ| tasmā tāya saṃyaugābhinivaisassa pahānaṃ hauti, kāmasaṃyaugādikassa kilaisābhinivaisassa kilaisappavattiyā pahānaṃ hautīti atthau| aivaṃ vitthāratau tadaṅgappahānaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| pāḷiyaṃ pana ‘‘tadaṅgappahānañ‍ca diṭṭhigatānaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ samādhiṃ bhāvayatau’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.24) saṅkhaipainaiva vuttaṃ|

851. yaṃ  pana asanivicak‍kābhihatassa rukkhassa viya ariyamaggañāṇaina saṃyaujanādīnaṃ dhammānaṃ yathā na puna pavatti, aivaṃ pahānaṃ, idaṃ samucchaidappahānaṃ nāma| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ ‘‘samucchaidappahānañ‍ca laukuttaraṃ khayagāmimaggaṃ bhāvayatau’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.24)| iti imaisu tīsu pahānaisu samucchaidappahānamaiva idha adhippaitaṃ| yasmā pana tassa yauginau pubbabhāgai vikkhambhanatadaṅgappahānānipi tadatthānaiva, tasmā pahānattayampi iminā pariyāyaina maggañāṇassa kic‍canti vaiditabbaṃ| paṭirājānaṃ vadhitvā raj‍jaṃ pattaina hi yampi tatau pubbai kataṃ, sabbaṃ ‘‘idañ‍cidañ‍ca rañ‍ñā kata’’ntiyaiva vuc‍cati|

852. sacchikiriyāpīti laukiyasacchikiriyā laukuttarasacchikiriyāti dvaidhā bhin‍nāpi laukuttarāya dassanabhāvanāvasaina bhaidatau tividhā hauti| tattha ‘‘paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhīmhi, vasīmhi, paṭhamajjhānaṃ sacchikataṃ mayā’’tiādinā (pārā॰ 203-204) nayaina āgatā paṭhamajjhānādīnaṃ phassanā laukiyasacchikiriyā nāma| phassanāti adhigantvā ‘‘idaṃ mayā adhigata’’nti pac‍cakkhatau ñāṇaphassaina phusanā| imamaiva hi atthaṃ sandhāya ‘‘sacchikiriyā pañ‍ñā phassanaṭṭhai ñāṇa’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ mātikā 1.24) uddisitvā ‘‘yai yai dhammā sacchikatā haunti, tai tai dhammā phassitā hauntī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.75) sacchikiriyaniddaisau vuttau|

apica attanau santānai anuppādaitvāpi yai dhammā kaivalaṃ aparappac‍cayaina ñāṇaina ñātā, tai sacchikatā haunti| tainaiva hi ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sacchikātabbaṃ| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ sacchikātabbaṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, sacchikātabba’’ntiādi (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.29) vuttaṃ|

aparampi vuttaṃ ‘‘rūpaṃ passantau sacchikarauti| vaidanaṃ…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ passantau sacchikarauti| cakkhuṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ…pai॰… amataugadhaṃ nibbānaṃ passantau sacchikarautīti| yai yai dhammā sacchikatā haunti, tai tai dhammā phassitā hauntī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.29)|

paṭhamamaggakkhaṇai pana nibbānadassanaṃ dassanasacchikiriyā| saisamaggakkhaṇaisu bhāvanāsacchikiriyāti| sā duvidhāpi idha adhippaitā| tasmā dassanabhāvanāvasaina nibbānassa sacchikiriyā imassa ñāṇassa kic‍canti vaiditabbaṃ|

853. dvai  bhāvanā abhimatāti bhāvanā pana laukiyabhāvanā laukuttarabhāvanāti dvaiyaiva abhimatā| tattha laukiyānaṃ sīlasamādhipañ‍ñānaṃ uppādanaṃ, tāhi ca santānavāsanaṃ laukiyabhāvanā| laukuttarānaṃ uppādanaṃ, tāhi ca santānavāsanaṃ laukuttarabhāvanā| tāsu idha laukuttarā adhippaitā| laukuttarāni hi sīlādīni catubbidhampaitaṃ ñāṇaṃ uppādaiti| taisaṃ sahajātapac‍cayāditāya taihi ca santānaṃ vāsaitīti laukuttarabhāvanāvassa kic‍canti|

aivaṃ –

kic‍cāni pariñ‍ñādīni, yāni vuttāni abhisamayakālai|

tāni ca yathāsabhāvaina, jānitabbāni sabbānīti||

aittāvatā ca –

‘‘sīlai patiṭṭhāya narau sapañ‍ñau, cittaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvaya’’nti|| –

aivaṃ sarūpainaiva ābhatāya pañ‍ñābhāvanāya vidhānadassanatthaṃ yaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘mūlabhūtā dvai visuddhiyau sampādaitvā sarīrabhūtā pañ‍ca visuddhiyau sampādaintaina bhāvaitabbā’’ti, taṃ vitthāritaṃ hauti| kathaṃ bhāvaitabbāti ayañ‍ca pañhau vissaj‍jitauti|

iti sādhujanapāmauj‍jatthāya katai visuddhimaggai

pañ‍ñābhāvanādhikārai

ñāṇadassanavisuddhiniddaisau nāma

bāvīsatimau paricchaidau|

23. pañ‍ñābhāvanānisaṃsaniddaisau

ānisaṃsapakāsanā

854. yaṃ  pana vuttaṃ ‘‘pañ‍ñābhāvanāya kau ānisaṃsau’’ti, tattha vadāma| ayañhi pañ‍ñābhāvanā nāma anaikasatānisaṃsā| tassā dīghaināpi addhunā na sukaraṃ vitthāratau ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaituṃ| saṅkhaipatau panassā nānākilaisaviddhaṃsanaṃ, ariyaphalarasānubhavanaṃ, niraudhasamāpattisamāpaj‍janasamatthatā, āhunaiyyabhāvādisiddhīti ayamānisaṃsau vaiditabbau|

nānākilaisaviddhaṃsanakathā

855. tattha yaṃ nāmarūpaparicchaidatau paṭṭhāya sak‍kāyadiṭṭhādīnaṃ vasaina nānākilaisaviddhaṃsanaṃ vuttaṃ, ayaṃ laukikāya pañ‍ñābhāvanāya ānisaṃsau| yaṃ ariyamaggakkhaṇai saṃyaujanādīnaṃ vasaina nānākilaisaviddhaṃsanaṃ vuttaṃ, ayaṃ laukuttarāya pañ‍ñābhāvanāya ānisaṃsauti vaiditabbau|

bhīmavaigānupatitā, asanīva siluc‍cayai|

vāyuvaigasamuṭṭhitau, arañ‍ñamiva pāvakau||

andhakāraṃ viya ravi, sataijuj‍jalamaṇḍalau|

dīgharattānupatitaṃ, sabbānatthavidhāyakaṃ||

kilaisajālaṃ pañ‍ñā hi, viddhaṃsayati bhāvitā|

sandiṭṭhikamatau jañ‍ñā, ānisaṃsamimaṃ idha||

phalasamāpattikathā

856. ariyaphalarasānubhavananti  na kaivalañ‍ca kilaisaviddhaṃsanañ‍ñaiva, ariyaphalarasānubhavanampi pañ‍ñābhāvanāya ānisaṃsau| ariyaphalanti hi sautāpattiphalādi sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ vuc‍cati| tassa dvīhākāraihi rasānubhavanaṃ hauti| maggavīthiyañ‍ca phalasamāpattivasaina ca pavattiyaṃ| tatrāssa maggavīthiyaṃ pavatti dassitāyaiva|

857. apica  yai ‘‘saṃyaujanappahānamattamaiva phalaṃ nāma, na kauci añ‍ñau dhammau atthī’’ti vadanti, taisaṃ anunayatthaṃ idaṃ suttampi dassaitabbaṃ – ‘‘kathaṃ payaugapaṭippassaddhipañ‍ñā phalai ñāṇaṃ? sautāpattimaggakkhaṇai dassanaṭṭhaina sammādiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhiyā vuṭṭhāti, tadanuvattakakilaisaihi ca khandhaihi ca vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi vuṭṭhāti| tampayaugapaṭippassaddhattā uppaj‍jati sammādiṭṭhi, maggassaitaṃ phala’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.63) vitthāraitabbaṃ|

‘‘cattārau maggā apariyāpan‍nā, cattāri ca sāmañ‍ñaphalāni, imai dhammā appamāṇārammaṇā’’ (dha॰ sa॰ 1422)| ‘‘mahaggatau dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau’’ti (paṭṭhā॰ 2.12.62) aivamādīnipi caittha sādhakāni|

858. phalasamāpattiyaṃ pavattidassanatthaṃ panassa idaṃ pañhākammaṃ – kā phalasamāpatti, kai taṃ samāpaj‍janti, kai na samāpaj‍janti, kasmā samāpaj‍janti, kathañ‍cassā samāpaj‍janaṃ hauti, kathaṃ ṭhānaṃ, kathaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ, kiṃ phalassa anantaraṃ, kassa ca phalaṃ anantaranti?

859. tattha kā phalasamāpattīti yā ariyaphalassa niraudhai appanā|

860. kai taṃ samāpaj‍janti, kai na samāpaj‍jantīti sabbaipi puthuj‍janā na samāpaj‍janti| kasmā? anadhigatattā| ariyā pana sabbaipi samāpaj‍janti| kasmā? adhigatattā| uparimā pana haiṭṭhimaṃ na samāpaj‍janti, puggalantarabhāvupagamanaina paṭippassaddhattā| haiṭṭhimā ca uparimaṃ, anadhigatattā| attanau attanauyaiva pana phalaṃ samāpaj‍jantīti idamaittha san‍niṭṭhānaṃ|

kaici pana ‘‘sautāpan‍nasakadāgāminaupi na samāpaj‍janti| uparimā dvaiyaiva samāpaj‍jantī’’ti  vadanti| idañ‍ca taisaṃ kāraṇaṃ, aitai hi samādhismiṃ paripūrakārinauti| taṃ puthuj‍janassāpi attanā paṭiladdhalaukiyasamādhisamāpaj‍janatau akāraṇamaiva| kiñ‍caittha kāraṇākāraṇacintāya| nanu pāḷiyaṃyaiva vuttaṃ – ‘‘katamai dasa gautrabhudhammā vipassanāvasaina uppaj‍janti? sautāpattimaggapaṭilābhatthāya uppādaṃ pavattaṃ…pai॰… upāyāsaṃ bahiddhā saṅkhāranimittaṃ abhibhuyyatīti gautrabhu| sautāpattiphalasamāpattatthāya sakadāgāmimaggaṃ  …pai॰… arahattaphalasamāpattatthāya… suñ‍ñatavihārasamāpattatthāya… animittavihārasamāpattatthāya uppādaṃ…pai॰… bahiddhā saṅkhāranimittaṃ abhibhuyyatīti gautrabhū’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.60)| tasmā sabbaipi ariyā attanau attanau phalaṃ samāpaj‍jantīti niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ|

861. kasmā samāpaj‍jantīti diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāratthaṃ| yathā hi rājā raj‍jasukhaṃ, daivatā dibbasukhaṃ anubhavanti, aivaṃ ariyā ‘‘ariyaṃ laukuttarasukhaṃ anubhavissāmā’’ti addhānapparicchaidaṃ katvā icchiticchitakkhaṇai phalasamāpattiṃ samāpaj‍janti|

862. kathañ‍cassā samāpaj‍janaṃ hauti, kathaṃ ṭhānaṃ, kathaṃ vuṭṭhānanti dvīhi tāva ākāraihi assā samāpaj‍janaṃ hauti – nibbānatau añ‍ñassa ārammaṇassa amanasikārā nibbānassa ca manasikārā| yathāha – ‘‘dvai khau, āvusau, pac‍cayā animittāya caitauvimuttiyā samāpattiyā sabbanimittānañ‍ca amanasikārau, animittāya ca dhātuyā manasikārau’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.458)|

863. ayampanaittha samāpaj‍janak‍kamau| phalasamāpattatthikaina hi ariyasāvakaina rahaugataina paṭisal‍līnaina udayabbayādivasaina saṅkhārā vipassitabbā| tassa pavattānupubbavipassanassa saṅkhārārammaṇagautrabhuñāṇānantarā phalasamāpattivasaina niraudhai cittaṃ appaiti| phalasamāpattinin‍natāya caittha saikkhassāpi phalamaiva uppaj‍jati, na maggau|

yai pana vadanti ‘‘sautāpan‍nau ‘phalasamāpattiṃ samāpaj‍jissāmī’ti vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā sakadāgāmī hauti| sakadāgāmī ca anāgāmī’’ti, tai vattabbā ‘‘aivaṃ sati anāgāmī arahā bhavissati, arahā pac‍caikabuddhau, pac‍caikabuddhau ca buddhau| tasmā na kiñ‍ci aitaṃ, pāḷivasainaiva ca paṭikkhitta’’ntipi na gahaitabbaṃ| idamaiva pana gahaitabbaṃ – saikkhassāpi phalamaiva  uppaj‍jati, na maggau| phalañ‍cassa sacai anaina paṭhamajjhānikau maggau adhigatau hauti| paṭhamajjhānikamaiva uppaj‍jati| sacai dutiyādīsu añ‍ñatarajjhānikau, dutiyādīsu añ‍ñatarajjhānikamaivāti| aivaṃ tāvassā samāpaj‍janaṃ hauti|

864. ‘‘tayau  khau, āvusau, pac‍cayā animittāya caitauvimuttiyā ṭhitiyā sabbanimittānañ‍ca amanasikārau, animittāya ca dhātuyā manasikārau, pubbai ca abhisaṅkhārau’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.458) vacanatau panassā tīhākāraihi ṭhānaṃ hauti| tattha pubbai ca abhisaṅkhārauti samāpattitau pubbai kālaparicchaidau| ‘‘asukasmiṃ nāma kālai vuṭṭhahissāmī’’ti paricchin‍nattā hissā yāva sau kālau nāgacchati, tāva ṭhānaṃ hauti| aivamassā ṭhānaṃ hautīti|

865. ‘‘dvai khau, āvusau, pac‍cayā animittāya caitauvimuttiyā vuṭṭhānāya sabbanimittānañ‍ca manasikārau, animittāya ca dhātuyā amanasikārau’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.458) vacanatau panassā dvīhākāraihi vuṭṭhānaṃ hauti| tattha sabbanimittānanti rūpanimittavaidanāsañ‍ñāsaṅkhāraviñ‍ñāṇanimittānaṃ| kāmañ‍ca na sabbānaivaitāni aikatau manasikarauti sabbasaṅgāhikavasaina panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| tasmā yaṃ bhavaṅgassa ārammaṇaṃ hauti, taṃ manasikarautau phalasamāpattivuṭṭhānaṃ hautīti aivamassā vuṭṭhānaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

866. kiṃ phalassa anantaraṃ, kassa ca phalaṃ anantaranti phalassa tāva phalamaiva vā anantaraṃ hauti, bhavaṅgaṃ vā| phalaṃ pana atthi maggānantaraṃ, atthi phalānantaraṃ, atthi gautrabhuanantaraṃ, atthi naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanānantaraṃ| tattha maggavīthiyaṃ maggānantaraṃ, purimassa purimassa pacchimaṃ pacchimaṃ phalānantaraṃ| phalasamāpattīsu purimaṃ purimaṃ gautrabhuanantaraṃ| gautrabhūti caittha anulaumaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ paṭṭhānai – ‘‘arahatau anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saikkhānaṃ anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau’’ti (paṭṭhā॰ 1.1.417)| yaina phalaina niraudhā vuṭṭhānaṃ hauti, taṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanānantaranti| tattha ṭhapaitvā maggavīthiyaṃ uppan‍naṃ phalaṃ avasaisaṃ sabbaṃ phalasamāpattivasaina pavattaṃ nāma| aivamaitaṃ maggavīthiyaṃ phalasamāpattiyaṃ vā uppaj‍janavasaina,

paṭippassaddhadarathaṃ, amatārammaṇaṃ subhaṃ|

vantalaukāmisaṃ santaṃ, sāmañ‍ñaphalamuttamaṃ||

aujavantaina sucinā, sukhaina abhisanditaṃ|

yaina sātātisātaina, amataina madhuṃ viya||

taṃ  sukhaṃ tassa ariyassa, rasabhūtamanuttaraṃ|

phalassa pañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaitvā, yasmā vindati paṇḍitau||

tasmā ariyaphalassaitaṃ, rasānubhavanaṃ idha|

vipassanābhāvanāya, ānisaṃsauti vuc‍cati||

niraudhasamāpattikathā

867. niraudhasamāpattisamāpaj‍janasamatthatāti na kaivalañ‍ca ariyaphalarasānubhavanaṃyaiva, ayaṃ pana niraudhasamāpattiyā samāpaj‍janasamatthatāpi imissā pañ‍ñābhāvanāya ānisaṃsauti vaiditabbau|

tatridaṃ niraudhasamāpattiyā vibhāvanatthaṃ pañhākammaṃ – kā niraudhasamāpatti, kai taṃ samāpaj‍janti, kai na samāpaj‍janti, kattha samāpaj‍janti, kasmā samāpaj‍janti, kathañ‍cassā samāpaj‍janaṃ hauti, kathaṃ ṭhānaṃ, kathaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ, vuṭṭhitassa kiṃnin‍naṃ cittaṃ hauti, matassa ca samāpan‍nassa ca kau visaisau, niraudhasamāpatti kiṃ saṅkhatā asaṅkhatā laukiyā laukuttarā nipphan‍nā anipphan‍nāti?

868. tattha kā niraudhasamāpattīti yā anupubbaniraudhavasaina cittacaitasikānaṃ dhammānaṃ appavatti| kai taṃ samāpaj‍janti, kai na samāpaj‍jantīti sabbaipi puthuj‍janā, sautāpan‍nā, sakadāgāminau, sukkhavipassakā ca anāgāminau, arahantau na samāpaj‍janti| aṭṭhasamāpattilābhinau pana anāgāminau, khīṇāsavā ca samāpaj‍janti| ‘‘dvīhi balaihi saman‍nāgatattā, tayau ca saṅkhārānaṃ paṭippassaddhiyā, sauḷasahi ñāṇacariyāhi, navahi samādhicariyāhi vasībhāvatā pañ‍ñā niraudhasamāpattiyā ñāṇa’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ mātikā 1.34) hi vuttaṃ| ayañ‍ca sampadā ṭhapaitvā aṭṭhasamāpattilābhinau anāgāmikhīṇāsavai añ‍ñaisaṃ natthi| tasmā taiyaiva samāpaj‍janti, na añ‍ñai|

869. katamāni panaittha dvai balāni…pai॰… katamā vasībhāvatāti? na aittha kiñ‍ci amhaihi vattabbaṃ atthi| sabbamidaṃ aitassa uddaisassa niddaisai vuttamaiva| yathāha –

‘‘dvīhi  balaihīti dvai balāni samathabalaṃ vipassanābalaṃ| katamaṃ samathabalaṃ? naikkhammavasaina cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samathabalaṃ| abyāpādavasaina… ālaukasañ‍ñāvasaina… avikkhaipavasaina…pai॰… paṭinissaggānupassiassāsavasaina… paṭinissaggānupassipassāsavasaina cittassa aikaggatā avikkhaipau samathabalanti| kainaṭṭhaina samathabalaṃ? paṭhamajjhānaina nīvaraṇai na kampatīti samathabalaṃ| dutiyajjhānaina vitak‍kavicārai…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāya na kampatīti samathabalaṃ| uddhac‍cai ca uddhac‍casahagatakilaisai ca khandhai ca na kampati na calati na vaidhatīti samathabalaṃ| idaṃ samathabalaṃ|

‘‘katamaṃ vipassanābalaṃ? anic‍cānupassanā vipassanābalaṃ| dukkhānupassanā… anattānupassanā… nibbidānupassanā… virāgānupassanā… niraudhānupassanā… paṭinissaggānupassanā vipassanābalaṃ| rūpai anic‍cānupassanā…pai॰… rūpai paṭinissaggānupassanā vipassanābalaṃ| vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai… cakkhusmiṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇai anic‍cānupassanā| jarāmaraṇai paṭinissaggānupassanā vipassanābalanti| kainaṭṭhaina vipassanābalaṃ? anic‍cānupassanāya nic‍casañ‍ñāya na kampatīti vipassanābalaṃ| dukkhānupassanāya sukhasañ‍ñāya na kampatīti… anattānupassanāya attasañ‍ñāya na kampatīti… nibbidānupassanāya nandiyā na kampatīti… virāgānupassanāya rāgai na kampatīti… niraudhānupassanāya samudayai na kampatīti… paṭinissaggānupassanāya ādānai na kampatīti vipassanābalaṃ| avij‍jāya  ca avij‍jāsahagatakilaisai ca khandhai ca na kampati na calati na vaidhatīti vipassanābalaṃ| idaṃ vipassanābalaṃ|

‘‘tayau ca saṅkhārānaṃ paṭippassaddhiyāti katamaisaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ saṅkhārānaṃ paṭippassaddhiyā? dutiyajjhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vitak‍kavicārā vacīsaṅkhārā paṭippassaddhā haunti| catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa assāsapassāsā kāyasaṅkhārā paṭippassaddhā haunti| sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nassa sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca cittasaṅkhārā paṭippassaddhā haunti| imaisaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ saṅkhārānaṃ paṭippassaddhiyā|

‘‘sauḷasahi  ñāṇacariyāhīti katamāhi sauḷasahi ñāṇacariyāhi? anic‍cānupassanā ñāṇacariyā| dukkhā… anattā… nibbidā… virāgā… niraudhā… paṭinissaggā… vivaṭṭānupassanā ñāṇacariyā| sautāpattimaggau ñāṇacariyā| sautāpattiphalasamāpatti ñāṇacariyā| sakadāgāmimaggau…pai॰… arahattaphalasamāpatti ñāṇacariyā| imāhi sauḷasahi ñāṇacariyāhi|

‘‘navahi samādhicariyāhīti katamāhi navahi samādhicariyāhi? paṭhamajjhānaṃ samādhicariyā| dutiyajjhānaṃ…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti samādhicariyā| paṭhamajjhānapaṭilābhatthāya vitak‍kau ca vicārau ca pīti ca sukhañ‍ca cittaikaggatā ca…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiṃ paṭilābhatthāya vitak‍kau ca vicārau ca pīti ca sukhañ‍ca cittaikaggatā ca| imāhi navahi samādhicariyāhi|

‘‘vasīti pañ‍ca vasiyau – āvaj‍janavasī, samāpaj‍janavasī, adhiṭṭhānavasī, vuṭṭhānavasī, pac‍cavaikkhaṇavasī| paṭhamajjhānaṃ yatthicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ yāvaticchakaṃ āvaj‍jati, āvaj‍janāya dandhāyitattaṃ natthīti āvaj‍janavasī| paṭhamajjhānaṃ yatthicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ yāvaticchakaṃ samāpaj‍jati, samāpaj‍janāya dandhāyitattaṃ natthīti samāpaj‍janavasī…pai॰… adhiṭṭhāti adhiṭṭhānai…pai॰… vuṭṭhāti vuṭṭhānai…pai॰… pac‍cavaikkhati pac‍cavaikkhaṇāya dandhāyitattaṃ natthīti pac‍cavaikkhaṇavasī| dutiyaṃ…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiṃ yatthicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ yāvaticchakaṃ āvaj‍jati  …pai॰… pac‍cavaikkhati| pac‍cavaikkhaṇāya dandhāyitattaṃ natthīti pac‍cavaikkhaṇavasī| imā pañ‍ca vasiyau’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.83)|

870. aittha ca ‘‘sauḷasahi ñāṇacariyāhī’’ti uk‍kaṭṭhaniddaisau aisa| anāgāminau pana cuddasahi ñāṇacariyāhi hauti| yadi aivaṃ sakadāgāminau dvādasahi sautāpan‍nassa ca dasahi kiṃ na hautīti? na hauti, samādhipāribandhikassa pañ‍ca kāmaguṇikarāgassa appahīnattā| taisaṃ hi sau appahīnau| tasmā samathabalaṃ na paripuṇṇaṃ hauti, tasmiṃ aparipūrai dvīhi balaihi samāpaj‍jitabbaṃ niraudhasamāpattiṃ balavaikal‍laina samāpaj‍jituṃ na sak‍kaunti| anāgāmissa pana sau pahīnau, tasmā aisa paripuṇṇabalau  hauti| paripuṇṇabalattā sak‍kauti| taināha bhagavā – ‘‘niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanakusalaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau’’ti (paṭṭhā॰ 1.1.417)| idañhi paṭṭhānai mahāpakaraṇai anāgāminauva niraudhā vuṭṭhānaṃ sandhāya vuttanti|

871. kattha samāpaj‍jantīti pañ‍cavaukārabhavai| kasmā? anupubbasamāpattisabbhāvatau| catuvaukārabhavai pana paṭhamajjhānādīnaṃ uppatti natthi| tasmā na sak‍kā tattha samāpaj‍jitunti| kaici pana ‘‘vatthussa abhāvā’’ti vadanti|

872. kasmā samāpaj‍jantīti saṅkhārānaṃ pavattibhaidai uk‍kaṇṭhitvā diṭṭhaiva dhammai acittakā hutvā ‘‘niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ patvā sukhaṃ viharissāmā’’ti samāpaj‍janti|

873. kathañ‍cassā samāpaj‍janaṃ hautīti samathavipassanāvasaina ussak‍kitvā katapubbakic‍cassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ niraudhayatau, aivamassa samāpaj‍janaṃ hauti| yau hi samathavasainaiva ussak‍kati, sau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiṃ patvā tiṭṭhati| yau pana vipassanāvasainaiva ussak‍kati, sau phalasamāpattiṃ patvā tiṭṭhati| yau pana ubhayavasainaiva ussak‍kitvā pubbakic‍caṃ katvā naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ niraudhaiti, sau taṃ samāpaj‍jatīti ayamaittha saṅkhaipau|

874. ayaṃ pana vitthārau – idha bhikkhu niraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jitukāmau katabhattakic‍cau sudhautahatthapādau  vivittai aukāsai supañ‍ñattamhi āsanai nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā, sau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jitvā vuṭṭhāya tattha saṅkhārai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati|

vipassanā panaisā tividhā hauti – saṅkhāraparigaṇhanakavipassanā, phalasamāpattivipassanā, niraudhasamāpattivipassanāti| tattha saṅkhāraparigaṇhanakavipassanā mandā vā hautu tikkhā vā, maggassa padaṭṭhānaṃ hautiyaiva| phalasamāpattivipassanā  tikkhāva vaṭṭati maggabhāvanāsadisā| niraudhasamāpattivipassanā pana nātimandanātitikkhā vaṭṭati| tasmā aisa nātimandāya nātitikkhāya vipassanāya tai saṅkhārai vipassati|

tatau dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jitvā vuṭṭhāya tattha saṅkhārai tathaiva vipassati| tatau tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jitvā vuṭṭhāya tattha saṅkhārai tathaiva vipassati| tathā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jitvā vuṭṭhāya catubbidhaṃ pubbakic‍caṃ karauti – nānābaddhaavikaupanaṃ, saṅghapaṭimānanaṃ, satthupak‍kausanaṃ, addhānaparicchaidanti|

875. tattha nānābaddhaavikaupananti yaṃ iminā bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikābaddhaṃ na hauti, nānābaddhaṃ hutvā ṭhitaṃ pattacīvaraṃ vā mañ‍capīṭhaṃ vā nivāsagaihaṃ vā añ‍ñaṃ vā pana kiñ‍ci parikkhārajātaṃ, taṃ yathā na vikuppati, aggiudakavātacauraundūrādīnaṃ vasaina na vinassati, aivaṃ adhiṭṭhātabbaṃ|

tatridaṃ adhiṭṭhānavidhānaṃ ‘‘idañ‍ca idañ‍ca imasmiṃ sattāhabbhantarai mā agginā jhāyatu, mā udakaina vuyhatu, mā vātaina viddhaṃsatu, mā cauraihi hariyatu, mā undūrādīhi khaj‍jatū’’ti| aivaṃ adhiṭṭhitai taṃ sattāhaṃ tassa na kauci parissayau hauti|

anadhiṭṭhahatau pana aggiādīhi vinassati mahānāgatthairassa viya| thairau kira mātuupāsikāya gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| upāsikā yāguṃ datvā āsanasālāya nisīdāpaisi| thairau niraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jitvā nisīdi| tasmiṃ nisin‍nai āsanasālāya agginā gahitāya saisabhikkhū attanau attanau nisin‍nāsanaṃ gahaitvā palāyiṃsu| gāmavāsikā san‍nipatitvā  thairaṃ disvā ‘‘alasasamaṇau’’ti āhaṃsu| aggi tiṇavaiṇukaṭṭhāni jhāpaitvā thairaṃ parikkhipitvā aṭṭhāsi| manussā ghaṭaihi udakaṃ āharitvā nibbāpaitvā chārikaṃ apanaitvā paribhaṇḍaṃ katvā pupphāni vikiritvā namassamānā aṭṭhaṃsu| thairau paricchin‍nakālavasaina vuṭṭhāya tai disvā ‘‘pākaṭaumhi jātau’’ti vaihāsaṃ uppatitvā piyaṅgudīpaṃ agamāsi| idaṃ nānābaddhaavikaupanaṃ nāma|

yaṃ aikābaddhaṃ hauti nivāsanapāvuraṇaṃ vā nisin‍nāsanaṃ vā, tattha visuṃ adhiṭṭhānakic‍caṃ natthi| samāpattivasainaiva naṃ rakkhati āyasmatau sañ‍jīvassa viya| vuttampi caitaṃ ‘‘āyasmatau sañ‍jīvassa samādhivipphārā iddhi, āyasmatau sāriputtassa samādhivipphārā iddhī’’ti|

876. saṅghapaṭimānananti saṅghassa paṭimānanaṃ udikkhanaṃ| yāva aisau bhikkhu āgacchati, tāva saṅghakammassa akaraṇanti atthau| aittha ca na paṭimānanaṃ aitassa pubbakic‍caṃ, paṭimānanāvaj‍janaṃ pana pubbakic‍caṃ| tasmā aivaṃ āvaj‍jitabbaṃ ‘‘sacai mayi sattāhaṃ niraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jitvā nisin‍nai saṅghau uttikammādīsu kiñ‍cidaiva kammaṃ kattukāmau hauti, yāva maṃ kauci bhikkhu āgantvā na pak‍kausati, tāvadaiva vuṭṭhahissāmī’’ti| aivaṃ katvā samāpan‍nau hi tasmiṃ samayai vuṭṭhātiyaiva|

yau pana aivaṃ na karauti, saṅghau ca san‍nipatitvā taṃ apassantau ‘‘asukau bhikkhu kuhi’’nti ‘‘niraudhasamāpan‍nau’’ti vuttai saṅghau kañ‍ci bhikkhuṃ paisaiti ‘‘gaccha naṃ saṅghassa vacanaina pak‍kausāhī’’ti| athassa taina bhikkhunā savanūpacārai ṭhatvā ‘‘saṅghau taṃ āvusau paṭimānaitī’’ti vuttamattaiva vuṭṭhānaṃ hauti| aivaṃ garukā hi saṅghassa āṇā nāma| tasmā taṃ āvaj‍jitvā yathā sayamaiva vuṭṭhāti, aivaṃ samāpaj‍jitabbaṃ|

877. satthupak‍kausananti idhāpi satthupak‍kausanāvaj‍janamaiva imassa kic‍caṃ| tasmā tampi aivaṃ āvaj‍jitabbaṃ ‘‘sacai mayi sattāhaṃ niraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jitvā nisin‍nai satthā autiṇṇavatthusmiṃ sikkhāpadaṃ vā pañ‍ñapaiti, tathārūpāya vā atthuppattiyā dhammaṃ daisaiti, yāva maṃ  kauci āgantvā na pak‍kausati, tāvadaiva vuṭṭhahissāmī’’ti| aivaṃ katvā nisin‍nau hi tasmiṃ samayai vuṭṭhātiyaiva|

yau pana aivaṃ na karauti, satthā ca saṅghai san‍nipatitai taṃ apassantau ‘‘asukau bhikkhu kuhi’’nti ‘‘niraudhasamāpan‍nau’’ti vuttai kañ‍ci bhikkhuṃ paisaiti ‘‘gaccha naṃ mama vacanaina pak‍kausā’’ti| athassa taina bhikkhunā savanūpacārai ṭhatvā ‘‘satthā āyasmantaṃ āmantaitī’’ti vuttamattaiva vuṭṭhānaṃ hauti| aivaṃ garukaṃ hi satthupak‍kausanaṃ, tasmā taṃ āvaj‍jitvā yathā sayamaiva vuṭṭhāti, aivaṃ samāpaj‍jitabbaṃ|

878. addhānaparicchaidauti jīvitaddhānassa paricchaidau| iminā bhikkhunā addhānaparicchaidai sukusalaina bhavitabbaṃ| attanau ‘‘āyusaṅkhārā sattāhaṃ  pavattissanti na pavattissantī’’ti āvaj‍jitvāva samāpaj‍jitabbaṃ| sacai hi sattāhabbhantarai nirujjhanakai āyusaṅkhārai anāvaj‍jitvāva samāpaj‍jati, nāssa niraudhasamāpatti maraṇaṃ paṭibāhituṃ sak‍kauti| antauniraudhai maraṇassa natthitāya antarāva samāpattitau vuṭṭhāti| tasmā aitaṃ āvaj‍jitvāva samāpaj‍jitabbaṃ| avasaisaṃ hi anāvaj‍jitumpi vaṭṭati| idaṃ pana āvaj‍jitabbamaivāti vuttaṃ|

879. sau aivaṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jitvā vuṭṭhāya imaṃ pubbakic‍caṃ katvā naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jati| athaikaṃ vā dvai vā cittavārai atik‍kamitvā acittakau hauti, niraudhaṃ phusati| kasmā panassa dvin‍naṃ cittānaṃ uparicittāni na pavattantīti? niraudhassa payaugattā| idañhi imassa bhikkhunau dvai samathavipassanādhammai yuganaddhai katvā aṭṭha samāpattiārauhanaṃ anupubbaniraudhassa payaugau, na naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyāti niraudhassa payaugattā dvin‍naṃ cittānaṃ upari na pavattanti|

yau pana bhikkhu ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanatau vuṭṭhāya idaṃ pubbakic‍caṃ akatvā naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jati, sau paratau acittakau bhavituṃ na sak‍kauti, paṭinivattitvā puna ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaiyaiva patiṭṭhāti| maggaṃ agatapubbapurisūpamā caittha vattabbā –

aikau  kira purisau aikaṃ maggaṃ agatapubbau antarā udakakandaraṃ vā gambhīraṃ udakacikkhal‍laṃ atik‍kamitvā ṭhapitaṃ caṇḍātapasantattapāsāṇaṃ vā āgamma taṃ nivāsanapāvuraṇaṃ asaṇṭhapaitvāva kandaraṃ aurūḷhau parikkhārataimanabhayaina punadaiva tīrai patiṭṭhāti| pāsāṇaṃ ak‍kamitvāpi santattapādau punadaiva aurabhāgai patiṭṭhāti| tattha yathā sau purisau asaṇṭhapitanivāsanapāvuraṇattā kandaraṃ autiṇṇamattauva, tattapāsāṇaṃ ak‍kantamattau aiva ca paṭinivattitvā auratauva patiṭṭhāti, aivaṃ yaugāvacaraupi pubbakic‍cassa akatattā naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍namattauva paṭinivattitvā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai patiṭṭhāti|

yathā pana pubbaipi taṃ maggaṃ gatapubbapurisau taṃ ṭhānaṃ āgamma aikaṃ sāṭakaṃ daḷhaṃ nivāsaitvā aparaṃ hatthaina gahaitvā kandaraṃ uttaritvā tattapāsāṇaṃ  vā ak‍kantamattakamaiva karitvā paratau gacchati, aivamaivaṃ katapubbakic‍cau bhikkhu naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍jitvāva paratau acittakau hutvā niraudhaṃ phusitvā viharati|

880. kathaṃ ṭhānanti aivaṃ samāpan‍nāya panassā kālaparicchaidavasaina caiva antarāāyukkhayasaṅghapaṭimānanasatthupak‍kausanābhāvaina ca ṭhānaṃ hauti|

881. kathaṃ vuṭṭhānanti anāgāmissa anāgāmiphaluppattiyā, arahatau arahattaphaluppattiyāti aivaṃ dvaidhā vuṭṭhānaṃ hauti|

882. vuṭṭhitassa kiṃnin‍naṃ cittaṃ hautīti nibbānanin‍naṃ| vuttaṃ haitaṃ ‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhitassa khau, āvusau visākha, bhikkhunau vivaikanin‍naṃ cittaṃ hauti vivaikapauṇaṃ vivaikapabbhāra’’nti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.464)|

883. matassa ca samāpan‍nassa ca kau visaisauti ayampi atthau suttai vuttauyaiva| yathāha – ‘‘yvāyaṃ, āvusau, matau kālaṅkatau, tassa kāyasaṅkhārā niruddhā paṭippassaddhā, vacīsaṅkhārā… cittasaṅkhārā niruddhā paṭippassaddhā, āyu parikkhīṇau, usmā vūpasantā, indriyāni paribhin‍nāni| yau cāyaṃ bhikkhu sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nau, tassapi kāyasaṅkhārā  niruddhā paṭippassaddhā, vacīsaṅkhārā… cittasaṅkhārā niruddhā paṭippassaddhā, āyu aparikkhīṇau, usmā avūpasantā, indriyāni aparibhin‍nānī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.457)|

884. niraudhasamāpatti saṅkhatātiādipucchāyaṃ pana saṅkhatātipi asaṅkhatātipi laukiyātipi laukuttarātipi na vattabbā| kasmā? sabhāvatau natthitāya| yasmā panassā samāpaj‍jantassa vasaina samāpan‍nā nāma hauti, tasmā nipphan‍nāti vattuṃ vaṭṭati, nau anipphan‍nā|

iti santaṃ samāpattiṃ, imaṃ ariyanisaivitaṃ|

diṭṭhaiva dhammai nibbānamitisaṅkhaṃ upāgataṃ|

bhāvaitvā ariyaṃ pañ‍ñaṃ, samāpaj‍janti paṇḍitā||

yasmā tasmā imissāpi, samāpattisamatthatā|

ariyamaggaisu pañ‍ñāya, ānisaṃsauti vuc‍catīti||

āhunaiyyabhāvādisiddhikathā

885. āhunaiyyabhāvādisiddhīti  na kaivalañ‍ca niraudhasamāpattiyā samāpaj‍janasamatthatāva, ayaṃ pana āhunaiyyabhāvādisiddhipi imissā laukuttarapañ‍ñābhāvanāya ānisaṃsauti vaiditabbau| avisaisaina hi catubbidhāyapi aitissā bhāvitattā bhāvitapañ‍ñau puggalau sadaivakassa laukassa āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalīkaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa|

886. visaisatau panaittha paṭhamamaggapañ‍ñaṃ tāva bhāvaitvā mandāya vipassanāya āgatau mudindriyaupi sattakkhattuparamau nāma hauti, sattasugatibhavai saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| majjhimāya vipassanāya āgatau majjhimindriyau kaulaṃkaulau nāma hauti, dvai vā tīṇi vā kulāni  sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| tikkhāya vipassanāya āgatau tikkhindriyau aikabījī nāma hauti, aikañ‍ñaiva mānusakaṃ bhavaṃ nibbattaitvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti|

887. dutiyamaggapañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaitvā sakadāgāmī nāma hauti, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti|

888. tatiyamaggapañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaitvā anāgāmī nāma hauti| sau indriyavaimattatāvasaina antarāparinibbāyī, upahac‍caparinibbāyī, asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī, uddhaṃsautau akaniṭṭhagāmīti pañ‍cadhā| idha vihāyaniṭṭhau hauti| tattha antarāparinibbāyīti yattha katthaci suddhāvāsabhavai upapaj‍jitvā āyuvaimajjhaṃ appatvāva parinibbāyati| upahac‍caparinibbāyīti āyuvaimajjhaṃ atik‍kamitvā parinibbāyati| asaṅkhāraparinibbāyīti asaṅkhāraina appayaugaina uparimaggaṃ nibbattaiti| sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyīti sasaṅkhāraina sappayaugaina uparimaggaṃ nibbattaiti| uddhaṃsautau akaniṭṭhagāmīti yatthupapan‍nau, tatau uddhaṃ yāva akaniṭṭhabhavā āruyha tattha parinibbāyati|

889. catutthamaggapañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaitvā kauci saddhāvimuttau hauti, kauci pañ‍ñāvimuttau hauti, kauci ubhataubhāgavimuttau hauti, kauci taivij‍jau, kauci chaḷabhiñ‍ñau, kauci paṭisambhidappabhaidappattau mahākhīṇāsavau| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ ‘‘maggakkhaṇai panaisa taṃ jaṭaṃ vijaṭaiti nāma| phalakkhaṇai vijaṭitajaṭau sadaivakassa laukassa aggadakkhiṇaiyyau hautī’’ti|

aivaṃ anaikānisaṃsā, ariyapañ‍ñāya bhāvanā|

yasmā tasmā karaiyyātha, ratiṃ tattha vicakkhaṇau||

890. aittāvatā ca –

sīlai patiṭṭhāya narau sapañ‍ñau, cittaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvayaṃ|

ātāpī nipakau bhikkhu, sau imaṃ vijaṭayai jaṭanti|| –

imissā  gāthāya sīlasamādhipañ‍ñāmukhaina daisitai visuddhimaggai sānisaṃsā pañ‍ñābhāvanā paridīpitā hautīti|

iti sādhujanapāmauj‍jatthāya katai visuddhimaggai

pañ‍ñābhāvanādhikārai

pañ‍ñābhāvanānisaṃsaniddaisau nāma

taivīsatimau paricchaidau|

nigamanakathā

891. aittāvatā  ca –

‘‘sīlai patiṭṭhāya narau sapañ‍ñau, cittaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvayaṃ|

ātāpī nipakau bhikkhu, sau imaṃ vijaṭayai jaṭa’’nti|| –

imaṃ gāthaṃ nikkhipitvā yadavaucumha –

‘‘imissā dāni gāthāya, kathitāya mahaisinā|

vaṇṇayantau yathābhūtaṃ, atthaṃ sīlādibhaidanaṃ||

‘‘sudul‍labhaṃ labhitvāna, pabbaj‍jaṃ jinasāsanai|

sīlādisaṅgahaṃ khaimaṃ, ujuṃ maggaṃ visuddhiyā||

‘‘yathābhūtaṃ ajānantā, suddhikāmāpi yai idha|

visuddhiṃ nādhigacchanti, vāyamantāpi yauginau||

‘‘taisaṃ  pāmauj‍jakaraṇaṃ, suvisuddhavinicchayaṃ|

mahāvihāravāsīnaṃ, daisanānayanissitaṃ||

‘‘visuddhimaggaṃ bhāsissaṃ, taṃ mai sak‍kac‍ca bhāsatau|

visuddhikāmā sabbaipi, nisāmayatha sādhavau’’ti||

svāyaṃ bhāsitau hauti|

892. tattha ca –

taisaṃ sīlādibhaidānaṃ, atthānaṃ yau vinicchayau|

pañ‍can‍nampi nikāyānaṃ, vuttau aṭṭhakathānayai||

samāharitvā taṃ sabbaṃ, yaibhuyyaina sanicchayau|

sabbasaṅkaradausaihi, muttau yasmā pakāsitau||

tasmā visuddhikāmaihi, suddhapañ‍ñaihi yaugihi|

visuddhimaggai aitasmiṃ, karaṇīyauva ādarauti||

893.

 vibhaj‍javādisaiṭṭhānaṃ, thairiyānaṃ yasassinaṃ|

mahāvihāravāsīnaṃ, vaṃsajassa vibhāvinau||

bhadantasaṅghapālassa, sucisal‍laikhavuttinau|

vinayācārayuttassa, yuttassa paṭipattiyaṃ||

khantisaurac‍camaittādi-guṇabhūsitacaitasau|

ajjhaisanaṃ gahaitvāna, karauntaina imaṃ mayā||

saddhammaṭṭhitikāmaina, yau pattau puñ‍ñasañ‍cayau|

tassa taijaina sabbaipi, sukhamaidhantu pāṇinau||

894.

 visuddhimaggau aisau ca, antarāyaṃ vinā idha|

niṭṭhitau aṭṭhapañ‍ñāsa-bhāṇavārāya pāḷiyā||

yathā tathaiva laukassa, sabbai kalyāṇanissitā|

anantarāyā ijjhantu, sīghaṃ sīghaṃ manaurathāti||

895. parama visuddha saddhā buddhi vīriya paṭimaṇḍitaina sīlācāraj‍java maddavādiguṇasamudayasamuditaina sakasamaya samayantaragahanajjhaugāhaṇasamatthaina pañ‍ñāvaiyyattiyasaman‍nāgataina tipiṭakapariyattibhaidai sāṭṭhakathai satthusāsanai appaṭihatañāṇappabhāvaina mahāvaiyyākaraṇaina karaṇasampattijanitasukhaviniggatamadhuraudāravacanalāvaṇṇayuttaina yuttamuttavādinā vādīvaraina mahākavinā chaḷabhiñ‍ñāpaṭisambhidādi bhaidaguṇapaṭimaṇḍitai uttarimanussadhammai appaṭihatabuddhīnaṃ thairavaṃsappadīpānaṃ thairānaṃ mahāvihāravāsīnaṃ vaṃsālaṅkārabhūtaina vipulavisuddhabuddhinā buddhaghausauti garūhi gahitanāmadhaiyyaina thairaina mudantakhaidakavattabbaina katau visuddhimaggau nāma|

896.

 tāva tiṭṭhatu laukasmiṃ, laukanittharaṇaisinaṃ|

dassaintau kulaputtānaṃ, nayaṃ sīlādisuddhiyā||

yāva  buddhauti nāmampi, suddhacittassa tādinau|

laukamhi laukajaiṭṭhassa, pavattati mahaisinauti||

iti sādhujanapāmauj‍jatthāya katā visuddhimaggakathā,

pāḷigaṇanāya pana sā aṭṭhapañ‍ñāsabhāṇavārā hautīti|

visuddhimaggapakaraṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|
